class="ne-text">重新投影</span></strong><span class="ne-text">到满足 heel-ball 固定距离的位置</span></li><li id="ud23b150b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">反过来 ball 先锁住也一样</span></li></ul><p id="ua2288c36" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，这两个点不仅是“检测点”，还是后面</span><strong><span class="ne-text">保持脚底形状不变</span></strong><span class="ne-text">的几何依据。</span></p><p id="ud2e9377c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在视频里也明确解释过：当第二个接触点第一次进入约束时，要重新投影，保证 heel 和 ball 的距离关系不变。</span></p><hr id="BHVqP" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="rWW42"><span class="ne-text">五、所以这些数不是“魔法数”，而是“当前角色脚底几何的手工标定”</span></h2><p id="u46ebccb8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一点特别关键。</span></p><p id="ufb909a52" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这些值并不是论文规定的标准数值。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">它们只是对</span><strong><span class="ne-text">当前这个角色</span></strong><span class="ne-text">来说比较合理的脚底局部采样位置。</span></p><p id="u8a84dba0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">换句话说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf79ab5e8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">换一个 mesh</span></li><li id="ub338e448" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">换一个 skeleton</span></li><li id="u3d960422" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">换一双鞋</span></li><li id="u62245c8b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">换一个脚骨 pivot</span></li></ul><p id="u69af125a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这些值都应该重新标</span></p><p id="ufdd1694d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为它们其实代表的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uccd70252" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">脚后跟大概在哪里</span></li><li id="u5ad4d957" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">前脚掌着力点大概在哪里</span></li><li id="u1924a96c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">两点之间脚底长度是多少</span></li></ul><h3 id="IUGMV"><span class="ne-text">标错会怎样</span></h3><p id="u3756a7b8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果这些点放得不好，会直接影响后面全部结果：</span></p><h4 id="tGYzQ"><span class="ne-text">放太靠前 / 靠后</span></h4><p id="uaa30099a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">会让脚的落地点判断错位，出现：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uca2bd2eb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">明明脚没落稳却判定接触</span></li><li id="u45a187ef" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">或明明已经踩住了却还在滑</span></li></ul><h4 id="mT97n"><span class="ne-text">左右不对称</span></h4><p id="u582a812b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">会让左右脚 cleanup 效果不一致</span></p><h4 id="BVsuc"><span class="ne-text">heel-ball 距离不真实</span></h4><p id="uae5d2f2f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">会让后面的重投影不符合实际脚长<br /></span><span class="ne-text">结果就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u4201aa1f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">脚底被拉扯</span></li><li id="u44d38caa" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ankle 目标位置不合理</span></li><li id="u4ce22a40" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">脚底朝向怪</span></li><li id="u4f49eacf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">后续 knee / root 调整变得更困难</span></li></ul><hr id="Ez4J5" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="SuzWa"><span class="ne-text">六、你可以把这四个骨骼理解成什么</span></h2><p id="u85092845" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">最准确的理解是：</span></p><p id="u47e25a6f" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">它们不是解剖骨骼，而是算法接触探针（contact probes）+ 脚底刚体几何锚点（rigid sole anchors）。</span></strong></p><p id="uf9b36319" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它们承担了三类职责：</span></p><h3 id="L3dvg"><span class="ne-text">1. 接触检测</span></h3><p id="u45bdc75e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">从原始 mocap 的 heel / ball 速度判断何时 planted</span></p><h3 id="joMVi"><span class="ne-text">2. 约束生成</span></h3><p id="u56eddd59" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">进入 planted 后，为每帧生成稳定的地面约束位置</span></p><h3 id="LBI2G"><span class="ne-text">3. 足底几何保持</span></h3><p id="u10c6dad6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">让 heel 和 ball 的距离在约束切换时保持不变，避免脚底形状乱掉</span></p><hr id="Ccp81" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="y76mJ"><span class="ne-text">七、和论文 / 视频的上下游关系</span></h2><p id="u0465c197" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这四个点在整条链路里的位置是：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="ztUmD" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>Prepare Data
  → 注入 Heel / Ball marker bones
  → 从 original 动画里取它们的世界位置与速度
  → 得到 left/right heel/ball contact timing
  → 生成 constraint positions
  → 反推出 ankle pose
  → 再做 root / leg / smoothing</code></pre><p id="uc92d8338" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以它们是整个算法的数据入口之一。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">没有这四个点，后面那套“heel-only / ball-only / double-contact”的 cleanup 逻辑就无从谈起。Jerome 自己也说得很明确：加这些骨，是为了让 skeleton 满足论文所需的结构。</span></p><hr id="L3K7m" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="VAFfq"><span class="ne-text">八、给你一个最终结论</span></h2><p id="u627f2459" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这四个新增骨骼的“讲究”可以压缩成 5 句话：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u7d98a27d" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">数量上</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：每只脚必须至少有 heel 和 ball 两个接触点，才能表达足底支撑而不只是一个孤点。</span></li><li id="ub43cd284" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">拓扑上</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：必须挂在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftFoot/RightFoot</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 下面，因为它们是脚刚体上的固定局部点。</span></li><li id="u9a2008d0" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">旋转上</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：用单位四元数，因为这里只关心位置采样，不需要独立朝向。</span></li><li id="u849e982c" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">数值上</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：这些 offset 是当前角色脚底几何的手工标定，左右镜像、前后分布、同底平面、固定脚长。</span></li><li id="u1c8e2e23" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">算法上</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：它们既用于接触检测，也用于后续保持 heel-ball 距离，从而支撑整个 footskate cleanup。</span></li></ol><p id="ud6225ee9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你这一步一旦理解透了，后面 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">extract_feet_contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contact_distance</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 那段代码就会非常顺。下一条我可以继续直接接着讲：</span></p><p id="u3339206b" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">为什么后面先算 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">contact_distance</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text">，以及它如何参与“第二个接触点进入约束时的重投影”。</span></strong></p></details>
#### keep_translation(retargeting)
<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u0b3496e6"><span class="ne-text">gemini</span></summary><p id="ua20c9f53" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">在动作捕捉数据处理和这段代码的上下文中，</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(68, 71, 70)">keep_translation</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">（保留位移）参数的核心目的是</span><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">控制是否将原始动捕文件（BVH）中各个骨骼节点的“绝对空间平移（位置）数据”应用到目标角色的骨骼上</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">。</span></p><p id="ue5da1017" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">根据代码中两次加载的不同设置，它的具体目的分为以下两个截然不同的方面：</span></p><h2 id="13bc4f5a"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">1. 设置为 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(68, 71, 70)">False</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)"> 的目的：进行标准动作重定向（人为复现滑步）</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u06c9f8b4" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">运行机制</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">：当 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(68, 71, 70)">keep_translation=False</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)"> 时，加载器会丢弃除了根节点（Root/Hips）之外所有子关节的空间位置数据，**仅仅将原始数据的“关节旋转角度（Rotations）”**映射给目标角色。</span></li><li id="u4d7fe217" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">实际目的</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">：这是 3D 动画中“动作重定向”（Motion Retargeting）的标准做法。因为目标 3D 模型与原始动捕演员的体型、骨骼长度几乎总是不一致的。如果强行套用位置数据，会导致目标模型的四肢被生硬拉扯断裂或严重变形。只传递旋转数据，能完美保持目标角色自身的骨骼比例。</span></li><li id="u3bd8f390" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">产生的后果</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">：由于放弃了原有的绝对位置，且骨骼长度发生了变化，脚部原有的空间几何约束被破坏，必然会导致脚部无法精确踩在原有的地平面上，从而</span><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">产生滑步（Footskate）</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">。这正是运行这套清理算法的先决条件。</span></li></ul><h2 id="6fd62f46"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">2. 设置为 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(68, 71, 70)">True</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)"> 的目的：提取真实的物理约束数据（Ground Truth）</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u6d4fc886" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">运行机制</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">：当 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(68, 71, 70)">keep_translation=True</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)"> 时，加载器会强制读取并应用动捕数据中每一个骨骼节点的绝对三维坐标。为了匹配这些绝对坐标，目标角色的骨骼长度会被强行拉伸或挤压（Squash and Stretch）。</span></li><li id="u89e9dfed" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">实际目的</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">：作者加载这层数据完全不是为了视觉展示，而是为了</span><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">提取参考数据</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: rgb(31, 31, 31)">。论文中的解析算法虽然能把脚钉在地上，但前提是它必须知道“脚应该在什么时候踩在什么位置”。保留了绝对平移数据后，作者就能通过计算脚跟（Heel）和脚前掌（Ball）在三维空间中的真实移动速度，准确判定出原始演员在哪几帧是完全踩实的。这些提取出的时间点和坐标，构成了整个滑步清理算法最核心的输入参数（约束条件）。</span></li></ul></details>
### Find feet contacts timing
[https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69be7d27-a020-83a2-a348-c836dc2339d3](https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69be7d27-a020-83a2-a348-c836dc2339d3)

[https://chatgpt.com/share/69bff041-7584-8009-b181-6252f863e398](https://chatgpt.com/share/69bff041-7584-8009-b181-6252f863e398)

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u07a02179"><span class="ne-text">gpt</span></summary><p id="u4ac9d192" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">好，我们把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Find feet contacts timing</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这一段，按 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">cell → 行 → 作用 → 上下游</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 的方式彻底拆开。</span></p><p id="uc9d70cf5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这部分在整条 notebook 里的定位很明确：</span></p><p id="ue208ff43" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">它不是在修脚滑，而是在先生成“脚什么时候应该锁地”的时间标签。</span></strong></p><p id="u59e02bd5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在视频里明确说，这篇 paper 本身只负责“已知 footplant 约束时，如何正确修正”，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">不负责检测什么时候该锁脚</span></strong><span class="ne-text">；所以他额外利用原始 mocap 的稳定性，用 heel / ball 的位置和速度来推 contact timing。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">notebook 这一节的 markdown 也直接写了：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">用原始运动检查 heel 和 ball 何时低于某个速度阈值，并假设此时脚已 planted；然后再平滑一下信号，避免 1、2、3 帧这种很小的误判断开。</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> (</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><hr id="UqPGy" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="0c457a0b"><span class="ne-text">一、这一部分一共有哪几个 cell</span></h1><p id="uf5eb68ca" class="ne-p"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Find feet contacts timing</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这块，实际可以分成 3 个 cell：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u48474ca0" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">markdown 说明 cell</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">解释这一节想做什么：从原始动画里检测 planted timing，再把抖动信号平滑掉。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></li><li id="u642326eb" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">真正计算 contacts 的 code cell</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">也就是这段：</span></li></ol><pre data-language="python" id="fbgHA" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ogquats, ogpos = lab.utils.quat_fk(original.quats, original.pos, original.parents)
agquats, agpos = lab.utils.quat_fk(animation.quats, animation.pos, animation.parents)

contacts = np.zeros([frame_count, 4], dtype=np.bool_)
contacts[:, :2], contacts[:, 2:] = lab.utils.extract_feet_contacts(
    ogpos, [left_heel, left_ball], [right_heel, right_ball], 0.04
)

# smooth out a little the signal ( we keep the signal on if it switch off for one or 2 frames )
for frame in range(2, frame_count-2):
    for c in range(4):
        contacts[frame, c] = contacts[frame, c] or (contacts[frame-2, c] and contacts[frame+2, c])

for frame in range(1, frame_count-1):
    for c in range(4):
        contacts[frame, c] = contacts[frame, c] or (contacts[frame-1, c] and contacts[frame+1, c])</code></pre><p id="u614d6eb1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段代码在 raw notebook 里可以直接找到。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><ol start="3" class="ne-ol"><li id="u74fc188d" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">可视化 code cell</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">把四路 contact signal 画出来，并用交互 viewer 显示“哪几个接触点此帧被判定为 planted”。对应代码是：</span></li></ol><pre data-language="python" id="dRJHs" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(10,1))
ax1.plot(contacts[:, 0])
ax1.plot(contacts[:, 1])
ax2.plot(contacts[:, 2])
ax2.plot(contacts[:, 3])

def render(frame, ori):
    ...
    if contacts[frame, c]:
        contacts_matrices[c, [0,2], 3] = ogpos[frame, contact_indices[c], [0,2]]
    ...
    ax1.set_xlim([frame-60,frame+60])
    ax2.set_xlim([frame-60,frame+60])

interact(render, frame=lab.Timeline(max=frame_count-1), ori=widgets.Checkbox())</code></pre><p id="u2d49d67f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段也能在 raw notebook 里看到。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><hr id="aGA7e" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="af43cd76"><span class="ne-text">二、先讲第一格 markdown：它在说什么</span></h1><p id="u985937f6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">markdown 的意思很简单：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua404f707" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">先用原始运动 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">original</span></strong></code></li><li id="u406b3fb2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">去看 heel / ball 的速度</span></li><li id="u520e8879" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">低于阈值就判定为 planted</span></li><li id="ud1284263" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后把这个 on/off 信号再做一点平滑</span></li><li id="u92ddff97" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">防止只断开 1～3 帧的噪声</span></li></ul><p id="ucbd203f7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这和 Jerome 视频里的口述完全一致：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">他会建一个 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame_count x 4</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的大 buffer，四列分别是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">left heel / left ball / right heel / right ball</span></code><span class="ne-text">；然后用一个速度启发式去判断是否接触地面；阈值大约是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">0.04</span></code><span class="ne-text">；再把只掉 1～2 帧的小洞补上。</span></p><hr id="PaU54" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="846f3910"><span class="ne-text">三、逐行讲解“计算 contacts”的那个 cell</span></h1><hr id="HO3sy" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b93bcef1"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="Bv8qb" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ogquats, ogpos = lab.utils.quat_fk(original.quats, original.pos, original.parents)</code></pre><h3 id="5a14671f"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="u35e2ba87" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">对 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">original</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这份原始动画做一次 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">FK（Forward Kinematics）</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，求出每一帧、每根骨头的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">全局旋转</span></strong><span class="ne-text">和</span><strong><span class="ne-text">全局位置</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="d3bfb845"><span class="ne-text">为什么要做</span></h3><p id="u02785eac" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为动画文件里存的通常是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u75bf9623" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">局部旋转 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">quats</span></code></li><li id="u296a8d45" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">局部平移 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pos</span></code></li><li id="ue8c90e3c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">父子骨架关系 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">parents</span></code></li></ul><p id="uf8f1ea24" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但你要判断“脚跟是不是 planted”，看的不是局部空间，而是：</span></p><p id="u9058a489" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">这个 heel / ball 在世界里到底动没动。</span></strong></p><p id="u8f559716" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以必须先把局部骨架变换沿父子链累积起来，得到世界空间位置。</span></p><h3 id="c129470d"><span class="ne-text">输出是什么</span></h3><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u266f8fcd" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ogquats</span></code><span class="ne-text">：original global quaternions</span></li><li id="u78eff502" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ogpos</span></code><span class="ne-text">：original global positions</span></li></ul><p id="ud87d814d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">og</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 可以理解成 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">original global</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><hr id="VfeUq" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="dbe2723d"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="yTmdw" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>agquats, agpos = lab.utils.quat_fk(animation.quats, animation.pos, animation.parents)</code></pre><h3 id="5a14671f-1"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="ud8f91e5e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">对 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这份“会滑脚的目标动画”也做同样的 FK。</span></p><h3 id="ab69c725"><span class="ne-text">你会问：这里为什么也要算？</span></h3><p id="u185efac6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为后面的可视化 cell 里，Jerome 会允许你在 viewer 里切换：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2b72f416" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">original</span></code></li><li id="uaf7511f4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">或者看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code></li></ul><p id="u76e3ea1b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样你能直观看到：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7d1c66cd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始运动里脚该锁的地方</span></li><li id="ue44ab3ea" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">以及 retarget 后版本实际已经滑到了哪里</span></li></ul><h3 id="83b7b461"><span class="ne-text">重要一点</span></h3><p id="u62ace6bc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在“纯 contact 检测”逻辑里，真正拿来判定 planted 的是 </span><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">ogpos</span></strong></code><span class="ne-text">，不是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这一点跟 Jerome 视频口述完全一致：他用原始稳定数据去检测 contact，而不是用已经滑了的版本去反推。</span></p><hr id="LvVOH" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="56c42dc5"><span class="ne-text">第 4 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="YPItk" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts = np.zeros([frame_count, 4], dtype=np.bool_)</code></pre><h3 id="5a14671f-2"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="u6ef9d034" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">创建一个大小为：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="f2zpr" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>[总帧数, 4]</code></pre><p id="u9320424e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">的布尔数组。</span></p><h3 id="e36b1767"><span class="ne-text">4 列分别代表什么</span></h3><p id="ud4aa3619" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">按 notebook 前面注入骨点的顺序，这 4 列对应：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf82656cc" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">0</span></code><span class="ne-text"> = left heel</span></li><li id="u9081ab45" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> = left ball</span></li><li id="uc585b494" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> = right heel</span></li><li id="ue4536c43" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> = right ball</span></li></ul><p id="u64a71f81" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在视频里也是这样描述的：一个大 buffer，四个 contact 通道，分别是 left heel / left ball / right heel / right ball。</span></p><h3 id="6d06f4c3"><span class="ne-text">为什么是布尔类型</span></h3><p id="u02952833" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这一层还不是约束位置，不是连续数值，而只是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u526f7ca2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这一帧接触</span></li><li id="u0f3f9845" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">还是不接触</span></li></ul><p id="u99c67c10" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以布尔就够了。</span></p><hr id="oZOQj" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="da888cd4"><span class="ne-text">第 5 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="V8MOn" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts[:, :2], contacts[:, 2:] = lab.utils.extract_feet_contacts(
    ogpos, [left_heel, left_ball], [right_heel, right_ball], 0.04
)</code></pre><p id="u94716f14" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这行是这整个 section 的核心。</span></p><hr id="TIf6t" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="de883297"><span class="ne-text">先看左边赋值</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="mzCN8" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts[:, :2], contacts[:, 2:]</code></pre><p id="u84004348" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">意思是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u75457e69" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">前两列填左脚结果</span></li><li id="u833cb3ff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">后两列填右脚结果</span></li></ul><p id="u6f93db2c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8df118ad" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts[:, :2]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> → 左 heel / 左 ball</span></li><li id="u28a1d684" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts[:, 2:]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> → 右 heel / 右 ball</span></li></ul><hr id="pdjg3" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="653800f8"><span class="ne-text">再看函数输入</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="PMZf8" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ogpos</code></pre><p id="ua22bfddf" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">表示使用 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">original global positions</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u17c9f79d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这非常重要。因为你要从“更可信的原始 mocap”里提取 contact，而不是从已经滑脚的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 里提。Jerome 视频也是这么说的。</span></p><hr id="Fh5Y8" class="ne-hr"><pre data-language="python" id="YQYtY" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>[left_heel, left_ball], [right_heel, right_ball]</code></pre><p id="ua91bd5c2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里告诉 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">extract_feet_contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u432a4340" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左脚要检查哪两个点</span></li><li id="u7216fcad" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右脚要检查哪两个点</span></li></ul><p id="ue662c165" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">为什么不是只给 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftFoot / RightFoot</span></code><span class="ne-text">？</span></p><p id="ud355028d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为脚底接触不是二元的“整只脚接触 / 不接触”，而是更细的：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ue3a4bdab" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">后跟先着地</span></li><li id="u13eae729" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">全脚掌压实</span></li><li id="u126554a8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">后跟离地只剩前掌</span></li><li id="u3da67419" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">最后整个离地</span></li></ul><p id="u6f3dbf20" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文里也把下肢足部接触分解成 heel / ball / toes 这种几何结构；Jerome notebook 是选了 heel + ball 作为接触检测的关键支点。</span></p><hr id="g1hVI" class="ne-hr"><pre data-language="python" id="WSO9z" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>0.04</code></pre><p id="uf0eab794" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">速度阈值</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u536783cc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 视频里明确说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7e42ce26" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果某个 heel/ball 动得比 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">0.04</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 更快，就认为它在动</span></li><li id="u1d7f7020" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果低于这个阈值，就认为它是 planted 的</span></li></ul><p id="u3c83ee8a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以你可以把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">extract_feet_contacts(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 直观理解成：</span></p><p id="u652ae930" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">“检查 heel / ball 的世界空间速度，小于阈值就标 1，否则标 0。”</span></strong></p><hr id="Xo2hJ" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="3d7766cf"><span class="ne-text">这一整行的最终作用</span></h3><p id="ufbedae5a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它把连续运动数据，变成了四路开关时序信号：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="xd1eG" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts[frame, 0]  # 左后跟是否接触
contacts[frame, 1]  # 左前掌是否接触
contacts[frame, 2]  # 右后跟是否接触
contacts[frame, 3]  # 右前掌是否接触</code></pre><p id="u93092c8e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是后面全部 footskate cleanup 的“约束开关表”。</span></p><hr id="qlNuc" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="f69e2868"><span class="ne-text">第 7 行注释</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="Fa11r" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code># smooth out a little the signal ( we keep the signal on if it switch off for one or 2 frames )</code></pre><p id="uf624fba8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这句注释其实已经把后面两层循环的目的讲透了：</span></p><p id="u6d41eb3b" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">如果接触信号只短暂断掉 1 帧或 2 帧，那大概率不是脚真的离地了，而是检测噪声。</span></strong></p><p id="uda308e84" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 视频里也明确说了同样的话：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">他不希望 contact 段中间因为某一帧的误判突然熄灭，所以会做一个小 smoothing，让它至少形成连续几帧。</span></p><hr id="nRvm0" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="def1548a"><span class="ne-text">第一个 smoothing 循环</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="U9C2Z" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for frame in range(2, frame_count-2):
    for c in range(4):
        contacts[frame, c] = contacts[frame, c] or (contacts[frame-2, c] and contacts[frame+2, c])</code></pre><h3 id="fe109be4"><span class="ne-text">先看循环范围</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="WECj6" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>range(2, frame_count-2)</code></pre><p id="ube27a897" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为你要访问 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame-2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame+2</span></code><span class="ne-text">，所以前后各留 2 帧边界，避免越界。</span></p><hr id="mLf2L" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="61feed73"><span class="ne-text">再看内部逻辑</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="Mk6ax" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts[frame, c] = contacts[frame, c] or (contacts[frame-2, c] and contacts[frame+2, c])</code></pre><p id="udd3ba182" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这句翻成中文是：</span></p><p id="ud3ce38c3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧本来已经是接触，那当然保留。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧不是接触，但它前 2 帧和后 2 帧都是接触，那么也把当前帧补成接触。</span></p><h3 id="04e4a9a5"><span class="ne-text">这在修什么</span></h3><p id="u17dbe9f0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它主要针对这种情况：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="uhHuT" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>1 1 0 0 1 1</code></pre><p id="ud313bf5a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">或者更一般地说，针对很短的 2 帧左右缺口。</span></p><h3 id="9cc392a2"><span class="ne-text">直觉是什么</span></h3><p id="u1fc15369" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果同一个 heel/ball 在较宽一点的时间尺度上看，两边都稳定 planted，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">那中间这几帧掉成 0，很可能只是速度阈值在边界上抖了一下。</span></p><p id="u60c6053c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这一层是一个“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">跨 2 帧的小洞填补</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”。</span></p><hr id="MpcZm" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="5a179ef3"><span class="ne-text">第二个 smoothing 循环</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="HbK2j" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for frame in range(1, frame_count-1):
    for c in range(4):
        contacts[frame, c] = contacts[frame, c] or (contacts[frame-1, c] and contacts[frame+1, c])</code></pre><h3 id="fe109be4-1"><span class="ne-text">先看循环范围</span></h3><p id="u64ab7e32" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里访问的是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame-1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame+1</span></code><span class="ne-text">，所以只需要前后各留 1 帧。</span></p><hr id="DLyky" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="d76121d6"><span class="ne-text">内部逻辑</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="p2CLd" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts[frame, c] = contacts[frame, c] or (contacts[frame-1, c] and contacts[frame+1, c])</code></pre><p id="uf2b26b8d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">翻成中文就是：</span></p><p id="uc5e90f73" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧不是接触，但它前一帧和后一帧都是接触，那就把它补成接触。</span></p><h3 id="04e4a9a5-1"><span class="ne-text">这在修什么</span></h3><p id="u6509a92c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">针对的是单帧洞：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="aB4jW" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>1 1 1 0 1 1 1</code></pre><h3 id="c55bf3af"><span class="ne-text">为什么两层循环分开做</span></h3><p id="u47716b99" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 Jerome 想处理的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u603657e7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先补比较明显的“2 帧小洞”</span></li><li id="u5bcd05c8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再补更细的“1 帧小洞”</span></li></ul><p id="u7df92681" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样最终 contact 段会更连续、更稳。</span></p><p id="uc3e527b5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它不是一个严格数学意义上的滤波器，而是一个非常实用的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">时序标签修补器</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="Uf5WP" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="1334b176"><span class="ne-text">四、这一格 cell 最终输出了什么</span></h1><p id="u677523c3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">跑完这格 cell 后，你并没有得到修好的动画。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">你得到的是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="CCMLB" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts[frame, c]</code></pre><p id="u816c0b55" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样一张 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">按帧的四路 planted 时间表</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ue5cec18d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这张表会进入后面的阶段：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua49c1073" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Constraints Positions</span></code></li><li id="u86524b47" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle position/orientation</span></code></li><li id="u6622393f" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Root placement</span></code></li><li id="u10d6b968" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Leg IK</span></code></li><li id="uc4ef7540" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Final Processing</span></code></li></ul><p id="u21022872" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，这格 cell 是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">后面五阶段算法的前置输入层</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，不是求解器本体。Jerome 视频里也是先讲 contact timing，再正式进入 paper 的五阶段。</span></p><hr id="sYsdD" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="4b3b8458"><span class="ne-text">五、逐行讲解“可视化 contacts”的那个 cell</span></h1><p id="u0449d25d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">接下来讲第二个 code cell，也就是把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 看见的那一格。</span></p><hr id="ivFq2" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b93bcef1-1"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="qE7Bu" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(10,1))</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u82067c46" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">创建一个 Matplotlib 图窗，里面有上下两个子图：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uacc4a8f1" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ax1</span></code><span class="ne-text">：画左脚两路信号</span></li><li id="u1d3152cc" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ax2</span></code><span class="ne-text">：画右脚两路信号</span></li></ul><h3 id="2eb786be"><span class="ne-text">为什么分两行</span></h3><p id="u0071037f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为四路都堆一行会看不清。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">分成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uc3074bdd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">上：left heel / left ball</span></li><li id="u6a34df06" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">下：right heel / right ball</span></li></ul><p id="u1d59817d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">更容易看相位关系。</span></p><hr id="BYaZG" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="34305dda"><span class="ne-text">第 2～5 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="h1nZg" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ax1.plot(contacts[:, 0])
ax1.plot(contacts[:, 1])
ax2.plot(contacts[:, 2])
ax2.plot(contacts[:, 3])</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-1"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uce9f4eb8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把四路布尔信号画成时间曲线。</span></p><p id="u751455b7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">虽然 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是 bool，但画图时会被当成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ufa06865e" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">False = 0</span></code></li><li id="ue60418f6" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">True = 1</span></code></li></ul><p id="ub31e6e27" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以你看到的是四条跳变的 on/off 曲线。</span></p><h3 id="3ca2c9ab"><span class="ne-text">工程意义</span></h3><p id="u3cd086ed" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样你能直观看：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u88002ce9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 heel 比左 ball 更早接触还是更晚接触</span></li><li id="ufca21e84" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右脚是不是和左脚交替</span></li><li id="u10efdb5c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">contact 检测有没有很多毛刺</span></li><li id="u906bd4a0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">smoothing 后是否更连续</span></li></ul><p id="u55dcf13c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步其实是在验证前一格 cell 的输出质量。</span></p><hr id="nuC1j" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="iBILm"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">def render(frame, ori):</span></code></h2><pre data-language="python" id="X1O6w" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def render(frame, ori):</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-2"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ua8531536" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">定义一个交互渲染函数：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udcd03755" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame</span></code><span class="ne-text">：当前查看哪一帧</span></li><li id="ue03658dc" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ori</span></code><span class="ne-text">：是看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">original</span></code><span class="ne-text">，还是看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code></li></ul><p id="u96a01154" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是 notebook 常见写法，后面配合 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">interact(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 可以做滑条和开关。</span></p><hr id="n2XnV" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="1fa9c2d8"><span class="ne-text">选择显示哪份动画</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="BfdYe" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>anim = original if ori else animation</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-3"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u08d6da24" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果勾选 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ori</span></code><span class="ne-text">，就显示原始参考动画；否则显示 retarget 后待修版本。</span></p><h3 id="b0f8b349"><span class="ne-text">为什么这一步很重要</span></h3><p id="u49882336" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这能让你直接对比：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0386b81e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始数据里脚底点什么时候稳定</span></li><li id="u80bfda79" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而目标动画里对应的脚是不是已经滑开了</span></li></ul><p id="u19ee25ea" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在视频里就是通过这种对照，说明“接触信号来自原始数据，但当前要修的是滑脚版本”。</span></p><hr id="H4Woc" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="f3ffd43f"><span class="ne-text">取当前帧局部变换</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="ilYvB" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>p = (anim.pos[frame,...])
q = (anim.quats[frame,...])</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-4"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uc27b30e5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">拿出当前帧所有骨骼的：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u810339c8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">位置 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">p</span></code></li><li id="u0dd841c1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">旋转 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">q</span></code></li></ul><p id="u4b4e06fb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是 viewer 画当前角色姿态所需的数据。</span></p><hr id="PfZux" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="04e56af3"><span class="ne-text">转成矩阵</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="h0kpe" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(q, p)</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-5"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u97839022" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把四元数旋转和位置，转成可以直接喂给 viewer 的变换矩阵。</span></p><p id="u31b9281f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">viewer 画骨架 / 网格时，更适合直接消费矩阵数组。</span></p><hr id="k2EXJ" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="169c5abc"><span class="ne-text">设阴影参考点</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="NPApo" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.set_shadow_poi(p[0])</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-6"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ucc982c86" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">设置阴影关注点（point of interest），一般是用根节点附近位置。</span></p><p id="u6ad3ac93" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个不属于 contact 算法本身，主要是为了显示效果。</span></p><hr id="Pi9cS" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="e6636767"><span class="ne-text">开始画角色</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="USey5" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.begin_shadow()
viewer.draw(character, a)
viewer.end_shadow()

viewer.begin_display()
viewer.draw_ground()
viewer.draw(character, a)</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-7"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ub2bc6e29" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先画 shadow pass，再画 ground 和 character 本体。</span></p><p id="u40b4e1da" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这部分也是纯显示。</span></p><hr id="DJSZa" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="2ed98a2b"><span class="ne-text">构造 contact target 的变换矩阵</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="HCxYr" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts_matrices = np.eye(4, dtype=np.float32)[np.newaxis,...].repeat(4, axis=0)</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-8"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u5e7dafe9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">创建 4 个 4x4 单位矩阵。</span></p><h3 id="43ef0dce"><span class="ne-text">为什么是 4 个</span></h3><p id="u12fbef0f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为有四个接触点：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u71085a9b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left heel</span></li><li id="u45975e36" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left ball</span></li><li id="u029d28ed" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right heel</span></li><li id="u137a8413" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right ball</span></li></ul><h3 id="7b411a46"><span class="ne-text">为什么先用单位矩阵</span></h3><p id="u010a023c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 viewer.draw(target, contacts_matrices) 需要的是一组实例变换矩阵。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">单位矩阵可以作为默认“什么都不平移、什么都不旋转”的起点。</span></p><hr id="lHhMe" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="a39671a7"><span class="ne-text">遍历四个 contact 点</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="vEV0u" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for c in range(4):</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-9"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uec3c1f3c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">逐个检查四个接触通道。</span></p><hr id="a31wU" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="54c2b813"><span class="ne-text">只显示当前处于 planted 的接触点</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="Kaimp" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if contacts[frame, c]:</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-10"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ucb339073" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧这个接触点为真，就把 target 放到地上显示出来；否则不动它。</span></p><h3 id="b91b84f1"><span class="ne-text">含义</span></h3><p id="ubd53edd9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这表示：</span></p><p id="u94bba132" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“这一帧，我认为这个 heel / ball 应该锁地。”</span></p><hr id="FDb4q" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="2b5a053b"><span class="ne-text">把 target 放到 contact 的 XZ 位置</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="PanRk" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts_matrices[c, [0,2], 3] = ogpos[frame, contact_indices[c], [0,2]]</code></pre><p id="u2b89caf8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是这一格里最值得你注意的一句。</span></p><h3 id="646efac8"><span class="ne-text">左边是什么意思</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="k4tIl" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts_matrices[c, [0,2], 3]</code></pre><p id="ub79d601c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">表示：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u368ea396" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">c</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 个 target 的变换矩阵</span></li><li id="u6e8df247" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">把第 4 列里的第 0、2 行，也就是 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">X 和 Z 平移</span></strong></li><li id="u92d90d06" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">设成某个值</span></li></ul><p id="u7ebb0d6e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">换句话说，就是只给 target 设置 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">平面位置</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="BmcXR" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="a5f14954"><span class="ne-text">右边是什么意思</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="ysGEC" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ogpos[frame, contact_indices[c], [0,2]]</code></pre><p id="u364ac145" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">表示：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9bde8b72" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧</span></li><li id="u8de0d43a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前这个 heel/ball 骨点</span></li><li id="u48fd3ea5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">取它的全局位置的 X 和 Z</span></li></ul><p id="uabaf57ef" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而且注意，这里用的是 </span><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">ogpos</span></strong></code><span class="ne-text">，不是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">也就是说，显示出来的 planted marker 是根据</span><strong><span class="ne-text">原始动画的接触点位置</span></strong><span class="ne-text">来的。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><hr id="sBV0J" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="a2f4043f"><span class="ne-text">为什么只设置 XZ，不设置 Y</span></h3><p id="u4a738604" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这个 target 是“地面上的接触标记”。</span></p><p id="u6b7d4845" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 想表达的是：</span></p><p id="u0ae73a22" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“脚该锁在地面平面上的哪个位置。”</span></p><p id="u02380c90" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以他只取：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8bef737c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">X</span></li><li id="ubf71c380" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Z</span></li></ul><p id="ua9883d78" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而把 Y 留在地面高度附近。</span></p><p id="ua0fa1e42" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这也和前面论文/视频的概念一致：接触最终是要“project to ground”。</span></p><hr id="BJq1J" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="cf443da0"><span class="ne-text">画这些 target</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="PAg1Z" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.draw(target, contacts_matrices)</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-11"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u395a28a4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把那 4 个小 target 画出来。</span></p><p id="u353db236" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果某个 contact 没激活，它就不会被移到对应接触点附近；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果激活了，就会出现在地面上相应位置。</span></p><p id="uaf1d4f75" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是你在 viewer 里看到的“当前帧哪些点被判成 planted”。</span></p><hr id="Hmyzh" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="40aa230c"><span class="ne-text">结束 display pass</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="hh5qI" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.end_display()</code></pre><p id="uaeb77e35" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">纯渲染流程收尾。</span></p><hr id="OMnGi" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="e67a197a"><span class="ne-text">画骨架辅助线和坐标轴</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="eXZA6" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.disable(depth_test=True)

viewer.draw_axis(character.world_skeleton_xforms(a), 5)
viewer.draw_lines(character.world_skeleton_lines(a))</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-12"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u281dff2c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把骨架的关节坐标轴和骨骼连线也画出来。</span></p><h3 id="f090517e"><span class="ne-text">为什么要这样做</span></h3><p id="u68f7a1c2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为你现在不是只看 mesh 外观，而是在 debug：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u534164af" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">heel / ball 的骨点位置</span></li><li id="ua9202908" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前整条骨架的姿态</span></li><li id="u7ea7c1bc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">planted marker 和脚的相对关系</span></li></ul><p id="ue4490c27" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这对理解“为什么脚在滑”非常有帮助。</span></p><hr id="atqNq" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="a547d5db"><span class="ne-text">真正执行绘制命令</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="rOUKd" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.execute_commands()</code></pre><p id="u22af0f02" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把前面排队好的绘制操作真正提交给 viewer。</span></p><hr id="UQeyX" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="9bbf3537"><span class="ne-text">让信号图跟着当前帧滚动</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="NvMVG" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ax1.set_xlim([frame-60,frame+60])
ax2.set_xlim([frame-60,frame+60])</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-13"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ude530696" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">让上面的 contact 折线图只显示当前帧前后大约 60 帧范围。</span></p><h3 id="f43bf9f9"><span class="ne-text">为什么这么做</span></h3><p id="u58826406" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不然如果整段动画几百帧全挤在一起，你根本看不出局部 timing。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这样你拖动 timeline 时，图也会跟着滚动，看局部相位关系非常清楚。</span></p><hr id="iLMqz" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="8b4bcc26"><span class="ne-text">最后一段交互控件</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="BQ7vr" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>interact(
    render, 
    frame=lab.Timeline(max=frame_count-1),
    ori = widgets.Checkbox())
viewer</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-14"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uc88385d0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">生成交互控件：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua9f793ac" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">一个时间轴 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame</span></code></li><li id="ue37a1e21" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">一个勾选框 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ori</span></code></li></ul><p id="u50102a8c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">于是你就可以：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ue37d3ae9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">拖动帧号</span></li><li id="u6650e86e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">切换 original / animation</span></li><li id="u7d2430b0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">同时观察角色、地面 planted 标记、contact 曲线</span></li></ul><p id="u5a35469b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这格 cell 的本质就是一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">可视化调试器</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="osEkf" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="3f3ed7b4"><span class="ne-text">六、把这两格 cell 放回整条算法链里</span></h1><p id="ue1cfc76f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">现在你可以把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Find feet contacts timing</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这部分理解成：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="aVEzx" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>原始 mocap original
    ↓
FK 得到 ogpos
    ↓
检查 heel / ball 世界空间速度
    ↓
低于阈值 0.04 → 判定 planted
    ↓
得到四路布尔 contact signal
    ↓
填补 1～2 帧的小洞，减少误判抖动
    ↓
可视化验证：信号曲线 + 地面 contact marker
    ↓
后面才进入：
constraint positions
ankle targets
root placement
leg solving
final processing</code></pre><p id="ub5299ecc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这部分的真正身份是：</span></p><p id="u3755689d" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">前置约束标签提取层 + 调试可视化层</span></strong></p><p id="uce920d59" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而不是 footskate cleanup 求解器本身。Jerome 视频里也是先做这一层，再正式进入 paper 五阶段。</span></p><hr id="tKwaH" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="713c2934"><span class="ne-text">七、你最容易混淆的几个点</span></h1><h2 id="1dc1ee3d"><span class="ne-text">1）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 不是接触位置，只是接触时序</span></h2><p id="u00e78f81" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它只回答：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u42f02535" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这帧左 heel 是不是 planted</span></li><li id="u1e56c20d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这帧左 ball 是不是 planted</span></li><li id="u7dc2ee8f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">…</span></li></ul><p id="uade162ac" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它还没回答“该锁在哪个地面坐标”。</span></p><hr id="sMnkk" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="2807e04a"><span class="ne-text">2）检测基于 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">original</span></code><span class="ne-text">，不是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code></h2><p id="uc1a15229" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是为了避免“用错误结果反推正确标签”。</span></p><hr id="s6yaI" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="59fbd6e8"><span class="ne-text">3）heel 和 ball 分开检测非常重要</span></h2><p id="uf386cdc3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为后面 paper 需要区分：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udb37903c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">单约束</span></li><li id="u79c8ff02" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">双约束</span></li><li id="u9f8793db" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">heel-only</span></li><li id="uffebb356" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ball-only</span></li><li id="ub5eaddae" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">过渡边界</span></li></ul><hr id="xDhV8" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="5f10c27e"><span class="ne-text">4）这两层 smoothing 不是在平滑骨骼，而是在平滑布尔标签</span></h2><p id="uc70541fd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">很多初学者第一次看会误以为它在滤波姿态。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">不是。它只是在修 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这个 on/off 信号。</span></p><hr id="BSCtB" class="ne-hr"><p id="u8f757a7b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果你愿意，下一条我可以继续按同样方式，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">逐行讲解下一节 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">Constraints Positions</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text"> 的 cells</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，也就是从这四路 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 如何继续变成真正的地面约束位置。</span></p></details>

#### 数据流程图
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/__mermaid_v3/5a6de96aafa92dc0967e6c29ecd46ea1.svg)

###  Constraints Positions
[https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69c0f405-5fac-83a5-bdd4-53fb35dd1143](https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69c0f405-5fac-83a5-bdd4-53fb35dd1143)

[https://chatgpt.com/share/69bff041-7584-8009-b181-6252f863e398](https://chatgpt.com/share/69bff041-7584-8009-b181-6252f863e398)

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u5adf2962"><span class="ne-text">GPT</span></summary><p id="u9c2630ba" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">可以，这一节我按你要的顺序来：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="uf7de61e3" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">先说这个步骤的目的</span></strong></li><li id="u9b9408cc" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">再说总体输入和输出</span></strong></li><li id="uc7b6b7da" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">最后把它拆成几个部分，按“论文原意 → Jerome 语音稿 → notebook 代码在干什么”来逐段讲</span></strong></li></ol><p id="u1807b2ce" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你引用的这段英文，其实就是这一步最核心的摘要。论文原文也几乎是同一件事：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">当处理第 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧时，如果某个受约束的 heel 或 ball 在前一帧 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi-1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 已经受约束，就直接沿用前一帧的约束位置；如果不是，就取接下来 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧里的位置做平均，再投影到地面；如果同一只脚的另一个点已经有约束位置，还要把当前点重新摆到与它保持正确 heel-ball 距离的位置上。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的语音稿也完全按这个逻辑解释，并补充说他在 notebook 里是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">整段动画一次性建一个大 buffer</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，而不是严格按论文那样流式逐帧推进。</span></p><hr id="w1QAF" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="dafe785a"><span class="ne-text">一、这一步的目的是什么</span></h1><p id="u1c4b0fed" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步的目的，不是改骨骼，不是解 IK，也不是修 root。</span></p><p id="uf8d95afc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它的目的只有一个：</span></p><p id="u5e835aa0" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把“这一帧这个 heel / ball 应该锁地”这种布尔信号，变成“它应该锁在地面的哪个具体位置”这种三维目标。</span></strong></p><p id="u63c923b1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">前一节 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Find feet contacts timing</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 只回答了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u00d25d04" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 heel 这一帧是不是 planted</span></li><li id="ub8a184e2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 ball 是不是 planted</span></li><li id="ucf03f7e3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右 heel / 右 ball 是不是 planted</span></li></ul><p id="u40357f06" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但它没有回答：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u81248b6b" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">锁在哪里</span></strong></li></ul><p id="u73ce2e20" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Constraints Positions</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 就是在做这个空间化。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">论文把它列成五阶段算法的第一步：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">先为每个 plant constraint 决定一个位置</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，后面 ankle、root、leg 全都依赖这个结果。</span></p><p id="u575a0e6c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把它理解成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u565d1bf1" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 给的是“什么时候锁”</span></li><li id="uf9966c03" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">constraint positions</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 给的是“锁到哪里”</span></li></ul><hr id="O2kyB" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="0483a7d4"><span class="ne-text">二、这一步的总体输入和输出是什么</span></h1><h2 id="e47d59b1"><span class="ne-text">输入</span></h2><p id="u6a5fe76b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一节实际依赖 4 类输入。</span></p><h3 id="pMlz6"><span class="ne-text">1）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code></h3><p id="u6c01d622" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">上一节生成的四路接触时序：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u1c722aec" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left heel</span></li><li id="u5ef2aba9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left ball</span></li><li id="u474c2deb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right heel</span></li><li id="u5af9f4e5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right ball</span></li></ul><p id="ue559a520" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它告诉你某一帧某个点是否该锁地。</span></p><h3 id="5548496d"><span class="ne-text">2）原始动画里 heel / ball 的世界空间位置</span></h3><p id="u563fdc9e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 明确说他这里还是基于原始稳定 mocap 去取这些点的位置，因为 paper 只负责“给定约束后正确修正”，不负责“自动判断什么时候锁脚”。所以约束位置这一步也建立在原始参考运动之上。</span></p><h3 id="82b4db00"><span class="ne-text">3）窗口长度 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1</span></code></h3><p id="u3e508a0f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是向前看多少帧做平均的窗口。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里说他这里用了 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1 = 10</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧。</span></p><h3 id="6dd1ed50"><span class="ne-text">4）heel-ball 的固定几何距离</span></h3><p id="u1268066d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为脚底被当成一个刚体的一部分，所以同一只脚的 heel 和 ball 不能被独立锁到两个互相矛盾的位置。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">论文明确说：如果同脚另一点已经有已知位置，就把当前点挪到“与另一点距离正确”的地面位置上。</span></p><hr id="IDdHY" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="8ba7c3a7"><span class="ne-text">输出</span></h2><p id="u9c039e8e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">输出是一个 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">constraint position buffer</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，也就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf5f9464d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">对每一帧</span></li><li id="u9db70d2b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">对 4 个接触点</span></li><li id="u01631b0e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">都给一个地面目标位置</span></li></ul><p id="u7e97c3e1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里说得很具体：他会建一个大 buffer，维度是整段动画长度 × 四个点 × XYZ 空间坐标。</span></p><p id="u245c4afb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这一节结束后，你得到的不是姿态，而是一张“地面目标点时间表”：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="HDVVR" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>frame i:
  left heel  -&gt; 地面点 A
  left ball  -&gt; 地面点 B
  right heel -&gt; 地面点 C
  right ball -&gt; 地面点 D</code></pre><p id="u953f3a00" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 才会把这些点翻译成脚踝位姿。</span></p><hr id="NzrjF" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="13e23d7c"><span class="ne-text">三、把这一步拆成 5 个部分来看</span></h1><p id="u4701ce31" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">我建议你把 notebook 这一节拆成下面 5 段来看。这样比死记代码行数更容易。</span></p><hr id="MMWv6" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="ddc9f14d"><span class="ne-text">第 1 部分：创建约束位置缓冲区</span></h2><p id="ua7f52276" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一部分代码的职责很简单：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u88ebfb56" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">定义 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1</span></code></li><li id="u320359d4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">创建一个三维 buffer</span></li><li id="u8b323cc2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">准备开始为四个接触点逐帧填目标位置</span></li></ul><h3 id="71287241"><span class="ne-text">这一段为什么存在</span></h3><p id="u19df2797" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 Jerome 这里不是在线逐帧流式处理，而是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">离线地把整段动画一次性算完</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">他自己在语音稿里说了，paper 可以 frame-by-frame 做，也可以维护合适大小的缓冲；但在 notebook 里，最容易实现的是“整段 animation 一次性算一个大 buffer”。</span></p><h3 id="7cc77991"><span class="ne-text">这段在工程上的意义</span></h3><p id="uec88819d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这代表 Jerome 的 notebook 是一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">教学/离线版实现</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uce2ff1e6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先把整条约束时间线算好</span></li><li id="u7a77af6a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再可视化</span></li><li id="u8017132e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再喂给后续 ankle 阶段</span></li></ul><p id="uef8acd25" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而不是严格意义上的在线 runtime solver。</span></p><hr id="eNzfR" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="5b257543"><span class="ne-text">第 2 部分：按帧、按四个点遍历</span></h2><p id="u027b7088" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这部分逻辑本质是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uc5856206" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">对动画的每一帧</span></li><li id="u84a132bc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">对 left heel / left ball / right heel / right ball</span></li><li id="uc7ff962c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">一个个检查</span></li></ul><h3 id="788a6d55"><span class="ne-text">它为什么必须双层循环</span></h3><p id="u4f57bf8a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为约束位置不是“整只左脚一个点、整只右脚一个点”，而是更细的四路信号。</span></p><p id="u45be2954" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这背后是论文的脚模型假设：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">lower body 里，foot 是通过 ankle / heel / ball / toes 来描述的，而不是把整个 foot 粗暴当成一个质点。</span></p><h3 id="xHpMj"><span class="ne-text">工程意义</span></h3><p id="u76bf9ce0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">只有拆成 heel 和 ball 两个点，你后面才区分得出：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua5f33e91" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">heel-only</span></li><li id="u32227aa6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ball-only</span></li><li id="u849219fe" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">heel+ball 双约束</span></li></ul><p id="u6b35d716" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这直接关系到下一节怎么求 ankle。</span></p><hr id="p0xmJ" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="70dfd4ba"><span class="ne-text">第 3 部分：如果前一帧已经 constrained，就直接沿用上一帧位置</span></h2><p id="ud7e93530" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一段就是你引用英文里的第一句：</span></p><p id="u0df392e2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">If it was, we keep the same constraint.</span></p><p id="u0933c426" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原文也是一模一样的意思：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 中受约束的某个 joint，在前一帧 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi-1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 也受约束，那么当前帧的约束位置与前一帧相同。</span></p><p id="u7635e16e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也说得很直白：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u6c428227" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果这一帧说“我在 constraint”</span></li><li id="uc3e2b33d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">并且上一帧已经在 constraint</span></li><li id="ufa399b17" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那平均值已经算过了</span></li><li id="u474d612b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以直接沿用上一帧那个 constraint。</span></li></ul><h3 id="3da1a001"><span class="ne-text">这段代码真正想保住什么</span></h3><p id="u99586006" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它想保住的是：</span></p><p id="uc3793071" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">一个连续 plant 段内部，约束点不要乱跳。</span></strong></p><p id="u97244d1b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 plant 的物理含义本来就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud7510967" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这段时间脚底这个点被“钉”在地上</span></li><li id="u1561f395" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以地面目标点应该恒定</span></li></ul><h3 id="05a3c031"><span class="ne-text">初学者最容易误解的地方</span></h3><p id="u372eec6d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里保持不变的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ueb87582a" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">约束目标点</span></strong></li></ul><p id="u4009cb81" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u462bb84c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始骨点位置</span></li><li id="u8e815c49" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">也不是最终 ankle 位置</span></li></ul><p id="u9cce1272" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">原始 mocap 里 heel / ball 可能还有毫米级抖动；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">但现在我们决定“既然这段时间它要锁地”，那 target 就不该跟着抖。</span></p><hr id="DDHBk" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b743bf3f"><span class="ne-text">第 4 部分：如果这是新进入的一段 constraint，就向前看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧做平均并落地</span></h2><p id="u52482625" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是你引用英文里的第二句：</span></p><p id="u62efdca6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">If it was not, we average the position over the next L1 number of frames and project it on the ground.</span></p><p id="ua3dab9d1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原文同样是这样写的：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">否则，将 joint </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">J</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 在接下来 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧中的位置做平均，再把结果投影到地面上得到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cJ</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u8b069264" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的语音稿也完整解释了这个过程：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u330844d7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">发现 heel 应该静止</span></li><li id="u9e2a9042" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">向前看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧</span></li><li id="u926e1a90" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只要这些帧也仍然在 constraint</span></li><li id="u9e20f627" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">就收集这些位置</span></li><li id="u1645b8e8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">求平均</span></li><li id="ua6c479ba" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后 drop it on the ground</span></li></ul><h3 id="c37afcbf"><span class="ne-text">这一步为什么不是“直接取当前帧”</span></h3><p id="u2e5fb4d8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为接触开始的那几帧里，原始骨点位置可能仍然有微小过渡、轻微抖动或者检测误差。</span></p><p id="u59ed28c6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果你直接取当前帧：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0733c39d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">锁地点会偏噪</span></li><li id="u75524372" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">后面 ankle、leg、root 都会跟着偏</span></li></ul><p id="u87a837b5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而取一个小窗口平均值，相当于在说：</span></p><p id="u22adeb2e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“我不要这个点一进入 constraint 就立刻相信单帧数据，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">我要看这一小段接触整体稳定落在什么地方。”</span></p><h3 id="592d3eab"><span class="ne-text">为什么还要投影到地面</span></h3><p id="uebbc5f84" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为你要的不是原始三维骨点空间位置，而是：</span></p><p id="uab58bc4f" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">脚底接触在地面上的约束点</span></strong></p><p id="u036ef509" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以要把这个平均值“压到地上”。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">notebook 的 markdown 直接就用了 </span><em><span class="ne-text">project it on the ground</span></em><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 口述里则说 </span><em><span class="ne-text">drop it on the ground</span></em><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="fKnEb" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="492145c6"><span class="ne-text">第 5 部分：如果同脚的另一点已经 constrained，就重投影以保持 heel-ball 正确距离</span></h2><p id="u3e3a4808" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是你引用英文里的第三句：</span></p><p id="u0cbab22f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">If the other part of the foot was already constrained ... we realign the current so the distance between heel and ball is correct.</span></p><p id="u5505fee3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原文说得很规范：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">由于 foot 被视为 rigid piece，如果同脚另一 joint </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">J'</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 也受约束且其位置 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cJ'</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 已知，那么当前 joint </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">J</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的约束位置 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cJ</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 必须被重定位到地面上与 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cJ'</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 保持正确距离的最近点。</span></p><p id="u316ef097" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的语音稿把这个过程讲得更直观：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf46108e0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果 heel 先算好了</span></li><li id="u13e44d74" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ball 在这一帧第一次进入 constraint</span></li><li id="u29198677" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那 ball 先落地以后，还得再挪一下</span></li><li id="u89dd3704" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">以保持 heel-ball 的距离不变</span></li><li id="u1fca17a6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">反过来也一样：如果 ball 先锁住，后来 heel 才锁住，就轮到 heel 去对齐这个距离。</span></li></ul><h3 id="ad24888e"><span class="ne-text">这一步为什么非常重要</span></h3><p id="u57a57958" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为如果你不做这个“重对齐”：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3b8a37cd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">heel 锁在地上某点</span></li><li id="u56e174d4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ball 也各自独立锁在另一个平均点</span></li><li id="u8f518096" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但两者之间距离和真实脚长不一致</span></li></ul><p id="u94ac534b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那你下一节在求 ankle 时，就会得到一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">自相矛盾的脚底刚体</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8c07b9f8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">要同时满足 heel 在 H</span></li><li id="u5e135527" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ball 在 B</span></li><li id="ue58749be" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">|H-B|</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 根本不是这只脚原本的长度</span></li></ul><p id="u1f05247c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这种矛盾会直接传到 ankle solver，最后造成奇怪的脚底扭曲或 impossible target。</span></p><h3 id="215dcc73"><span class="ne-text">直觉上这一步在干什么</span></h3><p id="ud92ebc5b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它其实是在加一句额外规则：</span></p><p id="u9c506469" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“虽然 heel / ball 都是单独检测出来的，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">但它们属于同一只脚，不能彼此独立撒野。”</span></p><hr id="LkIDd" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="3277dcc2"><span class="ne-text">四、如果把 notebook 代码翻译成伪代码，大概就是这样</span></h1><p id="u35f71551" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把 Jerome 这一节的实现压成伪代码，大概就是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="oz7UK" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>L1 = 10
constraint_pos_buffer = zeros(frame_count, 4, 3)

for frame in all_frames:
    for contact in [LH, LB, RH, RB]:

        if not contacts[frame, contact]:
            continue

        if frame &gt; 0 and contacts[frame-1, contact]:
            # 已经在上一帧锁住 -&gt; 直接继承
            constraint_pos_buffer[frame, contact] = constraint_pos_buffer[frame-1, contact]

        else:
            # 新开始的一段 contact -&gt; 向前看 L1 帧
            positions = collect_future_positions_while_contact_is_active(...)
            c = average(positions)
            c = project_to_ground(c)

            # 如果同脚另一点已有约束位置 -&gt; 对齐 heel-ball 固定距离
            if paired_contact_already_constrained(...):
                c = realign_to_keep_heel_ball_distance(c, other_c)

            constraint_pos_buffer[frame, contact] = c</code></pre><p id="u15868668" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这不是逐字符抄 notebook，而是把：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uebc6813f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">论文的 4.1 节</span></li><li id="u9abc89fe" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的语音解释</span></li><li id="ubbc35f98" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">以及 notebook 在这一节的实现意图</span></li></ul><p id="u2b06d456" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">重新还原成最清楚的工程逻辑。依据都在你这次给出的文字和上传文件里。</span></p><hr id="l6eaG" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="cac71e5e"><span class="ne-text">五、这一节做完以后，你应该看到什么结果</span></h1><p id="uce7fcde8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里说，算完这一节以后再去显示，就会看到：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub6b438b7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这些 target 不再只是脚底当帧的位置</span></li><li id="uf28ebdbf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而是“一小段时间平均后”的稳定目标</span></li><li id="u6f47b21c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">并且 heel 和 ball 之间的距离始终被保持。</span></li></ul><p id="u9e207d0f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，这一节的结果应该满足 3 个性质：</span></p><h3 id="5b8b2bb4"><span class="ne-text">1）稳定</span></h3><p id="u214825d9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">同一段 plant 里目标点不会一帧一跳。</span></p><h3 id="ce5878ca"><span class="ne-text">2）贴地</span></h3><p id="ubcd6fd65" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">目标点落在地面上。</span></p><h3 id="b4c5464a"><span class="ne-text">3）几何一致</span></h3><p id="ub84920ea" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">同一只脚的 heel 和 ball 距离合理。</span></p><p id="ub2f229b4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">只要这 3 件事成立，下一节 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 才有一个物理上讲得通的输入。</span></p><hr id="CF2pc" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="eb0d6d8e"><span class="ne-text">六、把它和下一节的关系说透</span></h1><p id="ua6a1e7e4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步结束后，你手里有的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud213c37a" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">脚底约束点</span></strong></li><li id="u753e8333" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">还不是脚踝位姿</span></li></ul><p id="ud6a18cd2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">下一节要做的是：</span></p><p id="u1b14be50" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">根据 heel / ball 的约束点，反推 ankle 的 target position 和 target orientation。</span></p><p id="u6a4dc41a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以关系是：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="oFbTL" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>contacts timing
    ↓
constraint positions
    ↓
ankle position / orientation
    ↓
root placement
    ↓
leg solving
    ↓
final smoothing</code></pre><p id="u4abe7f4b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文五阶段也是这个顺序。</span></p><hr id="VcS5v" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="22a5d65d"><span class="ne-text">七、最后给你一句最直观的浓缩版</span></h1><p id="u4458ae80" class="ne-p"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Constraints Positions</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这一节，本质上是在做：</span></p><p id="ue48b46c2" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把“这一帧脚底某个点该锁地”这件事，变成“它应该锁在地面的这个具体位置”，并保证这个位置在时间上稳定、在地面上合理、在脚底几何上自洽。</span></strong></p><hr id="DYj54" class="ne-hr"><p id="u89f298c5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你要是愿意，我下一条可以继续沿着这个结果，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">把 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text"> 重新按同样格式讲一遍，但这次我会严格围绕“它如何消费 constraint position buffer”来讲，这样上下游会更顺。</span></strong></p></details>

### Ankle Position and Orientation
[https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69c1082f-06e4-83a5-98cf-da8ffec297f9](https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69c1082f-06e4-83a5-98cf-da8ffec297f9)

[https://chatgpt.com/share/69c1123d-8124-8009-99e1-7444dd4c74e1](https://chatgpt.com/share/69c1123d-8124-8009-99e1-7444dd4c74e1)

#### 总览
<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="uf212835f"><span class="ne-text">GPT</span></summary><p id="u4fced013" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">好，这一节我按你要的方式来：</span></p><p id="u23f5f7e4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先讲 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">这一步的目的</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，再讲 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">总体输入 / 输出</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，然后把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 拆成几个部分，按 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">“代码在做什么 → 对应论文哪一句 → Jerome 口头是怎么解释的”</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 来讲。</span></p><p id="u1c6aac05" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先说结论：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="uf14c4416" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">这一步的本质，是把上一节得到的“脚底接触点该锁在哪”翻译成“脚踝（ankle）这一帧应该处于什么目标位姿”。</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">也就是：从 heel / ball 的地面约束，反推出 ankle 的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">目标全局朝向</span></strong><span class="ne-text">和</span><strong><span class="ne-text">目标全局位置修正</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，并且让这些修正在时间上连续，不要在 single </span><span class="ne-text">↔</span><span class="ne-text"> double constraint 切换时突然 pop。论文把这定义为第 2 阶段：为每个受约束帧选择满足约束的 ankle 全局位置和朝向，并要求这些调整连续变化。 Jerome 在语音稿里也用几乎同样的话说：双约束时可以精确算 ankle 的 orientation 和 position；单约束时只移动 ankle 的位置、不主动重定向；真正难点是单双约束切换，因此他保存的是相对于原动画的 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">offset</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，再把这个 offset 往前后传播和平滑。</span></p></div><hr id="W5W0G" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="VG5iq"><span class="ne-text">一、这一步的目的是什么</span></h1><p id="u93865fba" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一节不是在做腿部 IK，也不是在挪 root。</span></p><p id="u23e10452" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它属于整条五阶段链路中的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">第二步</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u18d0ab01" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先求 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">constraint positions</span></code></li><li id="ud9af6f67" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再求 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle position and orientation</span></code></li><li id="u1cf493de" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再求 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root placement</span></code></li><li id="u23eebf85" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再用 IK 让腿去满足 ankle 目标</span></li><li id="uac6542b2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span
