class="ne-text">最后做开关边界的平滑 dissipation。</span></li></ol><p id="u8fa7c214" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这里解决的是一个非常明确的问题：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="uc7e957d7" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">上一节已经告诉我 heel / ball 该锁在哪个地面点了，那这一帧整个“脚”应该怎么摆，尤其是 ankle 应该怎么摆？</span></strong></p></div><p id="u127b95d4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原文把这一节说得很清楚：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2cab7159" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">它要为当前帧选择 ankle 的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">全局位置和全局朝向</span></strong></li><li id="u72119048" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这些位置和朝向要满足上一阶段求出的约束位置</span></li><li id="u92f300ed" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">同时这些 ankle 调整必须是连续的，不能一帧一帧突然跳。</span></li></ul><p id="u91981321" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的口述版本更直观：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uaaa58a7c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">double constraint：这只脚 fully constrained，可以算出 ankle 的 orientation 和 position</span></li><li id="uaea31677" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">single constraint：只移动 ankle 的位置，不重新定向</span></li><li id="ud12eaa9c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但 single → double 或 double → single 直接切，会 pop</span></li><li id="u5a4dc8ca" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以保存的是相对原动画的 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">offset</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，不是绝对目标值。</span></li></ul><hr id="xxyfN" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="MoQbq"><span class="ne-text">二、总体输入是什么</span></h1><p id="ue6b2919e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一节开始时，前面已经有三类关键输入了。</span></p><h2 id="FSi3A"><span class="ne-text">1）上一节算出的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">constraint_positions_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="u6aef85dc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是最核心的输入。</span></p><p id="uc82c69f1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它给出每一帧里：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3e16cb3b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left heel 应该锁在哪</span></li><li id="ud5c1e02a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left ball 应该锁在哪</span></li><li id="u5c479bb8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right heel 应该锁在哪</span></li><li id="u50629710" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right ball 应该锁在哪</span></li></ul><p id="ufe6908c3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在前一段语音里已经说明，他先整段算出一个 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">constraint position buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text">，里面保存所有帧、四个接触点的目标位置。</span></p><hr id="fVHSC" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="qcIaV"><span class="ne-text">2）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code></h2><p id="u5a4127f4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是四路接触时序：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua91d0de3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 heel</span></li><li id="u188edf8f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 ball</span></li><li id="u66d4b4bb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右 heel</span></li><li id="u627b47ab" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右 ball</span></li></ul><p id="uff9d3ed1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一节要靠它来判断当前帧属于哪种情况：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u6857e88e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">double constrained</span></li><li id="ua2b0e5bf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">single constrained</span></li><li id="ua810dd85" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">unconstrained</span></li></ul><p id="u82140697" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文就用这三种分类来定义 ankle 的处理逻辑。</span></p><hr id="XNJnl" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="QrjSY"><span class="ne-text">3）原始 ankle 的当前状态</span></h2><p id="ue339c001" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是原动画中这一帧 ankle 本来在哪里、朝哪转。</span></p><p id="u7f61b6e6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里说得很明确：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u38d64d1a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">他不是记“新的绝对 ankle 位姿”，而是记“相对原动画我想施加的 correction offset”。</span></p></div><p id="u761d885a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">从 notebook 后面的可视化 cell 也能看出来，最终显示 ankle 目标时，用的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8eba38f8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agquats[frame, foot]</span></code></li><li id="uf6a85abc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再乘上 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, foot]</span></code></li><li id="ub9c59d79" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[frame, foot]</span></code></li><li id="ucd93a76e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再加上 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer[frame, foot]</span></code><span class="ne-text">。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></li></ul><p id="ub40caa44" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这说明这一步的内部表示就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua69b6e2e" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">rotation offset</span></strong></li><li id="ucbc61683" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">position offset</span></strong></li></ul><p id="u0e2e4fd7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而不是直接覆盖原姿态。</span></p><hr id="TzsMz" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="XeNKy"><span class="ne-text">三、总体输出是什么</span></h1><p id="u695b3922" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步的输出有两个核心 buffer。</span></p><h2 id="JErJY"><span class="ne-text">1）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="uc973419f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">保存每一帧、每只脚的 ankle </span><strong><span class="ne-text">旋转偏移</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u99d65bc8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面的 render cell 直接这么用：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7265ab7c" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 0, :], agquats[frame, LeftFoot])</span></code></li><li id="ua8281347" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 1, :], agquats[frame, RightFoot])</span></code></li></ul><p id="u549b5404" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，buffer 里存的是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">要乘到原始脚踝朝向上的修正四元数</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><hr id="ACV2k" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="X33zC"><span class="ne-text">2）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="ud9a5eb3c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">保存每一帧、每只脚的 ankle </span><strong><span class="ne-text">位置偏移</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ud2ef529b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">render cell 直接这么用：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf52329ea" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[...] + ankle_position_buffer[...]</span></code></li></ul><p id="u1b67c284" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">说明它存的是“相对原动画 ankle 位置，要额外平移多少”。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></p><hr id="hYmk5" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="tk5MD"><span class="ne-text">四、把这一节拆成几个部分来看</span></h1><p id="ub51d876f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">我建议你把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 理解成 5 个小模块：</span></p><pre data-language="latex" id="qnGwT" class="ne-codeblock language-latex"><code>A. 先建两个 ankle buffer
B. 先处理 double constraint
C. 再处理 single constraint 的基础位置偏移
D. 再做 backward / forward 扫描，把 double constraint 的旋转 offset 往邻居帧传播
E. 如果两边都命中，再交叉 blend，得到最终 ankle offset</code></pre><p id="u99a76ec2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这 5 段就是 notebook 这一格在做的事。</span></p><hr id="MCE0B" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="jNhfN"><span class="ne-text">五、Part A：先建 buffer，这一步在存什么</span></h1><p id="u77525229" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一段通常就是类似：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="cpqqG" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>L2 = ...
ankle_rotation_buffer = ...
ankle_position_buffer = ...</code></pre><p id="u3265f320" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">虽然 raw notebook 在网页里被压成少数超长行，这一格代码本体没法像普通 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">.py</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 那样完整逐字符展开，但从 Jerome 的语音稿和后面 render 对这两个 buffer 的消费方式，可以非常确定它就在做这件事。Jerome 直接说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uadeabea6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“I will have my buffer for the ankle rotation”</span></li><li id="u1c59594a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“and the buffer for the ankle position”</span></li><li id="u09b2f26e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“for both left and right”</span></li></ul><hr id="D2LAY" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="thKO5"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是什么</span></h2><p id="u2341f213" class="ne-p"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是 ankle 阶段的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">邻域窗口长度</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u38154aec" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原文明确说：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u48243347" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一阶段假设在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi-L2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi+L2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的范围内，constraint positions 都已知。</span></p></div><p id="u9f16bc0d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它的工程含义就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u61780b23" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧的 ankle 修正，不只看当前帧</span></li><li id="u50f4c6b4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">还会看前后附近若干帧</span></li><li id="u7397d319" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">用来把单双约束切换时的变化摊平</span></li></ul><hr id="q3bGP" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="ThMi6"><span class="ne-text">为什么 buffer 存的是 offset，不是绝对值</span></h2><p id="u33381a05" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是 Jerome 这版 notebook 的灵魂。</span></p><p id="u31663838" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">他说得非常明确：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="uac313290" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不要把“脚最后该在哪”记成绝对位姿；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">要记成“相对原动画差了多少”，也就是 offset；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">然后把这个 offset 往前后传播、慢慢衰减。</span></p></div><p id="uf29ebd1f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是为什么后面 render 里是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf98dda73" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始 ankle quaternion × </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li><li id="ue6dc5a75" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始 ankle position + </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text">。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></li></ul><hr id="ZnP3S" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="NF0wc"><span class="ne-text">六、Part B：先处理 double constraint</span></h1><p id="u247e2ed5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这部分是最“干净”的部分，因为几何上最明确。</span></p><p id="u0206ed20" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文定义：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ubdf18465" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果 heel 和 ball 都 constrained</span></li><li id="ue257738c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那么这个 ankle 是 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">doubly constrained</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></li></ul><p id="u33611c10" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 也说：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u84df35c6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“the simplest case”<br /></span><span class="ne-text">“I have a fully constrained foot”<br /></span><span class="ne-text">“I can compute the ankle orientation and the position.”</span></p></div><hr id="a8sHD" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="kwNRn"><span class="ne-text">这一段代码在逻辑上做什么</span></h2><p id="u23afc9e3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">可以把它理解成两步。</span></p><h3 id="Ms1Xu"><span class="ne-text">第一步：先求目标朝向</span></h3><p id="u26a6d36f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文给出的公式是：</span></p><p id="u2e1adc15" class="ne-p"><img src="https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2026/png/45532692/1774258607634-8c70ee11-d827-4c57-ae39-02a2ad871f52.png" width="260" id="ua4b4ceb3" class="ne-image"></p><p id="uf8efd1d1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">[<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Q'_A = Rot(p_B - p_H,\ c_B - c_H) * Q_A<br /></span><span class="ne-text">]</span></p><p id="uf04b9227" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">意思是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u67a65da3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始 heel→ball 的方向是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pB - pH</span></code></li><li id="u0090fa84" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">目标 heel→ball 的方向是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cB - cH</span></code></li><li id="u7fdb4dd9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先算一个最短旋转，把原始足底轴对齐到目标足底轴</span></li><li id="u08feeb60" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后把这个旋转乘到原始 ankle 全局朝向 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">QA</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 上，得到新 ankle 的全局朝向。</span></li></ul><h3 id="hljQF"><span class="ne-text">第二步：再求目标位置</span></h3><p id="u4ee25cff" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文接着给出：</span></p><p id="u55dd7bb4" class="ne-p"><img src="https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2026/png/45532692/1774258618882-6d08a681-5ec4-4642-8629-b668b33f58cd.png" width="217.6" id="uba5d0ac2" class="ne-image"></p><p id="ude0bf34d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">[<br /></span><span class="ne-text">p'_A = c_H - Q'_A(o_H)<br /></span><span class="ne-text">]</span></p><p id="u8e1f4974" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ue62ff7dc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">已知 heel 最终必须落在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cH</span></code></li><li id="u5d1e2f0c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">已知 heel 相对 ankle 的局部偏移 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">oH</span></code></li><li id="u54493346" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">又已知新的 ankle 朝向是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Q'A</span></code></li><li id="uef9bf4d6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以就能反推出 ankle 的全局位置。</span></li></ul><hr id="uY8gz" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="QrObo"><span class="ne-text">notebook 里这一段保存的不是绝对位姿</span></h2><p id="ue91ca0a9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的实现不是把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Q'A</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">p'A</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 原样存下来，而是把它们转成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uce166cc5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">相对原动画的 rotation offset</span></li><li id="u5d68444b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">相对原动画的 position offset</span></li></ul><p id="u80dd3589" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样做的原因只有一个：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="ue2c6f550" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面 single </span><span class="ne-text">↔</span><span class="ne-text"> double 切换时，offset 更容易平滑传播。</span></p></div><hr id="DlPPY" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="OUbfK"><span class="ne-text">七、Part C：single constraint 的基础处理</span></h1><p id="u449312cd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">当只有 heel 或只有 ball 被锁住时，逻辑就变了。</span></p><p id="u72b5015e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua3aad61e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">single constrained ankle：只有一个子点被约束</span></li><li id="u18b5dcb2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">给定任何 ankle 朝向，都能通过平移 ankle 让那个点落到目标位置</span></li><li id="u3d2da773" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以最自然的做法是：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">保留原来朝向，只做平移</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></li></ul><p id="u4393e76d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里的说法几乎一模一样：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u2d240706" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“you only have to do is not compute any reorientation of the ankle”<br /></span><span class="ne-text">“you only move the position.”</span></p></div><hr id="qujv6" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="qpm8U"><span class="ne-text">这段代码逐行在干什么</span></h2><p id="u907ab008" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">逻辑上等价于：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="u7R7N" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if single_constraint:
    rotation_offset = identity
    position_offset = cJ - pJ</code></pre><p id="u137457c2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">其中：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf1e95b75" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">J</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是当前被锁的那个点，可能是 heel，也可能是 ball</span></li><li id="ub4471979" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pJ</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是它当前在原动画里的全局位置</span></li><li id="ua3952f7f" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cJ</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是上一节求出来的约束地面点</span></li></ul><p id="u32b2313e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8c443cd6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">rotation buffer 这里基本不加新旋转</span></li><li id="u13c5d966" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">position buffer 只存一个平移量，把 J 拉到 cJ</span></li></ul><hr id="w3UR0" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="lpxl1"><span class="ne-text">为什么不能到这里就结束</span></h2><p id="u2e28c573" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为如果你只做这一步，会在下面这种切换时爆出 pop：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud4d873f5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧：single constraint，只做平移</span></li><li id="u31d96d48" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">下一帧：double constraint，突然又多出一个旋转修正</span></li></ul><p id="ub2f42e26" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里专门说了：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="ub854f0a6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果你这样直接从 single 切到 double，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">“you will pop in that right position instantly”。</span></p></div><p id="ufd54ebe6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以 single constraint 这一步只是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">基础偏移</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，还不够。</span></p><hr id="Sj5Kt" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="Nm8Sy"><span class="ne-text">八、Part D：backward scan，检查“前面是不是 double”</span></h1><p id="u7868d9f6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是这一节最关键的平滑逻辑之一。</span></p><p id="u40f49fdf" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的话很明确：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud7076e4c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧是 single constraint</span></li><li id="u3250ae91" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">就往回看</span></li><li id="ubb3e94de" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">看看是不是从一个 double constraint 过来的</span></li><li id="uccf4269a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果是，就把那边的 rotation offset 慢慢衰减传播回来。</span></li></ul><hr id="SDZHP" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="dHfGZ"><span class="ne-text">代码本质在干什么</span></h2><p id="u66468a15" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">可以抽象成：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="TaGqi" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L2):
    if frame-f 是最近一个 double-constrained 帧:
        alpha = ...
        取那一帧的 rotation offset / position offset
        乘上 alpha
        混到当前帧
        break</code></pre><h3 id="Kc8AK"><span class="ne-text">为什么先传播旋转，再传播位置</span></h3><p id="ua03ac0f7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 口头解释是：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u090bf6cb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“first thing you do is that you rotate the ankle back in time<br /></span><span class="ne-text">and then you move the foot.” </span></p></div><p id="u2ffa608c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这背后的几何直觉是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3f2240c7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">double constraint 里，脚的完整朝向已经被重新定了</span></li><li id="uad7e123a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">single constraint 如果完全不带这点旋转记忆，会突然丢失足底姿态</span></li><li id="ubaa10eb8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以先把“来自 double frame 的那一点朝向修正”慢慢往单约束段里退火</span></li></ul><hr id="STGr6" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Gc2dD"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 在做什么</span></h2><p id="u1b7cc6b6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里用的不是纯线性权重，而是 paper 里的 cubic ease-in / ease-out：</span></p><p id="u40efe15f" class="ne-p"><img src="https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2026/png/45532692/1774260083671-2feda3c1-a9f4-4b8d-8320-2b7c708a0e43.png" width="176" id="udbf01b83" class="ne-image"></p><p id="u8b5f0ec8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">[<br /></span><span class="ne-text">\alpha(t)=2t</span><sup><span class="ne-text">3-3t</span></sup><span class="ne-text">2+1<br /></span><span class="ne-text">]</span></p><p id="u0ea93268" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里特地强调：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8b2b2800" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“they don't do purely linear correction”</span></li><li id="u3e75ed52" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“they do ease in / ease out correction”</span></li><li id="u22f3e545" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“the formula for alpha comes from the paper.”</span></li></ul><p id="u39c98c07" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这段代码里的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 作用就是：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u13081789" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">距离最近的 double frame 越近，继承的 offset 越多；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">越远，继承得越少；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">并且衰减曲线是平滑的，不会硬折线。</span></p></div><hr id="MY02o" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="J1UNW"><span class="ne-text">九、Part E：forward scan，检查“后面是不是会进入 double”</span></h1><p id="u3007dc78" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">反方向同理。</span></p><p id="u9944531d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 也说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ubd6979c8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果当前 single，往前看</span></li><li id="u693b73a8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果未来不远处会进入 double constraint</span></li><li id="u66b61e70" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那也要提前吃进去一点将来的 offset</span></li><li id="u1d19fdbf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这样 single → double 不会在切换帧瞬间爆出来。</span></li></ul><hr id="w9gzo" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Mt6WR"><span class="ne-text">代码本质在做什么</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="ypL1l" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L2):
    if frame+f 是最近一个 future double-constrained 帧:
        alpha = ...
        取 future 的 offset
        乘上 alpha
        形成一个 forward contribution
        break</code></pre><h3 id="v31BH"><span class="ne-text">你可以把它理解成“预热”</span></h3><p id="u04fabbff" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不是等脚真的进入 fully constrained 再一下子改脚踝，</span></p><p id="u603eed42" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u92df471b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">前面几帧先偷偷改一点点</span></li><li id="ude29abf5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">越接近真正的 double frame，改得越多</span></li></ul><hr id="c8LDr" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="Amsho"><span class="ne-text">十、Part F：如果前后都命中，就 crossover 两边结果</span></h1><p id="ufe1b1de9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个是论文 Figure 3 的第三种情况，也是 Jerome 口头提到的 “weird case”。</span></p><p id="u2043baa9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u1c18764a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当两边都存在要 blend off 的 adjustment</span></li><li id="u9ae34aae" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">就需要把 forward 和 backward 两个修正再 blend 一次。</span></li></ul><p id="u9a66b0f0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的原话是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uaae0b8f0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“if you have that weird case when you actually go from double constraint back double constraint”</span></li><li id="u57f042a6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“you need to cross over that information.”</span></li></ul><hr id="YanBO" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Oc7gY"><span class="ne-text">这段代码在做什么</span></h2><p id="u8c442055" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">逻辑上大概是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="lRznv" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if backward_hit and forward_hit:
    local_quat = slerp(back_quat, front_quat, alpha_cross)
    local_pos  = lerp(back_pos, front_pos, alpha_cross)
elif only_backward_hit:
    用 backward
elif only_forward_hit:
    用 forward
else:
    用当前基础 single result</code></pre><h3 id="hXFMN"><span class="ne-text">为什么 rotation 用 slerp</span></h3><p id="u4ccc9fb6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 rotation 是 quaternion，不能线性插值。</span></p><p id="u75e66a6c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文在 Section 4.2 / 4.5 都是用 quaternion blending 的思想处理旋转平滑。</span></p><h3 id="ZU4xv"><span class="ne-text">为什么 position 用普通线性混合</span></h3><p id="u30b21b32" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 position offset 是 3D 向量，直接 lerp 就行。</span></p><p id="u12c39ea1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在口头里虽然主要强调的是 rotation 的“slerp that stuff”，但整体逻辑就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uc9f80779" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">rotation：球面插值</span></li><li id="u24025938" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">translation：线性插值</span></li></ul><hr id="DskZ3" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="cmZBa"><span class="ne-text">十一、这一节结束后，buffer 里到底是什么</span></h1><p id="u6dc7b56d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">到这一节结束时，你已经有了：</span></p><h2 id="tBWU1"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="u29cf7f35" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">每帧每只脚一个 quaternion correction。</span></p><h2 id="ZEhfq"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="uf4cf1ca1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">每帧每只脚一个 translation correction。</span></p><p id="u67a106d2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而且它们不是“生硬的约束解”，而是已经过了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9e533dba" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">double constraint 直接求解</span></li><li id="u805f855c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">single constraint 基础平移</span></li><li id="u5d080164" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">backward blend</span></li><li id="u2891b844" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">forward blend</span></li><li id="u6573270e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">crossover blend</span></li></ul><p id="ufe719a94" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">之后的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">平滑 ankle offset</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ua07e54d3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也说：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u5616ecdc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">算完之后，你看到的 buffer 里那些位置，就是“我想让 ankle 去的地方”。</span></p></div><p id="u7ff50349" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面的 render cell 正是拿这两个 buffer 去画 ankle 坐标轴：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf8a7926e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左脚：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer[frame,0]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer[frame,0]</span></code></li><li id="u0ad4c56e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右脚：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer[frame,1]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer[frame,1]</span></code><span class="ne-text">。(</span><a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" data-href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/logic-three-body/AnimationTech-HTC/refs/heads/main/labs/AnimationPapers/Footskate%20Cleanup%20for%20Motion%20Capture%20Editing.ipynb" target="_blank" class="ne-link"><span class="ne-text">GitHub</span></a><span class="ne-text">)</span></li></ul><hr id="gnfdY" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="eznoE"><span class="ne-text">十二、把这一步浓缩成一句最直观的话</span></h1><p id="ucfdf134c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 理解成：</span></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u0be83f3a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先把 heel / ball 的地面约束点，翻译成 ankle 的目标位姿；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">双约束时，脚是 fully constrained，直接反推 ankle 的位置和朝向；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">单约束时，只保留原朝向，先做位置修正；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">然后再把 double constraint 产生的旋转 / 平移 offset 往相邻 single 段前后传播，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">用一个 cubic alpha 做平滑衰减，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这样单双约束切换时 ankle 不会突然 pop。</span></p></div><hr id="X0aYR" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="NG4H6"><span class="ne-text">十三、你学习这一节时最该盯住的 6 个关键词</span></h1><p id="u9dd8edd5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你之后回看 notebook，只要盯住这 6 个词，这节就不会散：</span></p><h2 id="OYGNx"><span class="ne-text">1）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">constraint_positions_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="u06c49d66" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">上一节给的 heel / ball 目标地面点。</span></p><h2 id="KxUsH"><span class="ne-text">2）double constrained</span></h2><p id="u00bd71e6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">heel 和 ball 都锁，能直接求 ankle 朝向 + 位置。</span></p><h2 id="r60vw"><span class="ne-text">3）single constrained</span></h2><p id="u7e4e6e55" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">只锁一个点，默认不改朝向，只做平移。</span></p><h2 id="maoyA"><span class="ne-text">4）offset</span></h2><p id="u24900d43" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">保存相对原动画的 correction，而不是绝对位姿。</span></p><h2 id="ika5z"><span class="ne-text">5）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L2</span></code></h2><p id="u1f4d4cdc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">前后看多少帧，决定平滑影响范围。</span></p><h2 id="IMhSd"><span class="ne-text">6）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code></h2><p id="u8b321388" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不是线性衰减，而是 cubic ease-in / ease-out。</span></p><hr id="LAKuh" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="vwXam"><span class="ne-text">十四、和你未来 UE / GASPALS / Shadow Mode 的关系</span></h1><p id="ubdbba390" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一节如果放到你未来的 GASPALS / Shadow Mode 里，它非常像一个独立可替换的小模块：</span></p><h2 id="gf3in"><span class="ne-text">它属于哪一层</span></h2><p id="u032f429a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它属于：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub95a9c19" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">接触约束 → 脚踝目标位姿生成层</span></strong></li></ul><p id="u8873936d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在动画链里，它的位置大概是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u4d4893d3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Motion Matching / Base Locomotion 输出之后</span></li><li id="ufb4b00b8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Leg IK / FBIK 之前</span></li></ul><h2 id="dDSkY"><span class="ne-text">它吃什么</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ubf53ee0d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">foot contact timing</span></li><li id="ub07df34d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">contact positions</span></li><li id="ud807988f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前原始脚踝姿态</span></li></ul><h2 id="UbSNa"><span class="ne-text">它吐什么</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u03b22784" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left/right ankle target offsets</span></li></ul><h2 id="i6q70"><span class="ne-text">为什么适合 Shadow Mode</span></h2><p id="u8dc9ffb7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为它边界特别干净：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9258dc3f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">输入明确</span></li><li id="u39a5c6e0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">输出明确</span></li><li id="ud5dea749" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">很容易 A/B 对比</span></li></ul><p id="ued705300" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你以后无论是保留规则法，还是想用 LMM / Control Operators 去学一个更聪明的 foot target layer，都可以把这一层当成非常清楚的接管点。GASPALS 的运动系统本质上也是“输入意图 → base pose → 再叠加修正”的架构，适合把这种 ankle target 逻辑挂在 locomotion 主干和后续 IK / post-process 之间。</span></p><hr id="z4bey" class="ne-hr"><p id="u4b8e3c24" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">下一条我可以继续用同样的粒度，给你讲 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Root Placement</span></code><span class="ne-text">，也就是这一步算完 ankle target 之后，为什么还要先挪 hips/root，而不是直接开始掰腿。</span></p></details>
| single constrain：单脚一个target |
| --- |
| ![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2026/png/45532692/1774259018967-6d2d237c-4626-41fe-950e-97d5e2bdc3de.png) |


#### 反向传播，信号均摊
<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u4e2ca8c1"><span class="ne-text">GPT(反向传播；均摊)</span></summary><p id="u0358f82c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里的“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">反向传播</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”不是机器学习里那个 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">backpropagation / 梯度反传</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ucea750c2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这一步里，它的意思是：</span></p><p id="uf5404d86" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把某个已经算出来的 ankle 修正 offset，沿着时间轴往更早的帧“倒着扩散”一点。</span></strong></p><p id="ub34b17e4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也可以叫：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u94d5eb2e" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">往回传播</span></strong></li><li id="ub4e4af6f" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">向过去帧扩散</span></strong></li><li id="u2dbc31b0" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">back in time</span></strong></li><li id="ued66bdc0" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">把 future/past 的 double-constraint 修正提前或延后摊开</span></strong></li></ul><hr id="oxcup" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="ad9e95a5"><span class="ne-text">一、先用最直观的话解释</span></h1><p id="u4808b2e0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">假设你现在有这样一段接触状态：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="NPnh6" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>帧 10  11  12  13  14  15
    S   S   S   S   S   D</code></pre><p id="u5ddb4169" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">其中：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7644fbd5" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">S</span></code><span class="ne-text"> = single constraint</span></li><li id="uecd6e39f" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">D</span></code><span class="ne-text"> = double constraint</span></li></ul><p id="u956848ac" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">到了第 15 帧，脚突然从 single 变成 double。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">double constraint 会比 single 多出一个“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">脚踝旋转修正</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”，因为这时 heel 和 ball 都锁住了，脚底方向被更完整地确定了。</span></p><p id="ued798486" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果你只在第 15 帧才突然把这段旋转修正加上去，会发生什么？</span></p><p id="uc0999844" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">角色脚踝会在第 14 → 15 帧突然“啪”地转一下。</span></p><p id="u2e90c0c5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是 pop。</span></p><p id="u6a8e61bb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以算法会说：</span></p><p id="ud081095c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不要等到第 15 帧才突然全部加上。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">把第 15 帧那段旋转 correction，提前分一点给第 14 帧，再少一点给第 13 帧，再少一点给第 12 帧……</span></p><p id="u67953e35" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这件事，就是你说的“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">反向传播</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”。</span></p><p id="u8c8225f0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="bEHLC" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>15帧的修正
↓
往前摊给 14、13、12...</code></pre><p id="uea951ce2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以它本质上是：</span></p><p id="u17252e8b" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把将来会出现的脚踝修正，提前在更早的帧里预热一点。</span></strong></p><hr id="Og7j3" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="511e86f9"><span class="ne-text">二、为什么要这样做</span></h1><p id="uae79f750" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">single constraint</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">double constraint</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的 ankle 解法不一样：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud73d5e07" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">single</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：通常只做位置修正，不主动重定向 ankle</span></li><li id="u5f1a7b9f" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">double</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：会解出完整的 ankle 朝向 + 位置</span></li></ul><p id="uf51f2e38" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以如果你不做传播，状态切换时会出现：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="ZXXxo" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>前一帧：只有 position correction
后一帧：突然多出 rotation correction</code></pre><p id="u8b63cd24" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就会造成非常明显的脚踝跳变。</span></p><p id="u6846d710" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以算法的核心思路是：</span></p><p id="uabc4bf30" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把“突然出现的修正”变成“提前慢慢长出来的修正”。</span></strong></p><hr id="e4cSM" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="c54216ee"><span class="ne-text">三、它在代码里对应哪一段</span></h1><p id="ufed07166" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你补齐的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">compute_single_constrained_ankle()</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 里，其实有两段相关逻辑：</span></p><hr id="LoC2A" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="cbfe63f4"><span class="ne-text">1）backward scan</span></h2><p id="ued6800ad" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是你看到的这种：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="bA7sK" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L2):
    if frame-f &gt;= 0 and contacts[frame-f, heel] and contacts[frame-f, ball]:
        ...</code></pre><p id="ueb7d7cfb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段是在干什么？</span></p><p id="uda0da641" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它是在看：</span></p><p id="u1ecffdd6" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">当前 frame 是 single constraint 时，往前找，看看过去最近有没有 double-constrained 帧。</span></strong></p><p id="u8184bacb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果找到了过去某一帧 double constraint，说明：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u5c0acb25" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">之前脚踝曾经有一套“完整约束下算出来的 rotation offset”</span></li><li id="u86da4179" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那么当前 single 帧不应该完全丢掉这套信息</span></li><li id="ubdfe2032" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">应该让它</span><strong><span class="ne-text">向当前帧慢慢淡出</span></strong></li></ul><p id="ufba2bcf2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个方向更准确地说，是：</span></p><p id="uaeb77816" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">从过去的 double 帧，把修正延续到现在。</span></strong></p><p id="u9f96c5ec" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">向前延续</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”。</span></p><hr id="nhXBl" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="f399e0e4"><span class="ne-text">2）forward scan</span></h2><p id="ue4a1d8c4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="Y41ZV" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L2):
    if frame+f &lt; frame_count and contacts[frame+f, heel] and contacts[frame+f, ball]:
        ...</code></pre><p id="u0696a409" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段是在看：</span></p><p id="u5f637b7e" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">当前 frame 是 single constraint 时，往后找，看看未来最近会不会进入 double constraint。</span></strong></p><p id="u8894ec03" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果未来某一帧会进入 double constraint，说明：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf3fcbcfb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">将来会出现一段新的 rotation offset</span></li><li id="u0aa6d051" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那么当前帧不应该等到那一帧再突然吃满</span></li><li id="u6c3b07b7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而应该把未来的那套修正，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">往当前帧提前摊一点</span></strong></li></ul><p id="ud9fe0959" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个才更符合你说的“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">反向传播</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”直觉：</span></p><p id="ucd8bd3d2" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">从未来的 double 帧，把修正往更早帧倒着传播回来。</span></strong></p><p id="ude2183e8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="rprbE" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>future double → current single</code></pre><hr id="PCRAw" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="0d7c8ae0"><span class="ne-text">四、所以“反向传播”到底指哪一个方向</span></h1><p id="u2186d045" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">严格说，这一步里有两个时间方向：</span></p><hr id="PVwmB" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="dc7c3820"><span class="ne-text">情况 A：从过去 double 向现在淡出</span></h2><p id="u1926eb65" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u38009bca" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">过去是 double</span></li><li id="uedda735a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">现在变成 single</span></li></ul><p id="u966ce564" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那就要把过去 double 的修正，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">向现在缓慢消退</span></strong></p><p id="u233e5fcd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个方向你可以理解成：</span></p><p id="u897c4d02" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">正向延续 / 向后淡出</span></strong></p><hr id="piYIl" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="737dbc46"><span class="ne-text">情况 B：从未来 double 向现在预热</span></h2><p id="ucafc7edb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u6b91754c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">现在是 single</span></li><li id="u1c73b3ff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">未来会进入 double</span></li></ul><p id="u8d5a24bb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那就要把未来 double 的修正，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">向当前帧提前倒灌一点</span></strong></p><p id="uf6451696" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个方向你可以理解成：</span></p><p id="u0d4f2987" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">反向传播 / 往回传播 / back in time</span></strong></p><hr id="lRbG5" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="54bbba80"><span class="ne-text">结论</span></h2><p id="u482f4d4f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果你说“这个步骤中的反向传播”，通常更接近指：</span></p><p id="u4e7edf51" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把未来某个 double-constraint 帧里已经知道的 ankle correction，往更早的 single 帧回传一点。</span></strong></p><p id="u5b30facf" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为它确实是在“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">回到过去帧</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”。</span></p><p id="u2ed59148" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但完整算法其实是双向的：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udc98e3a1" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">backward scan</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：看过去 double</span></li><li id="ub8e4dc44" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">forward scan</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：看未来 double</span></li><li id="ubedd806a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果两边都有，就把两边结果再 blend 一次</span></li></ul><hr id="J0fgz" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="d958af14"><span class="ne-text">五、为什么保存的是 offset，而不是绝对 ankle 目标</span></h1><p id="uc9944d1d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正是 Jerome 一直强调的点。</span></p><p id="u8c14fcf5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为你要传播的是一种“修正量”，不是“最终答案”。</span></p><p id="u57ab2d6b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">比如原动画脚踝在这里：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="hfwPA" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>原动画 ankle = A
double constraint 算出来的 ankle = B</code></pre><p id="u9d4ea1e4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那你真正保存的是：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="lGnxS" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>offset = B - A</code></pre><p id="u6a1df940" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">然后做传播时不是说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u04933c48" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 14 帧直接跳到 B</span></li><li id="u7d355a81" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 13 帧直接跳到 B</span></li></ul><p id="ua8ab5d95" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而是说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u4aaaa831" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 14 帧先加 80% offset</span></li><li id="u072161f6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 13 帧加 50% offset</span></li><li id="ua017eb46" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 12 帧加 20% offset</span></li></ul><p id="udc741f0c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以本质是：</span></p><p id="u50b225e9" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">传播 correction，不是传播最终 pose。</span></strong></p><p id="uc1c4bda8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这也是为什么 buffer 里存的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ueb0dce0f" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u3ed6d938" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="u744a402a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而不是“最终 ankle absolute pose”。</span></p><hr id="ASA18" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="e6ea3557"><span class="ne-text">六、α 在这里起什么作用</span></h1><p id="ucaffa29b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">传播不是硬拷贝，而是衰减传播。</span></p><p id="ud3d06b7f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">代码里会先算：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="vXlt9" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)
alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1</code></pre><p id="u4aee8b6d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这个 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的意思就是：</span></p><p id="u477e50da" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">离那个 double frame 越近，吃进去的修正越多；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">离得越远，吃进去的修正越少。</span></p><p id="uff6ae6ca" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以反向传播不是“整段复制过去”，而是：</span></p><p id="u083ae15b" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">一个随时间逐渐变小的 correction 扩散。</span></strong></p><p id="u515d4cc8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是为什么它看起来更像“过渡带”，而不是“突然切换”。</span></p><hr id="jOMXa" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="8c222e07"><span class="ne-text">七、给你一个完整的小例子</span></h1><p id="u1c65e899" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">假设：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="OtYAl" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>帧号:   20  21  22  23  24  25
状态:    S   S   S   S   S   D</code></pre><p id="ua6a78bc3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">第 25 帧算出：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u837eee26" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">一段 ankle rotation offset = </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">R</span></code></li></ul><p id="u40040004" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果不传播：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="q1h6x" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>20: I
21: I
22: I
23: I
24: I
25: R</code></pre><p id="uc0d378d1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那么 24 → 25 会突然跳。</span></p><p id="u2989d3a1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果做“反向传播”：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="o3SAM" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>20: 0.1R
21: 0.2R
22: 0.35R
23: 0.55R
24: 0.8R
25: 1.0R</code></pre><p id="u939b5fcd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样到了第 25 帧，脚踝早就开始朝那个目标方向“靠拢”了，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以切换会自然得多。</span></p><hr id="xehqg" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="29ef2ecb"><span class="ne-text">八、这一步和 Root / IK 的关系</span></h1><p id="u58cf6d78" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一点也很重要。</span></p><p id="uab07a784" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里传播的不是最终腿部姿态，而只是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub3913179" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">ankle rotation offset</span></strong></li><li id="u87257811" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">ankle position offset</span></strong></li></ul><p id="u13ee8329" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，这一节只是先把：</span></p><p id="u9e60ec96" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“脚踝目标” 做平滑</span></p><p id="uae145943" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面的：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ucd691327" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Root Placement</span></li><li id="u0ecb7d1c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Leg IK</span></li></ul><p id="uee735b43" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">才会去真正让整条腿跟着这个平滑目标走。</span></p><p id="u9cb8041a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以你可以把这里的“反向传播”理解成：</span></p><p id="uda5947a8" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">先把末端目标的时序做顺，再让骨架去追它。</span></strong></p><hr id="YX3dO" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="afcd12eb"><span class="ne-text">九、最短记忆版</span></h1><p id="u63912697" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以直接记成这三句：</span></p><h3 id="c96e5f6f"><span class="ne-text">1）这里的反向传播不是梯度反传</span></h3><p id="u737d8fda" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">沿时间轴把 ankle correction 往更早帧扩散</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="32365205"><span class="ne-text">2）它解决的问题是</span></h3><p id="u5076391f" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">single </span></strong><strong><span class="ne-text">↔</span></strong><strong><span class="ne-text"> double constraint 切换时，ankle correction 不能突然开/关。</span></strong></p><h3 id="7bf7e1e8"><span class="ne-text">3）它传播的不是最终脚踝绝对位置</span></h3><p id="u259d3b44" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">相对原动画的 offset</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，并且用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 做衰减。</span></p><hr id="ftLxL" class="ne-hr"><p id="u1f275991" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果你愿意，我下一条可以直接给你画一张：</span></p><p id="u1d8a10b2" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">“single → double 时，ankle offset 如何反向传播”的 mermaid 时间轴图</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p></details>
#### 数据流程图
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/__mermaid_v3/881c1fc1f38df48fa0238e5760c1ae04.svg)

### Root Position
[https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1/c/69c112a0-e0b0-83a6-8eeb-b9c51323986a](https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1/c/69c112a0-e0b0-83a6-8eeb-b9c51323986a)

[https://chatgpt.com/share/69c11fc4-e388-8009-8701-d8b47d35d9e5](https://chatgpt.com/share/69c11fc4-e388-8009-8701-d8b47d35d9e5)

#### 总览
<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="udb9c79a8"><span class="ne-text">GPT</span></summary><p id="u0b870197" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">好，这一步我按你指定的方式来讲：</span></p><p id="u2302e485" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先讲 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">Root Position / Root Placement 这一步的目的</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，再讲 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">整体输入 / 输出</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，然后把你贴的整块代码拆成几个部分，逐段解释每一行在做什么、它和论文/语音稿分别是什么关系。最后我给你一张 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">mermaid 流程图</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ud93f0cfb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先给你一句最直观的话：</span></p><p id="ud35b3f02" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">这一步不是在“真正解腿 IK”，而是在 IK 之前，先把骨盆 / root 往一个更合理的位置轻轻挪一下，让腿尽量够得着前一步已经算好的 ankle 目标。</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">论文第三阶段的原话就是：先计算 root 应该放在哪里，使得上一步得到的 ankle 目标位置能被腿通过伸展所到达；同时又要兼顾 root 调整的平滑性，所以有时 ankle 目标会被允许“略微超出可达范围”，剩余误差再由下一步轻微拉伸腿去补。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也用更直白的话说：如果你把脚修到一个离髋太远的位置，腿根本够不到，所以这一步就是把 hips 往能让脚够到的位置拉；若只有一只脚受约束，就沿那只脚的方向拉；若两只脚都受约束，就要找“两条腿都能够到”的位置，也就是两个球体可达域的交集附近。</span></p><hr id="OPr5L" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="j4z3H"><span class="ne-text">一、这一步的目的是什么</span></h1><p id="u209c8a3a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步在整条五阶段链路里的位置是：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="ude3627b7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Constraint Positions</span></li><li id="uda6c6283" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Ankle Position and Orientation</span></li><li id="uce75db62" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">Root Placement</span></strong></li><li id="uec79756c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Meet Target Ankle Configuration（腿部 IK）</span></li><li id="u89514abc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Final smoothing / dissipation</span></li></ol><p id="u076f273f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，前两步已经给你了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0f7ef2be" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">哪些脚底点该锁地</span></li><li id="u6937232e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">以及 ankle 这一帧应该去哪里、朝哪儿转</span></li></ul><p id="u64943f98" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但还没解决一个现实问题：</span></p><p id="ud2c05262" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">如果 ankle 目标离 hips 太远，腿即使完全伸直也够不到，怎么办？</span></strong></p><p id="u1e1cee77" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文对此的回答很明确：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0f9732ff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先别急着掰腿</span></li><li id="ucd8f9ef5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先尽量用一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">最小 root 位移</span></strong></li><li id="u48adf252" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">把 root 挪到“腿能触达 ankle 目标”的区域里</span></li><li id="u2aaab7a9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这样后面的 IK 会稳定得多，也不容易爆膝盖。</span></li></ul><p id="u5bf2b921" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这一步的核心目的只有一句：</span></p><p id="ue57a3048" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">先做一个“宏观位移纠正”——挪 hips / root，让后面的局部腿 IK 更容易成功。</span></strong></p><hr id="adR0l" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="6d511042"><span class="ne-text">二、总体输入和输出是什么</span></h1><h2 id="N5jPW"><span class="ne-text">输入</span></h2><p id="uf7708bc7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步主要吃 4 类输入。</span></p><h3 id="f4972686"><span class="ne-text">1）前一步的 ankle 目标</span></h3><p id="uadc9957c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua40769b8" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u8e0fbba2" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="ubfbd0d79" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在你这段 root 代码里，真正直接用到的是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">因为 root 这里只关心“脚踝目标位置能不能够到”，暂时不关心脚踝朝向。论文也是先把 root 位置问题表述成 ankle </span><strong><span class="ne-text">position reachability</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 的问题。</span></p><hr id="GwJRZ" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="39ff558b"><span class="ne-text">2）当前动画里的 hip / ankle 原始位置</span></h3><p id="u7a041ef9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">代码里用的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub76a8f7d" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :]</span></code></li><li id="u1af90b39" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :]</span></code></li><li id="u8deeeb6a" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :]</span></code></li><li id="uff17c4f5" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :]</span></code></li></ul><p id="u65da1e3e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里在 notebook 的骨架语义里：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3516ecb8" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftUpLeg / RightUpLeg</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 代表左右髋关节位置</span></li><li id="ub85bc5b7" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftFoot / RightFoot</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 实际上就是脚踝关节<br /></span><span class="ne-text">因为 heel / ball 是后来注入到 foot 下面的子骨点，所以 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Foot</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 本身就是 ankle 这一层。</span></li></ul><hr id="L8pKr" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="2a374821"><span class="ne-text">3）腿的最大长度 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">leg_length</span></code></h3><p id="u8ee1c8b7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是“从 hip 到 ankle 最多能伸多长”。</span></p><p id="uf04fb31f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文里这叫 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">lmax</span></code><span class="ne-text">，并明确说：若目标 ankle 距 root 的距离 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ltarget</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 大于 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">lmax</span></code><span class="ne-text">，就要把 root 投影回可达球体里。</span></p><hr id="a0Khf" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="8ecad453"><span class="ne-text">4）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code></h3><p id="u1e576d69" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">代码里用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">np.any(contacts[frame, :])</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 作为一个门控：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ufe81fe20" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这一帧如果根本没有脚接触约束</span></li><li id="uce03f2d1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">就没有必要计算 root 修正</span></li></ul><p id="u86dca82c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这和论文语义也一致：root placement 是为满足当前存在的 ankle constraints 服务的。</span></p><hr id="BZ4sd" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="tpRwE"><span class="ne-text">输出</span></h2><p id="u62a841f3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">输出是两个 buffer，但真正阶段输出是这个：</span></p><h3 id="FKCND"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer[frame, :]</span></code></h3><p id="ub78d162f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它表示：</span></p><p id="ub472e7bb" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">这一帧 root / hips 相对于原动画，需要额外平移多少。</span></strong></p><p id="u5519e67b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在你最后的 render 里能看出来：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="yaZMg" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :] + root_buffer[frame, :]</code></pre><p id="ua0ee63a9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这说明 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 存的不是绝对世界位置，而是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">相对原 hips 的偏移量</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="uf4b30e9c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">另外还有一个中间 buffer：</span></p><h3 id="-1"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_temp_root_buffer</span></code></h3><p id="uff4eb37b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它是每帧</span><strong><span class="ne-text">独立计算出来、尚未平滑</span></strong><span class="ne-text">的 root offset。</span></p><p id="u517ee42e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文里也明确说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udf08cbd4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先为每一帧各自算一个 root translation</span></li><li id="ucfb8cea1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再在一个邻域里做平均</span></li><li id="u7d091c32" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">因为 root 直接影响全身，哪怕很小的跳变也会非常显眼，所以必须再平滑。</span></li></ul><hr id="S2GCx" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="0b36ff67"><span class="ne-text">三、先给你一个“代码与论文的对应关系”总览</span></h1><p id="u5050a832" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你贴的这段代码，和论文第三阶段的关系是这样的：</span></p><h2 id="823a24e9"><span class="ne-text">论文原意</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9b1435f9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">单脚约束：把 root 投影到一个球面/球体内</span></li><li id="ud27c6bf6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">双脚约束：投影到两个球体的交集</span></li><li id="uf388b99a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">为防止 root popping，再在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 邻域里做平均</span></li><li id="u3c30fd97" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">平均后即便略微出界，也没关系，下一步可通过轻微 stretch 补上。</span></li></ul><h2 id="2bad034b"><span class="ne-text">Jerome notebook 的工程化改写</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub7dc0efc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">单脚约束：直接算一个“往脚方向拉多少”的 offset</span></li><li id="u8f2abd06" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">双脚约束：不用论文里“严格的球面交 circle 投影”，而是用一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">交替投影迭代</span></strong><span class="ne-text">近似</span></li><li id="u7ca5b1c6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">平滑：不用论文里“对当前 constrained block 做 weighted average”的完整形式，而是用一个简单的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">固定窗口 box average</span></strong></li><li id="uefa69ec3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后把结果存在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 里，用 render 显示出来。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里对这版实现的口头总结就是：比较两条腿的距离是否超限，算 offset，然后把一串帧上的 root correction 做平均。</span></li></ul><p id="ue213ddc5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这点你一定要分清：</span></p><p id="ub028c03c" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">notebook 是忠于论文思想的教学实现，但不是逐公式逐操作的完全等价实现。</span></strong></p><hr id="BuTBO" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="c18696ff"><span class="ne-text">四、Part 1：初始化参数和 buffer</span></h1><p id="u319b5e40" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先看这三行：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="JvIx3" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>L3 = 8
root_buffer = np.zeros([frame_count, 3], dtype=np.float32)
leg_length = animation.pos[0,character.bone_index('LeftLeg'),0] + animation.pos[0,character.bone_index('LeftFoot'),0]</code></pre><hr id="dpMCH" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Dsvcu"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="uDHYx" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>L3 = 8</code></pre><h3 id="fqzxG"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="ua99a810a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">定义第三阶段的邻域窗口长度 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L3</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="a8378a1c"><span class="ne-text">它对应论文哪部分</span></h3><p id="ufa824057" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文 Figure 2 和 Section 4.3 都明确说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2c49944e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第三阶段 root placement 需要依赖前后 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧范围内的信息</span></li><li id="uc17c636d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">用邻域平滑来减少 root popping。</span></li></ul><h3 id="cee261b8"><span class="ne-text">直观解释</span></h3><p id="ub8d65f34" class="ne-p"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L3 = 8</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的意思就是：</span></p><p id="ufe44e161" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步不是只看当前帧，而是后面会在当前帧前后大约 8 帧范围内做 root correction 的时间平滑。</span></p><hr id="kEkKL" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="PXZFF"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="egJgT" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>root_buffer = np.zeros([frame_count, 3], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><h3 id="wEkgQ"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="ua8969483" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">创建最终 root 偏移缓冲区。</span></p><h3 id="687d71dc"><span class="ne-text">shape 是什么含义</span></h3><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2f81afbe" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame_count</span></code><span class="ne-text">：整段动画每一帧</span></li><li id="u589b2ea1" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">3</span></code><span class="ne-text">：XYZ 三个方向的平移偏移</span></li></ul><h3 id="vimH2"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ud9bd361f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面每一帧算完、平滑完的 root offset，都会写进这里。</span></p><hr id="Pa03T" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b639dbb2"><span class="ne-text">第 3 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="fwctj" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>leg_length = animation.pos[0,character.bone_index('LeftLeg'),0] + animation.pos[0,character.bone_index('LeftFoot'),0]</code></pre><h3 id="LggOw"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="ua05f831c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">用左腿的两段骨长近似求出“整条腿最大可达长度”。</span></p><h3 id="430de82c"><span class="ne-text">为什么这么写</span></h3><p id="u86f7173c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在这个 notebook 的骨架里：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uadd16bf1" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftLeg</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的局部 offset 对应大腿或小腿其中一段长度</span></li><li id="u4fc73edc" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftFoot</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的局部 offset 对应另一段长度</span></li><li id="uebdcd336" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而且这个数据的腿链主要沿局部 x 轴展开</span></li></ul><p id="u553ad92d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以直接取它们的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">x</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 分量相加，就得到一个可用的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">leg_length</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="a8378a1c-1"><span class="ne-text">它对应论文哪部分</span></h3><p id="ud843c1c3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文里这一量叫 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">lmax</span></code><span class="ne-text">，即“maximum leg length”，并用它和当前目标距离比较，判断 root 是否需要被拉近。</span></p><h3 id="d63fda0b"><span class="ne-text">这里的一个实现假设</span></h3><p id="uaded71d5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这行代码有一个很强的 notebook 特定假设：</span></p><p id="uf25b238e" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">腿长信息恰好编码在这些局部偏移的 x 分量里。</span></strong></p><p id="u6e2ff723" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果换骨架、换坐标约定，这样写未必稳。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">更通用的写法通常会用两个 offset 向量的范数再相加。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">但在这个 notebook 的具体 skeleton 上，这样写是成立的。</span></p><hr id="CaZBE" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="ddaacf46"><span class="ne-text">五、Part 2：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">compute_root_offset</span></code><span class="ne-text"> —— 这一步的核心几何函数</span></h1><p id="u23ab23c9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">现在看主函数：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="UV9pu" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def compute_root_offset(left_hips, right_hips, left_ankle, right_ankle, leg_length):</code></pre><p id="u77050066" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这整个函数的任务，可以浓缩成一句话：</span></p><p id="ub26dd5a5" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">给定当前左右 hip 位置、左右目标 ankle 位置，以及最大腿长，算出这一帧 root 至少该往哪里平移，才能让腿尽量够得到。</span></strong></p><hr id="eGRIK" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="55b26104"><span class="ne-text">2.1 内部 helper：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_project(root, ankle)</span></code></h2><pre data-language="python" id="xpnDY" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def _project(root, ankle):
    vector = ankle - root
    dist = np.sqrt(np.sum(vector*vector))
    if dist &gt; leg_length:
        return True, (vector/dist)*(dist-leg_length)
    else:
        return False, np.zeros([3], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><p id="u7162c7f0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这几行是整个 root placement 的最小几何原子。</span></p><hr id="cBTus" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="sbTgr"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="DSI74" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>vector = ankle - root</code></pre><h3 id="XXbE0"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u33e6eb96" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">从当前 hip/root 指向目标 ankle 的向量。</span></p><h3 id="a4c9570d"><span class="ne-text">直观上在问什么</span></h3><p id="u5594efae" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“这条腿当前需要伸向哪个方向？”</span></p><hr id="rnTJH" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="dbe2723d-1"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="aKO1E" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>dist = np.sqrt(np.sum(vector*vector))</code></pre><h3 id="BcwzD"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u7fe9d7b1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">求当前 hip 到目标 ankle 的直线距离。</span></p><h3 id="17d2b479"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="uf075fcdb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正对应论文里的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ltarget</span></code><span class="ne-text">，也就是“target ankle position 离 root 有多远”。</span></p><hr id="qvP2F" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="dfc2eed4"><span class="ne-text">第 3～6 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="UqaiT" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if dist &gt; leg_length:
    return True, (vector/dist)*(dist-leg_length)
else:
    return False, np.zeros([3], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><h3 id="6dc7e054"><span class="ne-text">这段在干什么</span></h3><p id="u27156c8c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果目标 ankle 距离已经超过了最大腿长：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud07342bb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那就说明</span><strong><span class="ne-text">这条腿够不到</span></strong></li><li id="u7781fe7e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">于是返回 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">True</span></code></li><li id="uf2460bac" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">并给出一个 offset，大小恰好是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">dist - leg_length</span></code></li><li id="u843a8a5f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">方向则是朝向 ankle 的单位向量</span></li></ul><p id="u3dcb2fd7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><p id="ua1ab35c5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“root 至少还要朝 ankle 方向移动这么多，腿才刚好够到。”</span></p><h3 id="4b893b02"><span class="ne-text">为什么这个 offset 公式是对的</span></h3><p id="u4a8aa0b3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ueee8cdaa" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前距离是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">dist</span></code></li><li id="u6e84eb51" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">最大允许距离是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">leg_length</span></code></li><li id="u9156e405" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">还差 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">dist - leg_length</span></code></li><li id="ub6e6c50f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而最小改动方案显然就是沿当前连线方向拉近</span></li></ul><h3 id="17d2b479-1"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="ubff378be" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文说的是：</span></p><p id="ud824bb6c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">若 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ltarget &gt; lmax</span></code><span class="ne-text">，就把 root 投影回“以 ankle target 为球心、半径等于最大腿长”的可达球体上。</span></p><p id="u12f33db1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 这个 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_project</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的写法，实际上就是在直接返回“为了完成这次投影，root 需要增加的最小位移”。</span></p><hr id="z4TUx" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="05124532"><span class="ne-text">2.2 分别检查左右腿</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="SVHWv" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>left, left_offset = _project(left_hips, left_ankle)
right, right_offset = _project(right_hips, right_ankle)</code></pre><h3 id="hkylH"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ufd568d2e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">分别判断：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf27a3599" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左腿是否够得到左 ankle target</span></li><li id="u4e3aa407" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右腿是否够得到右 ankle target</span></li></ul><p id="u5935f3a5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">并各自算出如果不够，需要多少 root offset。</span></p><h3 id="d1767486"><span class="ne-text">为什么要分左右</span></h3><p id="u0b4e10c9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为可能出现 3 种典型情况：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u93e4ded9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只有左腿不够</span></li><li id="u90f36107" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只有右腿不够</span></li><li id="uda39e6da" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">两条腿都不够</span></li></ol><p id="u54947954" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面代码就是按这三种分支处理。</span></p><hr id="SlyQN" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b6deba87"><span class="ne-text">2.3 只有一条腿超限的情况</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="C1ayi" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if left and not right:
    return left_offset

if right and not left:
    return right_offset</code></pre><h3 id="6dc7e054-1"><span class="ne-text">这段在干什么</span></h3><p id="uc631703d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果只有一条腿目标超出可达范围：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3dc28073" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那就只按那条腿给出的 offset 去挪 root</span></li></ul><h3 id="d892dfe2"><span class="ne-text">对应 Jerome 语音稿</span></h3><p id="u11475ec6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的原话是：</span></p><p id="u1bc36a5a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果只有一只脚被约束，而且太远了，你只能把 root 朝着你正在拉的那只脚的方向移动。</span></p><h3 id="17d2b479-2"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="ud23ce246" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文里单脚情况就是：把 root 投影到单个球体上。</span></p><p id="u169cc995" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 这里的实现方式，和论文从几何上是同一件事，只是表达为“直接返回最小 offset”。</span></p><hr id="D1dXS" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="f8a01d6f"><span class="ne-text">2.4 两条腿都超限的情况：交替投影近似双球交</span></h2><p id="ubf058e22" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是函数最有意思的地方。</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="efvVR" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if left and right:
    current_offset = left_offset
    for i in range(5):
        loop, offset = _project(right_hips + current_offset, right_ankle)
        if not loop:
            return current_offset
        current_offset += offset
        loop, offset = _project(left_hips + current_offset, left_ankle)
        if not loop:
            return current_offset
        current_offset += offset
    return current_offset</code></pre><hr id="pxPTG" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="ff235d20"><span class="ne-text">先看大意</span></h3><p id="u1f4d430f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段代码在做：</span></p><p id="ub6ac1f9a" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">当左右两条腿都够不到时，用一个小迭代，在“满足左腿可达”和“满足右腿可达”之间来回投影，逼近一个两腿都尽量可达的位置。</span></strong></p><hr id="TzQ3B" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="b93bcef1-2"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="EECQK" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>current_offset = left_offset</code></pre><h3 id="byrpw"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ud3396d75" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先用左腿给出的 offset 作为初值。</span></p><p id="uf509a6cd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是先满足左腿，再看右腿还差多少。</span></p><hr id="V1AQS" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="dbe2723d-2"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="HKgPZ" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for i in range(5):</code></pre><h3 id="dGjwv"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u90e52468" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">最多迭代 5 次。</span></p><h3 id="c4ae10d2"><span class="ne-text">为什么是迭代</span></h3><p id="u22731735" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为“双腿都超限”时，root 理论上应该投影到</span><strong><span class="ne-text">两个可达球体的交集</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u30d8b912" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文给的是更严格的几何表述：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u50b58c80" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先分别投影到两个球体</span></li><li id="u2ee9c6a5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">若某个投影已经在双球 3D 交里，就可以接受</span></li><li id="u994213d6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">否则应投影到两个球面交出的那条圆上。</span></li></ul><p id="uee70494c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome notebook 没有去显式求那个交圈，而是用了一个更适合教学实现的近似：</span></p><p id="u59dca9a3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">交替投影（alternating projection）</span></p><p id="ub81f9b2b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u165dfae5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先修左腿</span></li><li id="u2527a8c1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再修右腿</span></li><li id="u73ded793" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再修左腿</span></li><li id="u12f1180c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再修右腿</span></li><li id="u6ca3ba9a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">几次后得到一个足够好的折中 offset</span></li></ul><p id="ufb9b5b2f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是 notebook 和论文在实现层面的一个明显差异。</span></p><hr id="sqNbI" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="7452508a"><span class="ne-text">右腿投影</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="jRQao" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>loop, offset = _project(right_hips + current_offset, right_ankle)
if not loop:
    return current_offset
current_offset += offset</code></pre><h3 id="d60678fe"><span class="ne-text">这三行在干什么</span></h3><p id="u040a199b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">意思是：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u71790fef" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">假设我们先应用了当前 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">current_offset</span></code></li><li id="u7d73c0f3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">看看此时右腿还够不够</span></li><li id="u23707dd4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果右腿已经够到了，就当前结果直接返回</span></li><li id="u02b031bf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">否则，把为了让右腿够到所需的额外 offset 再加进去</span></li></ol><hr id="G8PKM" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="d3d742e1"><span class="ne-text">左腿再投影</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="TW9uz" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>loop, offset = _project(left_hips + current_offset, left_ankle)
if not loop:
    return current_offset
current_offset += offset</code></pre><h3 id="fvlgH"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u3a307bf5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">刚刚为了满足右腿又动了一下 root，这可能重新把左腿搞超限。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以再回头检查左腿。</span></p><p id="uc88a6deb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正是一个经典的“在两个约束之间反复折中”的过程。</span></p><hr id="OofM0" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="06209572"><span class="ne-text">结束返回</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="Qm4Ob" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>return current_offset</code></pre><h3 id="DOlij"><span class="ne-text">含义</span></h3><p id="ub966243f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果 5 次迭代都没完全让两条腿同时进入可达域，就返回当前最好的近似解。</span></p><h3 id="8c6c5342"><span class="ne-text">和论文关系</span></h3><p id="uc2c693e1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文更“严格几何”，notebook 更“实用近似”。</span></p><p id="uc91528ca" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把 notebook 这里理解成：</span></p><p id="uf1b32574" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">“我不显式求双球交 circle，但我用来回修正的方法逼近那个区域。”</span></strong></p><hr id="RjDly" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="6c896782"><span class="ne-text">2.5 没有任何腿超限</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="dvpCe" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>return np.zeros([3], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><h3 id="DfxVY"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ucbd230bd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果左右腿都够得到当前 ankle targets，就不需要动 root。</span></p><h3 id="651f46eb"><span class="ne-text">非常合理</span></h3><p id="ub5df7cac" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 root
