placement 的目标是：</span></p><p id="u13f85742" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">只做最小必要改动。</span></strong></p><p id="u7d524c23" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">既然当前已经可达，就不应该额外改 hips。</span></p><hr id="CG9PP" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="3f9b02a5"><span class="ne-text">六、Part 3：逐帧计算未平滑的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_temp_root_buffer</span></code></h1><p id="ua13fe1c6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">下面这段：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="jfmfP" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>_temp_root_buffer = np.zeros([frame_count, 3], dtype=np.float32)
for frame in range(frame_count):
    if np.any(contacts[frame, :]):
        _temp_root_buffer[frame, :] = compute_root_offset(
            agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :],
            agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :],
            agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :]+ ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :],
            agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :]+ ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :],
            leg_length
        )</code></pre><p id="ub408e88f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段代码是在做：</span></p><p id="u289f0718" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">先不考虑时间平滑，单独对每一帧求一个“这一帧如果只顾可达性，root 至少该挪多少”的原始答案。</span></strong></p><hr id="pXdCi" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b93bcef1-3"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="lYdR6" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>_temp_root_buffer = np.zeros([frame_count, 3], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><h3 id="wCgv9"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u3ba4077c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">创建一个中间结果 buffer，专门存“每帧独立求出的 raw root offset”。</span></p><h3 id="8da194b8"><span class="ne-text">为什么不直接写进 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></h3><p id="u11c10de6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为接下来还要做时间平滑。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这和论文描述是完全一致的：先独立算，再平均。</span></p><hr id="X4SVU" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="dbe2723d-3"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="bCMTR" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for frame in range(frame_count):</code></pre><h3 id="yaQK2"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ud34c8872" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">逐帧处理整段动画。</span></p><hr id="kqehX" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b639dbb2-1"><span class="ne-text">第 3 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="pQAaR" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if np.any(contacts[frame, :]):</code></pre><h3 id="jYZiy"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uf826e516" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">只有当这一帧至少存在一个脚部接触约束时，才需要计算 root 修正。</span></p><h3 id="01bf1a73"><span class="ne-text">为什么这样合理</span></h3><p id="uf01be947" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果这一帧根本没有脚底锁地要求，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">那 root placement 这一阶段就没有“必须让某个 ankle target 可达”的任务。</span></p><hr id="cFir6" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="e9b9942b"><span class="ne-text">调用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">compute_root_offset(...)</span></code></h2><pre data-language="python" id="txD2C" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>_temp_root_buffer[frame, :] = compute_root_offset(
    agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :],
    agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :],
    agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :],
    agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :],
    leg_length
)</code></pre><p id="u8bc027f6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这几行是整段 root stage 的“输入拼装”。</span></p><hr id="JtnSx" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="449485c5"><span class="ne-text">左右 hips</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="F3ZLf" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :]
agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :]</code></pre><h3 id="Qqh23"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u17c95e70" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">取当前帧左右髋关节的全局位置。</span></p><h3 id="c44e7a18"><span class="ne-text">为什么不是用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Hips</span></code></h3><p id="u78ad983b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 root 实际要保证的是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">左右各自的腿链</span></strong><span class="ne-text">可达，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以最自然的判断点是每条腿真正的起点——左右髋。</span></p><hr id="YhTXP" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="77e9e54a"><span class="ne-text">左右 ankle targets</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="MrCat" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :]
agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :]</code></pre><h3 id="9dcfba81"><span class="ne-text">这两行非常关键</span></h3><p id="uc2b60b6e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它们不是当前原始 ankle 位置，而是：</span></p><p id="u58c616c8" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">原始 ankle 位置 + 上一步算好的 ankle 平移 offset</span></strong></p><p id="ua920fd05" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这一步实际在问的是：</span></p><p id="uc1ff34f7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“上一步我希望 ankle 去的位置，现在对这条腿来说够得到吗？”</span></p><p id="u0fc04cab" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就把第二阶段和第三阶段准确接上了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u585b2fe0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第二阶段给出 ankle target</span></li><li id="u0d684112" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第三阶段根据 ankle target 反推 root 该怎么动</span></li></ul><h3 id="17d2b479-3"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="u2f3c917c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文 4.3 开头就说：</span></p><p id="u04ea31cd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">到这一步，我们已经知道 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi-L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi+L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 范围内 constrained ankles 的目标全局位置和朝向了；下一步要调整腿去满足这些目标，但在这之前先尝试移动 root，使目标 ankle positions 变得可达。</span></p><hr id="OGVpI" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="af3a413c"><span class="ne-text">七、Part 4：对 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_temp_root_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 做时间平均，得到最终 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></h1><p id="u1d665447" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">下面是第二个 for：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="P5KF0" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for frame in range(frame_count):
    if np.any(contacts[frame, :]):
        count = 0
        value = np.zeros([3], dtype=np.float32)
        for f in range(-L3+1,L3):
            if frame+f &gt;= 0 and frame+f &lt; frame_count:
                value += _temp_root_buffer[frame+f, :]
                count +=1
        root_buffer[frame, :] = value/count</code></pre><p id="uf0d79989" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段就是论文里“root popping 的简化平滑解法”。</span></p><hr id="wDApI" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="a0d2a0ad"><span class="ne-text">为什么还要平滑</span></h2><p id="ub307db1c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文非常强调：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u1295d4a7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">root 的单帧投影可能在“单脚约束”和“双脚约束”切换时发生很大变化</span></li><li id="u69db5766" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">即便 root 原本很平稳，投影结果也可能突然跳</span></li><li id="u6568afe9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">root 一跳，全身都会跳，所以这种 popping 非常显眼。</span></li></ul><p id="u2c347847" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 也在语音稿里直接说：</span></p><p id="udfb3df82" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">为了避免 root 只在某一帧突然被拉一下，他们会把 root correction 在一串帧上做平均。</span></p><hr id="eKGVn" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b93bcef1-4"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="ouYBF" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for frame in range(frame_count):</code></pre><h3 id="oFJL3"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ucd2cab0b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">再次逐帧遍历，但这次不是“独立求值”，而是“对邻域求平均”。</span></p><hr id="vv79E" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="dbe2723d-4"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="wqYp8" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if np.any(contacts[frame, :]):</code></pre><h3 id="PiIY9"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u4b4b009f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">只对有约束的帧输出平滑后的 root 修正。</span></p><hr id="hHHwY" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="08c9530d"><span class="ne-text">初始化累加器</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="tDpve" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>count = 0
value = np.zeros([3], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><h3 id="M1rFO"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ub441fefb" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">准备对邻域内的 raw root offsets 求均值。</span></p><hr id="BGr67" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="f37348d8"><span class="ne-text">邻域循环</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="OLJug" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(-L3+1,L3):</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-15"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u7c4a8da7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">遍历当前帧前后大约 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 长度的窗口。</span></p><p id="u2ed8cee7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">当 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L3 = 8</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 时，这个范围是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf3037b3a" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-7, -6, ..., 0, ..., 6, 7</span></code></li></ul><p id="u31471a95" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是总共 15 帧左右的邻域。</span></p><h3 id="17d2b479-4"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="uaccd3ebd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文说 root translations 是在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi-L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Fi+L3</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的邻域里先独立计算，再做 average。</span></p><hr id="zNB9X" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="8f7a40b2"><span class="ne-text">边界判断</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="zWkDH" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if frame+f &gt;= 0 and frame+f &lt; frame_count:</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-16"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u7da59b97" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">避免访问越界帧。</span></p><hr id="ZOizC" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="cc4a801e"><span class="ne-text">累加邻域结果</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="McA3A" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>value += _temp_root_buffer[frame+f, :]
count +=1</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-17"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u52a568f5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把邻域里的 raw root offset 全部加起来，准备求平均。</span></p><h3 id="138a6766"><span class="ne-text">注意</span></h3><p id="u02f1eb12" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">简单平均</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，即 box filter。</span></p><p id="uc4cd6bf7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而论文原文写得更精细一些：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf88ce32c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先对邻域内各帧独立算 root translations</span></li><li id="ud1753385" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再对包含当前帧的 constrained block 做 weighted average。</span></li></ul><p id="u34fac977" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 这一版 notebook把它简化成：</span></p><p id="u36fb2def" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“固定窗口里直接平均。”</span></p><p id="ud83f65da" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这也是 notebook 相比论文的又一个实现简化。</span></p><hr id="xo2fx" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="ded40649"><span class="ne-text">写入最终结果</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="SmeV0" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>root_buffer[frame, :] = value/count</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-18"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u3e4f0259" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把平滑后的 root offset 写入最终 buffer。</span></p><h3 id="571ea983"><span class="ne-text">这意味着什么</span></h3><p id="u3d725c8a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">现在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 里的值，不再是“单帧最精确的可达解”，而是：</span></p><p id="u8dd8c9aa" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">在时间上更平滑、更适合整段动画观感的折中解。</span></strong></p><hr id="dYujV" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="bbd71d3a"><span class="ne-text">一个非常重要的后果</span></h2><p id="u5cd8a2b6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这会带来论文和 Jerome 都明确承认的副作用：</span></p><p id="u0f62c3b3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">平均之后，root 不一定还严格处于所有球体可达域里。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">也就是说，腿有可能还是差一点点够不到。</span></p><p id="u52eb8323" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但这不是问题，因为：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u606d4e49" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">论文下一步会允许轻微 stretch 来精确满足 ankle targets</span></li><li id="u6e392b82" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也直说：平均后不能保证一定完全够到，但没关系，下一步 IK/leg stretch 会修掉。</span></li></ul><p id="ub37a0bd8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是第三阶段和第四阶段之间最关键的接口关系。</span></p><hr id="d3Bjr" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="1a2fd4f0"><span class="ne-text">八、Part 5：render 这一整块其实是“可视化 cell”，不是 root 计算本体</span></h1><p id="u2d10fc92" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你贴的代码后半段：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="I3ooj" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def render(frame):
    ...
interact(...)
viewer</code></pre><p id="ube9bc536" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">从 notebook 结构上看，应该视为</span><strong><span class="ne-text">下一格独立的显示 cell</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">你贴出来时缩进把它和上面的 for 混在一起了，但就 notebook 本意来说，它不是 root 计算循环内部的一部分，而是单独的“显示当前 root / ankle / constraint target”工具。</span></p><p id="uac9ed0ff" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一点我先说清楚，避免你以后照抄时误以为 render 定义真在 for 里面。</span></p><hr id="fLkdW" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="6740fe04"><span class="ne-text">8.1 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">render(frame)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 开头：先画原始 animation</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="nJNqZ" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>anim = animation
p = (anim.pos[frame,...])
q = (anim.quats[frame,...])

a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(q, p)
viewer.set_shadow_poi(p[0])

viewer.begin_shadow()
viewer.draw(character, a)
viewer.end_shadow()

viewer.begin_display()
viewer.draw_ground()
viewer.draw(character, a)</code></pre><h3 id="6dc7e054-2"><span class="ne-text">这段在干什么</span></h3><p id="ub7637dc8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先把当前帧原始角色画出来。</span></p><h3 id="d94c6193"><span class="ne-text">为什么先画原始角色</span></h3><p id="u0d1919e7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 root / ankle 的目标轴线等都是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">叠加调试信息</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">先画 base pose，后面才能看清：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u60038c59" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原角色当前在哪</span></li><li id="u686d7a02" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">约束点在哪</span></li><li id="u881b7d23" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ankle target 在哪</span></li><li id="u4808a0d6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">hips 经过 root_buffer 修正后又该在哪</span></li></ul><hr id="kSoSo" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="63208b91"><span class="ne-text">8.2 画上一节的 constraint targets</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="lPaFi" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts = np.eye(4, dtype=np.float32)[np.newaxis,...].repeat(4, axis=0)
contacts[:, :3, 3] = constraint_positions_buffer[frame, :, :]

viewer.draw(target, contacts)
viewer.end_display()</code></pre><h3 id="6dc7e054-3"><span class="ne-text">这段在干什么</span></h3><p id="u49042178" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把四个小 target 放到当前帧四个 constraint position 上。</span></p><h3 id="3ad82898"><span class="ne-text">意义</span></h3><p id="ub3dfb502" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以直观看到：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ufb773402" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这一步 root placement 到底是为了让 hips 去服务哪几个脚底目标点</span></li></ul><p id="uf29764c0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这相当于是把第一阶段的输出也同时显示出来。</span></p><hr id="FUfBn" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="830a67a4"><span class="ne-text">8.3 再画原骨架线</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="v5pQx" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.disable(depth_test=True)
viewer.draw_lines(character.world_skeleton_lines(a))</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-19"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u6be1ac1e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">显示当前角色骨架线条，便于对照 hips、ankle 和 target 的空间关系。</span></p><hr id="Apnho" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="026bf87a"><span class="ne-text">8.4 画左脚 ankle target 轴</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="uWEgG" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 0, :], agquats[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :])
gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :]
a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)</code></pre><h3 id="6dc7e054-4"><span class="ne-text">这段在干什么</span></h3><p id="uee882b4d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">画出左脚在上一阶段算出来的 ankle 目标位姿。</span></p><h3 id="773f64c2"><span class="ne-text">为什么 root stage 要把它画出来</span></h3><p id="u9d936a81" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 root stage 的任务正是：</span></p><p id="u03d4bdbc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">“让 hips 尽量移到能让这个 ankle target 够得着的位置。”</span></p><p id="ud7ac9387" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以你要同时看见 ankle target 才能判断 root 是否合理。</span></p><hr id="ur7Gj" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="3d0e22a0"><span class="ne-text">8.5 画右脚 ankle target 轴</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="IhO3j" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 1, :], agquats[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :])
gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :]
a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-20"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u0104da2d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">和左脚完全一样，只是换成右脚。</span></p><hr id="AbhCP" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="b7fd1eb9"><span class="ne-text">8.6 画 root / hips 的目标轴</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="PQhz9" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = agquats[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :]
gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :] + root_buffer[frame, :]
a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)</code></pre><h3 id="01aa570f"><span class="ne-text">这段是 root stage 可视化的核心</span></h3><p id="ucb46f448" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它画的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8e635749" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始 hips 的朝向不变</span></li><li id="u4e2a01f6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但位置加上 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer[frame, :]</span></code></li></ul><p id="u9175a50c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，显示“</span><strong><span class="ne-text">这一帧我希望 hips 被平移到哪里</span></strong><span class="ne-text">”。</span></p><h3 id="dad8dda1"><span class="ne-text">为什么只改位置不改朝向</span></h3><p id="u433510cf" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这一阶段论文和 notebook 都是在做 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">root translation</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，不是 root rotation。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">论文明确说 root placement 是“move the root by the smallest amount”，并且 footskate cleanup 里 root 修改的是位置，不是朝向。</span></p><hr id="TDteN" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="0cac1db3"><span class="ne-text">8.7 提交渲染</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="vD9Mh" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.execute_commands()</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-21"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u51dc6027" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把所有可视化内容提交给 viewer。</span></p><hr id="Abelj" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="72cbfbb6"><span class="ne-text">8.8 交互滑条</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="MzreS" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>interact(
    render, 
    frame=lab.Timeline(max=frame_count-1)
)
viewer</code></pre><h3 id="69fa3701-22"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u9fcdb55c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">让你可以拖 frame，看整段动画里：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u50844122" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">constraint targets</span></li><li id="u194de2a0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左右 ankle target axes</span></li><li id="u5d66644f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">root target axis</span></li></ul><p id="uefdc1d97" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">是怎么随时间变化的。</span></p><p id="u251429f7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也说了，他会把 root position buffer 显示出来，看“我希望 hips 在哪”。</span></p><hr id="G12cA" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="b154c217"><span class="ne-text">九、把这一整步浓缩成一句话</span></h1><p id="u0a963a82" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Root Position</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 理解成：</span></p><p id="u749806da" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">前一步已经决定了脚踝应该去哪里，这一步先不解腿，而是先问：现在 hips 放在哪，腿才有机会够得到那些脚踝目标？</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果只有一条腿超出可达范围，就沿那条腿的方向最小量移动 root；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果两条腿都超出，就在两个腿的可达球体约束之间做折中；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">然后为了避免 root popping，再把这些逐帧修正做时间平均；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">即便平均后略微不够，也没关系，下一步用腿部 IK 和轻微 stretch 再补。</span></p><hr id="DpjXb" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="50bc53c5"><span class="ne-text">十、你学习这一步时最应该盯住的几个关键词</span></h1><h2 id="dfb616cb"><span class="ne-text">1）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">leg_length</span></code></h2><p id="u714c9c38" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">最大腿长，也就是每条腿的可达半径。</span></p><h2 id="5c9efbf4"><span class="ne-text">2）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_project</span></code></h2><p id="uad16ed91" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">单腿可达性判断：不够就返回朝 ankle 的最小 root offset。</span></p><h2 id="0d959d30"><span class="ne-text">3）单腿 vs 双腿</span></h2><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0ddc6c7e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">单腿：单球约束</span></li><li id="ua0bcef66" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">双腿：双球交约束</span></li></ul><h2 id="8ecad453-1"><span class="ne-text">4）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_temp_root_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="u4ca84332" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">每帧独立解，强调“可达性”。</span></p><h2 id="acfa76a8"><span class="ne-text">5）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></h2><p id="u3fc2c9b9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">邻域平均后的平滑解，强调“观感连续性”。</span></p><h2 id="d92831ed"><span class="ne-text">6）平均后不一定严格可达</span></h2><p id="u2641541b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但残差由下一步 IK / stretch 补上。</span></p><hr id="Vp9DH" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="b5018d83"><span class="ne-text">十一、和你未来 UE / GASPALS / Shadow Mode 的关系</span></h1><p id="u20d39129" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">从你的长期目标看，这一步特别像一个很适合独立模块化的“中间层”：</span></p><h2 id="27a31801"><span class="ne-text">它属于哪一层</span></h2><p id="u1af4a8e0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">在 GASPALS / UE 动画链里，它大概位于：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud97c68c6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Motion Matching / Base Locomotion 输出之后</span></li><li id="u0b807ca6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Ankle / Foot target 已知之后</span></li><li id="ube32db81" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Leg IK / FBIK 之前</span></li></ul><h2 id="49afec77"><span class="ne-text">它的本质职责</span></h2><p id="u7532874c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不是生成动作，而是：</span></p><p id="uc0827b5f" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把“局部末端目标”转换成“更容易求解的上游根部位置修正”。</span></strong></p><p id="ua927f9ed" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这跟 UE 里很多现代工作流也很像：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udf9a631c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">宏观轨迹先由根或骨盆调整</span></li><li id="u60647b31" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">微观贴合再交给 IK / FBIK / Post Process</span></li></ul><p id="u26c0239d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这一步非常适合你以后在 Shadow Mode 里单独接管做 A/B：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u83c84bdd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">规则法 root placement</span></li><li id="ua021175d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">学习法 root correction</span></li><li id="u3bd83e39" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">或者结合 Motion Warping / Pose Search 去做更强的 root biasing</span></li></ul><p id="uca8cb589" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但不管实现变不变，这一层的职责都很稳定：</span></p><p id="ua4aacd1b" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">先帮下游 IK 降低难度。</span></strong></p></details>
#### 数据流程图
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/__mermaid_v3/d3cc188b82d7db62e4a0faae9e928f9b.svg)

#### Root位置确定
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2026/png/45532692/1774262228270-84315f74-1a07-47aa-b889-ddd42041f29b.png)

### Compute IK
[https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69c236b6-97fc-83ab-b84c-8cb63b278ef0](https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1-mldeformer/c/69c236b6-97fc-83ab-b84c-8cb63b278ef0)

[https://chatgpt.com/share/69c237e7-2f54-8009-985d-c5e5fdb837d4](https://chatgpt.com/share/69c237e7-2f54-8009-985d-c5e5fdb837d4)

#### 总览
<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="ueb802266"><span class="ne-text">GPT</span></summary><p id="u4eacfb0f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">好，这一步我按你要求的顺序来，而且我会把你贴的整块代码拆回到合适的位置里讲。</span></p><p id="u84c9461a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先给一句总纲：</span></p><p id="ua960de9f" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">Compute IK 这一步，不是在“再算 ankle 目标”，而是在前面三步已经知道 ankle 该去哪、root 该往哪挪之后，真正去改腿链，让腿的姿态落到那个目标上。</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">论文把它定义为第 4 阶段：在第 2 步得到的 target ankle configuration 和第 3 步得到的 root position 已知的前提下，调整 hip、knee、ankle 参数，使目标 ankle 位置和朝向被满足；尽量用关节旋转完成，必要时才允许长度调整来避免 knee pop。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里对这一步的总结也非常直白：先把腿看成一个三角形，先算膝盖应该弯多少；再把整条腿转向目标；再把 ankle 放回目标朝向和位置；如果腿太接近打直，就做 knee damping，避免 knee pop。</span></p><hr id="tTpmY" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="L5kLa"><span class="ne-text">一、这一步的目的是什么</span></h1><p id="u74868a04" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一阶段的目的可以分成两层。</span></p><h2 id="WSaK4"><span class="ne-text">第一层：精确满足 ankle target</span></h2><p id="u295c911e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">前两步已经给了你：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud1ad397a" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u54d66436" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="u6d9e96e9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是“这一帧 ankle 最终该在哪里、朝哪儿转”。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">第三步又给了你：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ue163a252" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="u3d0eac13" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是“hips 先往哪里平移，腿才比较有机会够到”。</span></p><p id="ue65483be" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那么第四步就要真正回答：</span></p><p id="ua3986118" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">现在我怎样改大腿、小腿、脚踝这些局部关节，才能让这条腿真的摆出那个目标姿态？</span></strong></p><p id="u0ef0dfb6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原话就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u340888a3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">使用上一步得到的 root position</span></li><li id="u7450f3d2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">调整 hip、knee、ankle</span></li><li id="u181ebcec" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">满足 target ankle positions and orientations。</span></li></ul><hr id="JXiG7" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="dgXrB"><span class="ne-text">第二层：避免 knee pop</span></h2><p id="uad140bfe" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果腿接近完全打直，那么“再多伸一点点”和“少伸一点点”，在几何上只差 1～2 cm，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">但对膝盖角度来说，可能会导致非常剧烈的跳变。论文把这叫 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">knee pop</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u49a56239" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里说得很形象：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u11c2aef1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">你只是想补 1、2 厘米</span></li><li id="u59a7d680" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但膝盖可能“刷”一下从这里跳到那里</span></li><li id="u37b83e46" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这个 pop 在视觉上非常明显<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以他会用一个多项式去压制接近完全打直时的 knee rotation。</span></li></ul><hr id="Pp48w" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="ZN1P9"><span class="ne-text">二、总体输入和输出是什么</span></h1><h2 id="PzZhs"><span class="ne-text">输入</span></h2><p id="u9c56514c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步真正用到的输入，有 5 组。</span></p><h3 id="lqjEJ"><span class="ne-text">1）原始要修的动画 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code></h3><p id="u31882fbf" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">代码一开始就 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">copy.deepcopy(animation)</span></code><span class="ne-text">，说明它是以原始待修动画为底稿。</span></p><h3 id="Zmol0"><span class="ne-text">2）上一步得到的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></h3><p id="ued559e36" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它先被加到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Hips</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 上，作为“已经修过 root 的新底姿态”。</span></p><h3 id="lTLYD"><span class="ne-text">3）第二步得到的 ankle targets</span></h3><p id="u6134795d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u51c65d58" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u56f58015" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="u44baca73" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这两个 buffer 直接参与左脚和右脚的最终对齐。</span></p><h3 id="kh9Hq"><span class="ne-text">4）原始全局 FK 结果 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agquats / agpos</span></code></h3><p id="u95802cd1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步很多目标都是“相对原动画的 offset”，所以仍然要回看原始 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos/agquats</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">代码里左脚、右脚都在用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[...] + ankle_position_buffer[...]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer, agquats[...])</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="tklZe"><span class="ne-text">5）腿长 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">len1 / len2</span></code></h3><p id="u76c64647" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是把腿当成两段骨链做解析 IK 的基础长度。</span></p><hr id="DYcQG" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Zmh2z"><span class="ne-text">输出</span></h2><p id="u104b619a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">输出只有一个核心对象：</span></p><h3 id="R3wD2"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved_animation</span></code></h3><p id="ud39639fe" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是整条动画在完成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u01667d9c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">root 平移</span></li><li id="u7f9c726b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">膝盖弯曲</span></li><li id="u248fd075" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">hip 指向目标</span></li><li id="u58aad834" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">ankle 精确对齐</span></li></ul><p id="u1a9cdb86" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">之后的最终求解结果。代码最后就是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="dsk4J" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>solved_animation = compute_final_animation()</code></pre><p id="u9a1e8542" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而 render 里也允许你切换：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3bc4e16f" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved=True</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 看求解后</span></li><li id="u9e7f6563" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved=False</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 看原始动画</span></li></ul><p id="u322de45a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这本质上就是 A/B 对照查看 IK 是否把脚真正放回目标。</span></p><hr id="M5FDS" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="yNq9t"><span class="ne-text">三、把这一步拆成几个部分</span></h1><p id="u7e45fd9a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">我建议你把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Compute IK</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 看成 7 段：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="RZene" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>A. 先看 knee damping polynomial 是什么
B. 复制动画，并先把 root 修正加上去
C. 预计算腿长相关常数
D. 解左腿：先算膝角，再转大腿，再强制脚踝贴目标
E. 解右腿：同样流程复制一遍
F. 返回 solved_animation
G. render 只是在可视化“求解前后 + 目标轴”</code></pre><p id="ucc1a2c1e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">下面我就按这 7 段讲。</span></p><hr id="UCU51" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="zNlUN"><span class="ne-text">四、Part A：先看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text"># knee damping polynomial</span></code></h1><p id="u0cf4f0ad" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你贴的第一小段是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="QL12Y" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>#knee damping polynomial

x = np.linspace(-1, 1, 100)
fig, (ax) = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(5,5))
ax.plot(x)
z = -.037037*x**7 + .0314815*x**6 + .0374074*x**5 - .0260185*x**4 - .0114815*x**3 + .00453704*x**2 + 1.00111*x

ax.plot(z)</code></pre><hr id="Ojtl4" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="f0mqW"><span class="ne-text">这段的目的是什么</span></h2><p id="ude6e053e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这不是 IK 本体，而是一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">可视化解释 cell</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><p id="u7a0e52ff" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把“原始 arccos 输入”到“经过 knee damping 多项式修正后的 arccos 输入”画出来，方便你看 Jerome 到底怎么压制 straight-leg 区域。</span></p><p id="u2ac72c1c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里专门说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ueebda6f7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">他会先算 arccos</span></li><li id="u81de1fa5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后把 arccos 输入送进一个 polynomial</span></li><li id="ue800369f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这个 polynomial 几乎是一条直线</span></li><li id="ud848fe21" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但在接近 straight-leg 的末端会刻意压一下</span></li><li id="u5831b79d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">目的是“永远留一点角度”，不要真的打成 180°，从而避免 knee pop。</span></li></ul><hr id="x64DO" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Y0FOL"><span class="ne-text">逐行解释</span></h2><h3 id="FWabV"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="vnQL1" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>x = np.linspace(-1, 1, 100)</code></pre><p id="u403f3b13" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">生成 100 个样本点，覆盖 arccos 的有效输入范围 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">[-1, 1]</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="rCYWc"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="TGPyg" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>fig, (ax) = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(5,5))</code></pre><p id="u0728379e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">创建一个图窗。</span></p><h3 id="SRH9e"><span class="ne-text">第 3 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="fS4A2" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ax.plot(x)</code></pre><p id="u860ce1e4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先把输入的“理想恒等映射”画出来。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这条线可以理解成“如果不做 knee damping，输入是多少就直接是多少”。</span></p><h3 id="gADG2"><span class="ne-text">第 4 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="q2sTQ" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>z = -.037037*x**7 + .0314815*x**6 + .0374074*x**5 - .0260185*x**4 - .0114815*x**3 + .00453704*x**2 + 1.00111*x</code></pre><p id="u15fe1ea0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是 Jerome 手工拟合出来的 damping 多项式。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">它不是论文公式原样抄过来，而是一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">工程近似实现</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。论文给的是分段函数 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">f(x)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和积分形式，用来限制接近完全伸直时的膝盖角度调整；Jerome 则直接把这个效果压成一个可用的多项式映射。</span></p><h3 id="WGayh"><span class="ne-text">第 5 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="sZsT1" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ax.plot(z)</code></pre><p id="u733374f9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把修正后的曲线画出来。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">你会看到绝大部分区域几乎贴着直线，但在接近 straight-leg 的边界开始“收住”。</span></p><hr id="UrDYx" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="Zri4u"><span class="ne-text">五、Part B：函数开头——拷贝动画，并先应用 root 修正</span></h1><p id="u5c9dc5da" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">现在进入真正的主函数。你贴出来的核心实现就是这个 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">compute_final_animation()</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ub9aec4ee" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先看开头：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="d63bz" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>import copy

def compute_final_animation():
    local_anim = copy.deepcopy(animation)
    local_anim.pos[:, character.bone_index('Hips'), :] += root_buffer
    gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)</code></pre><hr id="MBqp4" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Tbu0p"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="kmepy" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim = copy.deepcopy(animation)</code></pre><h3 id="J77XB"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ucf3b6316" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">深拷贝一份动画，避免直接污染原始 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">animation</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="GpM0s"><span class="ne-text">工程意义</span></h3><p id="u263e688c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面所有 IK 修改都写到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">local_anim</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 上。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这样你还能保留原始动画做对比。代码里 A/B render 也是基于这个思路。</span></p><hr id="ijHMY" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="OzJSP"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="SrEQH" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim.pos[:, character.bone_index('Hips'), :] += root_buffer</code></pre><h3 id="jY6cm"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u5841e5c7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先把第三步求出来的 root 修正应用到整段动画的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Hips</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 上。</span></p><h3 id="IT1wX"><span class="ne-text">为什么 IK 一开始先做这个</span></h3><p id="ud7627c76" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这一步是“在已经挪过 root 的前提下，再解腿”。</span></p><p id="u762f4df0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文也明确说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u34bbd803" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第四步使用上一步得到的 root position</span></li><li id="ube377f00" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后再去满足 ankle targets。</span></li></ul><p id="u7e89aec6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这一行是在把第三阶段和第四阶段真正串起来。</span></p><hr id="f68d4" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="MOdlm"><span class="ne-text">第 3 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="q7Hhc" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)</code></pre><h3 id="K24Fa"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u2e024a46" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">对应用了 root 修正后的动画做一次 FK，得到当前全局旋转 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">gq</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和全局位置 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">gp</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="GLf9w"><span class="ne-text">为什么要先 FK</span></h3><p id="uaa1c5dc9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">后面不管是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u429613c4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">算 hip 到 ankle 的向量</span></li><li id="u13397b9d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">还是计算 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">quat_between</span></code></li><li id="u22e2f8c3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">或者把 foot 强行塞回目标</span></li></ul><p id="uc5057779" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">都要用</span><strong><span class="ne-text">全局空间</span></strong><span class="ne-text">信息。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也说了，Compute IK 这一步是多阶段的：先改膝，再重算全局位置，再转整条腿，再回写局部。</span></p><hr id="rAIWj" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="BstlY"><span class="ne-text">六、Part C：预计算腿长相关常数</span></h1><p id="u2f0b9b07" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">接下来：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="WA3xi" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code># prepare sizes
    len1 = animation.pos[0, character.bone_index('LeftLeg'), 0]
    len2 = animation.pos[0, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), 0]
    l1l2_2 = np.array([len1 * len2 *2.0], dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count)
    l1_l2 = np.array([len1*len1 + len2*len2], dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count)</code></pre><hr id="Gzdt3" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Sx6PN"><span class="ne-text">这段整体在干什么</span></h2><p id="ucd55deb8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是在为</span><strong><span class="ne-text">余弦定理</span></strong><span class="ne-text">做准备。</span></p><p id="u51acb46c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的语音稿里说得很清楚：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u5143537f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">腿在这里被看成一个三角形</span></li><li id="u53964769" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">你知道两段腿长</span></li><li id="u3fd5260e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">也知道 hip 到目标 ankle 的距离</span></li><li id="ue5ce4f07" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以可以先算膝盖角度。</span></li></ul><hr id="ZAXyQ" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="zXlpH"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="ppcbH" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>len1 = animation.pos[0, character.bone_index('LeftLeg'), 0]</code></pre><p id="u3939fe6d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">取左腿第一段长度。</span></p><h2 id="fUOqU"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="dccjf" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>len2 = animation.pos[0, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), 0]</code></pre><p id="u270a9738" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">取左腿第二段长度。</span></p><h3 id="AS8mT"><span class="ne-text">为什么只用左腿</span></h3><p id="u75d463ea" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里默认左右腿骨长相同，所以后面右腿复用这两个长度常数。</span></p><h3 id="cMute"><span class="ne-text">为什么用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">x</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 分量</span></h3><p id="u46df14fe" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">和前面 root stage 一样，这是这个 notebook 骨架的一个实现假设：腿骨偏移主要存放在局部 x 轴上。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">对这个具体 skeleton 有效。</span></p><hr id="VXOAE" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="L87nA"><span class="ne-text">第 3 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="uQbBJ" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>l1l2_2 = np.array([len1 * len2 *2.0], dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count)</code></pre><p id="u16f04e68" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">预先构造 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">2 * len1 * len2</span></code><span class="ne-text">，对应余弦定理分母。</span></p><h2 id="WOvcA"><span class="ne-text">第 4 行</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="DOWH7" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>l1_l2 = np.array([len1*len1 + len2*len2], dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count)</code></pre><p id="u30d8f343" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">预先构造 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">len1² + len2²</span></code><span class="ne-text">，对应余弦定理分子的一部分。</span></p><h3 id="GeWhh"><span class="ne-text">为什么做成 repeat(frame_count)</span></h3><p id="u361aafc6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为后面是整段向量化计算，不是一帧一帧 for-loop。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 这份 notebook 很多步骤都是“整段动画 batch 化”在算。</span></p><hr id="bM67T" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="DPilY"><span class="ne-text">七、Part D：解左腿——先算膝角，再转整条腿，再把脚踝强制贴回目标</span></h1><p id="uf58f018b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一段是 Compute IK 的真正核心。我先把整段左腿代码贴出来：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="IoPNS" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code># compute left leg angle
    #------------------------
    ankle_vector = agpos[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[:, 0, :] - gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :]
    ankle_dist = np.sum(ankle_vector*ankle_vector, axis=-1)
    ankle_arccos = (l1_l2 - ankle_dist)/l1l2_2

    # knee damping using a polynomial to ease out the over stretch
    ankle_arccos = -.037037*ankle_arccos**7 + .0314815*ankle_arccos**6 + .0374074*ankle_arccos**5 - .0260185*ankle_arccos**4 - .0114815*ankle_arccos**3 + .00453704*ankle_arccos**2 + 1.00111*ankle_arccos

    # apply the rotation on the knee
    _angle = (np.pi - np.arccos(np.maximum(np.array(-1, dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count), ankle_arccos)))/2.0
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftLeg'), 0] = np.cos(-_angle)
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftLeg'), 3] = np.sin(-_angle)
    
    # recompute the global position of the foot
    local_anim.quats = lab.utils.quat_normalize(local_anim.quats)
    gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)
    
    # rotate the leg (must be done on global position, then convert back on local)
    rot = lab.utils.quat_between(gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] - gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :], ankle_vector)
    gq[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :] = lab.utils.quat_mul(rot, gq[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :])
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :] = lab.utils.quat_ik(gq, gp, local_anim.parents)[0][:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :]

    # recompute the global position
    local_anim.quats = lab.utils.quat_normalize(local_anim.quats)
    gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)
    
    # force the foot on the constraint in global and convert in local
    gq[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[:, 0, :], agquats[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :])
    gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = agpos[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[:, 0, :]
    lq, lp = lab.utils.quat_ik(gq, gp, local_anim.parents)
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = lq[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :]
    local_anim.pos[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = lp[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :]</code></pre><p id="u46827eef" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它其实可以再拆成 3 个小步骤：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u78baa058" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先算膝盖该弯多少</span></li><li id="u9b9a381e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再把整条腿转向目标</span></li><li id="uc1077d56" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">最后把脚踝强制放回目标位姿</span></li></ol><p id="ub71dda6c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这三步和 Jerome 语音稿的描述是一一对应的：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">“先算角度 → rotate the knee → recompute → rotate the entire leg → put back my ankle。”</span></p><hr id="V2xEp" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="qdf6m"><span class="ne-text">D1：先算左腿的目标几何量</span></h2><h3 id="LA1qv"><span class="ne-text">第 1 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="Sg516" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ankle_vector = agpos[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[:, 0, :] - gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :]</code></pre><h3 id="gLxli"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="ua4b94c18" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">构造从左 hip 指向</span><strong><span class="ne-text">左目标 ankle</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 的向量。</span></p><p id="u2b4b1a9c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">分三部分看：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7d98c629" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">agpos[:, LeftFoot, :]</span></code><span class="ne-text">：原始左 ankle 位置</span></li><li id="u2d92aa01" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">+ ankle_position_buffer[:, 0, :]</span></code><span class="ne-text">：加上第二阶段算出来的 ankle 目标平移 offset</span></li><li id="u9f6c7f09" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">- gp[:, LeftUpLeg, :]</span></code><span class="ne-text">：减去当前 root 修正后左 hip 的全局位置</span></li></ul><p id="u4b21f4d9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以它得到的是：</span></p><p id="u68c25f8b" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">“当前 root 位置下，左 hip 到左目标 ankle 的目标向量。”</span></strong></p><p id="ud5d4efb1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正是论文里 single-limb IK 的目标向量 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pT - pH</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的 notebook 版本。</span></p><hr id="uEn3Z" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="FgWjq"><span class="ne-text">第 2 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="De9bc" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ankle_dist = np.sum(ankle_vector*ankle_vector, axis=-1)</code></pre><h3 id="wKVfG"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ua2f644b1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">求这个向量的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">平方长度</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，也就是 hip 到目标 ankle 的距离平方。</span></p><h3 id="jrJrQ"><span class="ne-text">为什么不用 sqrt</span></h3><p id="ude6b62d3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为后面代入余弦定理时，公式本来就可以直接用平方长度，更省一步开方。</span></p><hr id="rUkPC" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="M2ca8"><span class="ne-text">第 3 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="UsKhH" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ankle_arccos = (l1_l2 - ankle_dist)/l1l2_2</code></pre><h3 id="Mlsqu"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u0f5bf3f8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步是在构造余弦定理里的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cos(theta)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 项。</span></p><p id="uf8eedbe6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">对于两段长度分别为 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">len1</span></code><span class="ne-text">, </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">len2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的腿，若 hip 到目标 ankle 的距离为 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">d</span></code><span class="ne-text">，则有：</span></p><p id="u56b417ba" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">[<br /></span><span class="ne-text">\cos \theta = \frac{len1^2 + len2^2 - d^2}{2 len1 len2}<br /></span><span class="ne-text">]</span></p><p id="uf122357e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正是这行代码。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里说“prepare all the different length compared to the distance, computing the vector, computing the arccos”，讲的就是这一段。</span></p><p id="u0fb075fa" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文里也明确说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud3f80b25" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先把腿看成 triangle</span></li><li id="u65e505fd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再用几何关系求 knee angle。</span></li></ul><hr id="lzL12" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="HUjZB"><span class="ne-text">D2：对这个 arccos 输入做 knee damping</span></h2><h3 id="SUGgj"><span class="ne-text">第 4 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="YUEP3" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ankle_arccos = -.037037*ankle_arccos**7 + .0314815*ankle_arccos**6 + .0374074*ankle_arccos**5 - .0260185*ankle_arccos**4 - .0114815*ankle_arccos**3 + .00453704*ankle_arccos**2 + 1.00111*ankle_arccos</code></pre><h3 id="Wqype"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ua91f8d47" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把原始的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cos(theta)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 输入通过一个多项式压一遍，得到“更保守的膝角输入”。</span></p><h3 id="BjgSv"><span class="ne-text">为什么这么做</span></h3><p id="u6c531a59" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为当腿接近打直时，直接按普通解析 IK 去算，会出现 knee pop。论文给的标准方案是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u99c0af27" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">用 knee damping 限制靠近完全伸直时的膝盖旋转量</span></li><li id="u66b79371" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果还差一点到不了目标，就再允许腿长轻微变化。</span></li></ul><p id="ua1009f5b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的 notebook 没有按论文去“真的拉长 thigh/shin bone”，而是自己做了一个工程改写：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ue8aa6f21" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先把 arccos 输入送进多项式</span></li><li id="u3439037d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">保证永远别真的打成完全 straight</span></li><li id="u6f72eb28" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">让残余误差更多由 ankle 附近吸收<br /></span><span class="ne-text">他在语音稿里亲口说了：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">“not exactly how they were doing it”，他不是去 stretch actual leg，而是锁住 knee angle，让 deformation 更多吸收到 ankle/foot relative to leg。</span></li></ul><p id="u39d6fd6b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这里你一定要分清：</span></p><h3 id="g3ylR"><span class="ne-text">论文</span></h3><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u33fe3244" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">knee damping</span></li><li id="ubd4d1b72" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后必要时 stretch leg length。</span></li></ul><h3 id="hzf9S"><span class="ne-text">Jerome notebook</span></h3><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ucef29adb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">用 polynomial 近似 knee damping</span></li><li id="u9a05a0ec" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再把剩余误差吸收到 ankle/foot 目标那边。</span></li></ul><hr id="mnpoE" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="N1zk2"><span class="ne-text">D3：把这个膝角真正写进左小腿 / 左膝关节</span></h2><h3 id="xGKD9"><span class="ne-text">第 5 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="ILTvr" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>_angle = (np.pi - np.arccos(np.maximum(np.array(-1, dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count), ankle_arccos)))/2.0</code></pre><h3 id="wYUM9"><span class="ne-text">这行在干什么</span></h3><p id="u2a54cec8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它先把前面那个经过 damping 的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_arccos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 取 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">arccos</span></code><span class="ne-text">，得到角度，再变形成当前骨架需要的“膝关节旋转半角”。</span></p><p id="ufd447f1d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">分开看：</span></p><h4 id="Ry9a0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">np.maximum(..., ankle_arccos)</span></code></h4><p id="u9facbe58" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">只做下界钳制到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-1</span></code><span class="ne-text">，避免数值误差导致 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">arccos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 输入掉到非法范围以下。</span></p><h4 id="T4di5"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">np.arccos(...)</span></code></h4><p id="u537b6ecc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cos(theta)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 变回角度。</span></p><h4 id="DrzdJ"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">np.pi - ...</span></code></h4><p id="u1c8e33ad" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把三角几何角转换成这个骨架链实际需要的弯曲角定义。</span></p><h4 id="ebLDQ"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">/ 2.0</span></code></h4><p id="u6e39d719" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为后面写 quaternion 时，</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">cos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">sin</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 用的是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">半角</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="PWM9J"><span class="ne-text">为什么是半角</span></h3><p id="u08232ca1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为单位四元数表示旋转时，本来就写成：</span></p><p id="uee4fe1b8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">[<br /></span><span class="ne-text">q = [\cos(\phi/2), \sin(\phi/2)\hat{n}]<br /></span><span class="ne-text">]</span></p><p id="uc848b8af" class="ne-p"><span id="kECaw" class="ne-math"><img src="https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/__latex/096f6de2ddaec9a2a644ad48f6f6bebf.svg"></span></p><p id="u6e7de2a5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这里 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_angle</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 是拿来直接塞进 quaternion 的半角变量。</span></p><hr id="JdmY8" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="ATgx8"><span class="ne-text">第 6、7 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="aSzDt" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftLeg'), 0] = np.cos(-_angle)
local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftLeg'), 3] = np.sin(-_angle)</code></pre><h3 id="QGz9m"><span class="ne-text">这两行在干什么</span></h3><p id="uba31d6de" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">直接重写左膝关节（这里骨架里叫 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftLeg</span></code><span class="ne-text">）的局部 quaternion。</span></p><h3 id="IBQhx"><span class="ne-text">为什么只写 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">[0]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">[3]</span></code></h3><p id="u7ef5afb2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这个骨架里膝盖被当成</span><strong><span class="ne-text">单自由度铰链</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，主要绕固定轴转。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这里的写法就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u946219c2" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">w = cos(half_angle)</span></code></li><li id="ud44ef9f6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">某个固定轴分量 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">= sin(half_angle)</span></code></li></ul><p id="uc68b1ab1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是只保留“绕一根轴的纯旋转”。</span></p><h3 id="pzqJu"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="ub756e143" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文明确说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udfb0d82a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">knee can only rotate about a single axis</span></li><li id="uf2ce45e0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以先调整 knee angle。</span></li></ul><p id="ued0d2e9f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 语音稿也说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uc9932fd6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先 compute the angle</span></li><li id="uc3600d1d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">then rotate the knee。</span></li></ul><hr id="fvWss" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="iAjtx"><span class="ne-text">D4：重算 FK，因为膝盖已经变了</span></h2><h3 id="ymtxj"><span class="ne-text">第 8、9 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="MrzhW" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim.quats = lab.utils.quat_normalize(local_anim.quats)
gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)</code></pre><h3 id="ZI9kW"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u51b2c069" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为你刚刚直接改了局部 quaternion，所以要：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u769f54ab" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先 normalize，避免数值误差</span></li><li id="ua3d7826e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再做 FK，得到“现在膝盖弯完之后”的全局姿态</span></li></ol><h3 id="yLdIn"><span class="ne-text">为什么必须重算</span></h3><p id="u2bc0ce99" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不然你后面根本不知道：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9a7d464b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前 foot 被膝盖弯曲带到了哪里</span></li><li id="udf1f42a3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">还差多少才能朝向 target</span></li></ul><p id="uc89ba698" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 语音稿里也明确说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u826cde49" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">rotate the knee</span></li><li id="u9665e42b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">then recompute the position。</span></li></ul><hr id="NSL19" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="qleok"><span class="ne-text">D5：把整条左腿再转向目标 ankle 方向</span></h2><h3 id="zyIaf"><span class="ne-text">第 10 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="xXlKH" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>rot = lab.utils.quat_between(gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] - gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :], ankle_vector)</code></pre><h3 id="f1ytd"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u758bbf9c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">计算一个 quaternion，把：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u84d398ed" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前膝盖弯完以后，左 hip→左 foot 的方向</span></li><li id="u02749bb0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">旋转到目标 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_vector</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的方向</span></li></ul><p id="u53ab2be3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是“整条腿朝目标再对准一下”。</span></p><h3 id="Qds9h"><span class="ne-text">对应论文</span></h3><p id="ub6cf7c64" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文原算法中，这一步就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u82578af5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先得到新 ankle 位置 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">\hat p_A</span></code></li><li id="u8b4101c6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Rot(\hat p_A - p_H, p_T - p_H)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 去旋转 hip，让腿真正 pointing toward target。</span></li></ul><p id="u795fbf03" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这行 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">quat_between(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 就是 notebook 的实现版本。</span></p><hr id="eFnXF" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="pQiwp"><span class="ne-text">第 11 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="gKf9U" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gq[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :] = lab.utils.quat_mul(rot, gq[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :])</code></pre><h3 id="qP8qT"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u1b4a30b4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把这次“整条腿指向目标”的全局旋转，乘到左大腿 / 左 hip 关节的全局旋转上。</span></p><h3 id="vqfGR"><span class="ne-text">直观理解</span></h3><p id="u162eac9a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">刚才是改膝盖“腿长和弯曲量”，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">现在是改 hip “整条腿朝哪指”。</span></p><hr id="nVC2h" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="Czecx"><span class="ne-text">第 12 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="MsdKw" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :] = lab.utils.quat_ik(gq, gp, local_anim.parents)[0][:, character.bone_index('LeftUpLeg'), :]</code></pre><h3 id="XhTGT"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="u04848449" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">前一行改的是全局旋转，但动画实际存的是局部 quaternion。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以这里要用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">quat_ik(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 把全局姿态逆回局部，取出新的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">LeftUpLeg</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 局部旋转写回去。</span></p><h3 id="a7TXs"><span class="ne-text">为什么这一行很关键</span></h3><p id="u9a1eb2e7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它说明 Jerome 这里的 IK 不是“黑盒数值迭代”，而是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u1a5ed686" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">在全局空间做几何操作</span></li><li id="ua89902d5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再转换回局部动画参数</span></li></ul><p id="ue122d5b3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这和论文强调的“analytic IK”思路是一致的。</span></p><hr id="HNQOD" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="owVAN"><span class="ne-text">D6：再次重算 FK，因为 hip 也被改了</span></h2><h3 id="HBkqg"><span class="ne-text">第 13、14 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="Djury" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim.quats = lab.utils.quat_normalize(local_anim.quats)
gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)</code></pre><h3 id="UEjvK"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ud35b90d2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">现在膝盖和 hip 都改了，再次刷新全局姿态。</span></p><h3 id="v9c7K"><span class="ne-text">为什么要再来一次</span></h3><p id="u8857e402" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为最后一步要“强制脚踝精确贴回目标位姿”，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以必须在最新骨架状态下操作。</span></p><hr id="uCx4R" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="sUJYo"><span class="ne-text">D7：最后强制把左脚踝贴到目标位置和目标朝向</span></h2><h3 id="ytudm"><span class="ne-text">第 15 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="sw17i" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gq[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[:, 0, :], agquats[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :])</code></pre><h3 id="GhKoP"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uff75e0ef" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把左脚踝的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">目标朝向</span></strong><span class="ne-text">直接设置成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u72c26801" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始 ankle 朝向</span></li><li id="ud2fbd8a4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再乘上第二阶段求出的 rotation offset</span></li></ul><p id="ub0c2dddc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正对应第二阶段的输出语义。</span></p><hr id="Opf1G" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="WFFZ0"><span class="ne-text">第 16 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="dF62c" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gp[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = agpos[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[:, 0, :]</code></pre><h3 id="rxQt9"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ua567e1c7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把左脚踝的</span><strong><span class="ne-text">目标位置</span></strong><span class="ne-text">直接设置成：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u19c9530d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">原始 ankle 位置</span></li><li id="u40752af3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再加上第二阶段求出的 position offset</span></li></ul><p id="u35fe50ed" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是“我要强制这个 foot 真的出现在那个 target ankle location 上”。</span></p><hr id="f9GMq" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="OQbwF"><span class="ne-text">第 17 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="c26Uo" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>lq, lp = lab.utils.quat_ik(gq, gp, local_anim.parents)</code></pre><h3 id="G81x0"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="ua6fbde0b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">现在你已经在全局空间里明确指定了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u152e24d5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 foot 的全局旋转</span></li><li id="u4e794862" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 foot 的全局位置</span></li></ul><p id="ua94ca1f5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">于是再次做全局→局部逆变换，得到新的局部 quats / pos。</span></p><h3 id="YnDHI"><span class="ne-text">为什么这一步叫“force the foot on the constraint”</span></h3><p id="u75b7bc7d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为这一刻不是“尽量朝目标”，而是：</span></p><p id="u2a95cd98" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">直接把 foot 的全局目标钉死。</span></strong></p><p id="u459c6b05" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在注释里也写了 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">force the foot on the constraint in global and convert in local</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="xCOjC" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="KRRJJ"><span class="ne-text">第 18、19 行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="KWPsD" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = lq[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :]
local_anim.pos[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] = lp[:, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :]</code></pre><h3 id="EjGYt"><span class="ne-text">作用</span></h3><p id="uc3b1c5c2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把计算出的左 foot 局部旋转和局部位置写回动画。</span></p><h3 id="Je90z"><span class="ne-text">到这里左腿发生了什么</span></h3><p id="u1866d917" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">左腿整个链已经经历了：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="ua1e414c6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">应用 root 修正</span></li><li id="uc10db0bd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">算膝盖角度</span></li><li id="u428df80a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">膝盖做 knee damping 后弯曲</span></li><li id="u8cc87241" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">大腿整体对准目标</span></li><li id="u7d0ed47f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">脚踝最后精确贴到目标位置 / 朝向</span></li></ol><p id="uc8551c8c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是 Jerome 说的“all the step of the paper”。</span></p><hr id="JDGol" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="LUxey"><span class="ne-text">八、Part E：右腿完全重复左腿流程</span></h1><p id="u72f35c01" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">右腿代码和左腿是镜像的：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="we1vJ" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code># compute right leg angle
    #------------------------
    ankle_vector = agpos[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[:, 1, :] - gp[:, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :]
    ankle_dist = np.sum(ankle_vector*ankle_vector, axis=-1)
    ankle_arccos = (l1_l2 - ankle_dist)/l1l2_2
    
    # knee damping using a polynomial to ease out the over stretch
    ankle_arccos = -.037037*ankle_arccos**7 + .0314815*ankle_arccos**6 + .0374074*ankle_arccos**5 - .0260185*ankle_arccos**4 - .0114815*ankle_arccos**3 + .00453704*ankle_arccos**2 + 1.00111*ankle_arccos
    
    # apply the rotation on the knee
    _angle = (np.pi - np.arccos(np.maximum(np.array(-1, dtype=np.float32).repeat(frame_count), ankle_arccos)))/2.0
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('RightLeg'), 0] = np.cos(-_angle)
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('RightLeg'), 3] = np.sin(-_angle)
    
    # recompute the global position of the foot
    local_anim.quats = lab.utils.quat_normalize(local_anim.quats)
    gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)
    
    # rotate the leg (must be done on global position, then convert back on local)
    rot = lab.utils.quat_between(gp[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] - gp[:, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :], ankle_vector)
    gq[:, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :] = lab.utils.quat_mul(rot, gq[:, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :])
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :] = lab.utils.quat_ik(gq, gp, local_anim.parents)[0][:, character.bone_index('RightUpLeg'), :]

    # recompute the global position
    local_anim.quats = lab.utils.quat_normalize(local_anim.quats)
    gq, gp = lab.utils.quat_fk(local_anim.quats, local_anim.pos, local_anim.parents)
    
    # force the foot on the constraint in global and convert in local
    gq[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[:, 1, :], agquats[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :])
    gp[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] = agpos[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[:, 1, :]
    lq, lp = lab.utils.quat_ik(gq, gp, local_anim.parents)
    local_anim.quats[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] = lq[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :]
    local_anim.pos[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] = lp[:, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :]</code></pre><p id="u977a675b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这整段和左腿完全对称，唯一变化是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u46a39c5f" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Left*</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 全换成 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Right*</span></code></li><li id="u1efa2864" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer[:, 0, :]</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 换成 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">[:, 1, :]</span></code></li></ul><h3 id="URVMt"><span class="ne-text">为什么右腿不用重新单独准备腿长</span></h3><p id="uad679f2d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为默认左右骨长一致，所以 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">len1 / len2 / l1_l2 / l1l2_2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 可以复用。</span></p><h3 id="Tq9b3"><span class="ne-text">工程意义</span></h3><p id="ua7d85d43" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这说明 notebook 的“单腿解析 IK 求解器”已经足够一般，只是左右各跑一遍。</span></p><hr id="AXeCm" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="jygGU"><span class="ne-text">九、Part F：返回最终求解结果</span></h1><p id="uf02dd043" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">最后这两行：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="KWmwc" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>return local_anim

solved_animation = compute_final_animation()</code></pre><hr id="ikwcp" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="IC4XE"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">return local_anim</span></code></h2><p id="u2469efa2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">返回整段已经求解完的动画。</span></p><h2 id="DCOV9"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved_animation = compute_final_animation()</span></code></h2><p id="ubada5a25" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">立即跑一遍，得到可视化用的结果缓存。</span></p><h3 id="YWHmx"><span class="ne-text">和论文的关系</span></h3><p id="ubb623128" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">到这里，第四阶段“Meeting the Target Ankle Configuration”已经做完了。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果你严格按论文顺序，下一步其实应该进入 final processing，去处理 constraint on/off 时的整体 blend off。</span></p><p id="u719396ea" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里也明确说了：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u004ad6c9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">到这里你会发现脚已经能 properly placed</span></li><li id="u17d5344f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">但在 constraint 开关时 still have pops</span></li><li id="ubc9d88be" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那就是下一步 final processing 要解决的。</span></li></ul><hr id="PZbZn" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="eklgM"><span class="ne-text">十、Part G：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">render(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这一整块在做什么</span></h1><p id="u67090ae5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你还贴了一个 render：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="HSxL6" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def render(frame, solved=True):
    
    anim = solved_animation if solved else animation
    p = (anim.pos[frame,...])
    q = (anim.quats[frame,...])
        
    a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(q, p)
    viewer.set_shadow_poi(p[0])
    
    viewer.begin_shadow()
    viewer.draw(character, a)
    viewer.end_shadow()
    
    viewer.begin_display()
    viewer.draw_ground()
    viewer.draw(character, a)
    
    contacts = np.eye(4, dtype=np.float32)[np.newaxis,...].repeat(4, axis=0)
    contacts[:, :3, 3] = constraint_positions_buffer[frame, :, :]
    
    viewer.draw(target, contacts)
    viewer.end_display()

    viewer.disable(depth_test=True)
   
    viewer.draw_lines(character.world_skeleton_lines(a))
    
    gquat = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 0, :], agquats[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :])
    gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :]
    a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
    viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)
    
    gquat = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 1, :], agquats[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :])
    gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :]
    a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
    viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)
    
    gquat = agquats[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :]
    gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :] + root_buffer[frame, :]
    a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
    viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)
    
    viewer.execute_commands()</code></pre><p id="u844ecf74" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这块不是 IK 计算本体，而是一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">验证器 / 调试显示器</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="Pm3YB" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="RCBzh"><span class="ne-text">1）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">anim = solved_animation if solved else animation</span></code></h2><p id="u7da2a525" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">允许你在 UI 里切换看：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud623fedc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">求解前</span></li><li id="u7b835821" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">求解后</span></li></ul><p id="u0d05c6ee" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是最直接的 A/B。</span></p><hr id="Yzr0y" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="JEnub"><span class="ne-text">2）前半段 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">viewer.draw(character, a)</span></code></h2><p id="u77bfcc5c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先画当前角色姿态。</span></p><hr id="MPYeK" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="HVGyF"><span class="ne-text">3）</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts[:, :3, 3] = constraint_positions_buffer[frame, :, :]</span></code></h2><p id="uab9cc3b5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">把第一阶段算出来的四个脚底地面 target 画出来。</span></p><h3 id="WgViA"><span class="ne-text">意义</span></h3><p id="u72813e16" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以直接看脚底最终有没有贴回这些 target。</span></p><hr id="V7ikQ" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Q6fUz"><span class="ne-text">4）左脚、右脚 ankle axis</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="bUVXv" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer, agquats[...])
gpos  = agpos[...] + ankle_position_buffer[...]</code></pre><p id="u83b2f58f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这两段画的是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">第二阶段算出来的 ankle target axes</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><h3 id="VlGlQ"><span class="ne-text">意义</span></h3><p id="u8186a0af" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以看到：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ue13ebdc6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">IK 解出来的腿</span></li><li id="u0544e0b3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">和“本来该满足的 ankle target”</span></li><li id="ua1dc5eea" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">是否真的对上</span></li></ul><hr id="rlJnj" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="ML62W"><span class="ne-text">5）hips axis</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="T0q6z" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gpos = agpos[frame, Hips] + root_buffer[frame]</code></pre><p id="u14e5b53e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段画的是第三阶段 root target。</span></p><h3 id="TcARW"><span class="ne-text">意义</span></h3><p id="u42566d77" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">于是一个画面里你同时能看到：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf34b1097" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第一阶段：constraint positions</span></li><li id="u9914c594" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第二阶段：ankle targets</span></li><li id="u09e2b72a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第三阶段：root target</span></li><li id="uf2380d1e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第四阶段：solved_animation 实际是否把整条腿摆到位</span></li></ul><p id="u869db4cc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就是为什么这个 render 很有价值：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">它不是只看结果 mesh，而是把整条求解链全部可视化。</span></p><hr id="L8R9P" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="nTkqY"><span class="ne-text">十一、这一步和论文的“同”与“不同”</span></h1><p id="uacdfe843" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一段你一定要吃透。</span></p><h2 id="dTt9d"><span class="ne-text">相同点</span></h2><p id="ue1e1b238" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 的 notebook 完整保留了论文第四阶段的主结构：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="ued434da3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先根据腿长和目标距离算膝角</span></li><li id="u1e459144" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再 rotate the leg toward target</span></li><li id="u7eb692ae" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再把 ankle 精确放到目标位姿</span></li><li id="u10f505ef" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这样完成 target ankle configuration。</span></li></ol><hr id="sS4mW" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="kfEXF"><span class="ne-text">不同点</span></h2><p id="ue4055f55" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">论文在 knee damping 后，如果 ankle 还差一点到不了 target，会：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ufaf8c52e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">轻微调整腿长</span></li><li id="uaa074a12" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">改 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">oK</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">oA</span></code></li><li id="ue4bb96dd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">真正 stretch bones。</span></li></ul><p id="u21dd014b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 则明确说：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ueff1d47a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“I will not stretch the actual leg”</span></li><li id="uec20f779" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“I will just lock the angle of the knee”</span></li><li id="uaa7d5d5b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“and stretch by moving the foot relative to the leg”</span></li><li id="u06482343" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">也就是把那点误差更多吸收到 ankle / foot 那边。</span></li></ul><p id="ue572de4e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以 notebook 是：</span></p><p id="ua3545fd2" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">论文思路的教学改写版</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">而不是一份 100% 逐公式对等的实现。</span></p><hr id="mD3v8" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="xNkKo"><span class="ne-text">十二、把这一步浓缩成一句话</span></h1><p id="u39de5f00"
