class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">Compute IK</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 理解成：</span></p><p id="uf9b268ac" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先在已经应用 root 修正的姿态上，把每条腿看成一个两段骨的三角形；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">先用余弦定理求膝盖该弯多少；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">再对这个膝角做 knee damping，避免腿接近打直时的 knee pop；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">然后把整条腿从 hip 处转向目标 ankle 方向；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">最后把 foot/ankle 在全局空间里强制贴回前面算好的 target position 和 target orientation；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">左腿做完，再对右腿重复一次。</span></p><hr id="bkavv" class="ne-hr"></details>
####  knee damping polynomial  
| ![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2026/png/45532692/1774336137932-eb48dd75-46c9-4812-9cb2-6994d3e54484.png) | 这张图的目的，是**直观展示 knee damping polynomial 对 IK 膝盖解算输入的“限幅/缓冲”作用**：蓝线代表原始的几何输入，橙线代表经过多项式修正后的输出，它说明在腿部接近完全伸直、最容易发生 knee pop 的危险区间里，系统不会直接使用原始解析解，而是把输入轻轻推回到更保守、更平滑的范围，从而避免膝盖突然打直或角度剧烈跳变；换句话说，这张图不是在展示角色动画本身，而是在展示 **“为防止膝盖弹跳而加入的非线性修正函数长什么样”**。 |
| --- | --- |


<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u5773ca53"><span class="ne-text">GPT</span></summary><p id="u860310c6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你这里“不明白橙线为什么比蓝线高”，核心原因其实有两层。</span></p><p id="u1175474e" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">第一层，是负数坐标的直觉问题。</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">你这张图的纵轴全是负数，比如 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-1.00、-0.98、-0.96...</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">在这种坐标里，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">越高不代表数值越小，反而代表数值越大</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。所以：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="lV5er" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>-0.98  比  -1.00  高</code></pre><p id="u04d358cd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-0.98 &gt; -1.00</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u52cf63fe" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以橙线比蓝线高，实际含义是：</span></p><p id="u03327f34" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">多项式输出 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">z</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text"> 比原始输入 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">x</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text"> 更大了一点点。</span></strong></p><p id="ue5341db4" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">不是“更猛地压下去”，而是“把它从 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 那个危险极限值往上抬了一点”。</span></p><hr id="zbz4F" class="ne-hr"><p id="ud75ed0e1" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">第二层，是这个 polynomial 本来就是故意这么设计的。</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><span class="ne-text">在这段 IK 里，</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">x</span></code><span class="ne-text">（也就是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_arccos</span></code><span class="ne-text">）接近 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 时，代表腿快要</span><strong><span class="ne-text">完全打直</span></strong><span class="ne-text">了。</span></p><p id="u897ff320" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">而代码后面会做：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="ppFPf" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>_angle = (np.pi - np.arccos(x)) / 2.0</code></pre><p id="u38f70482" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这时候如果：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub5dfd3bd" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">x = -1</span></code></li><li id="ueee7d375" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那么 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">arccos(-1) = π</span></code></li><li id="u72601b9f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_angle = 0</span></code></li></ul><p id="ue1a375e8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这就对应</span><strong><span class="ne-text">完全伸直</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，也就是最容易出现 knee pop 的危险状态。</span></p><p id="u90e2b3ef" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以 Jerome 才故意让 polynomial 在 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">-1</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 附近把输入稍微往上抬一点：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="oGVD2" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>原来: x = -1.00
修后: z ≈ -0.98</code></pre><p id="uc15bfe12" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这样会发生什么？</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0b8563d5" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">arccos(-1.00) = 180°</span></code></li><li id="u2c1625ca" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">arccos(-0.98) &lt; 180°</span></code></li><li id="u92f80c16" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">所以 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_angle</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 不再是 0，而会保留一个</span><strong><span class="ne-text">很小的弯曲角</span></strong></li></ul><p id="u49585664" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说：</span></p><p id="ucb804b34" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">橙线比蓝线高，不是在“出错”，而是在“故意把接近完全伸直的解拉回一点”，让膝盖永远别真的打成死直。</span></strong></p><p id="u12711908" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正是 knee damping 的目的。</span></p><hr id="DJkol" class="ne-hr"><p id="ua7ad83fe" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把它想成这样：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="OLCWr" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>蓝线：原始几何解
      “理论上可以完全打直，那就真的打直到 180°”

橙线：修正后的工程解
      “别真打直，留一点点弯曲余量，避免膝盖突然弹一下”</code></pre><hr id="aWfTz" class="ne-hr"><p id="ub183f4df" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">再用一个极简 ASCII 看：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="xIthD" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>危险区（原始）:
x = -1.00  ---&gt; arccos = 180° ---&gt; 膝盖完全打直 ---&gt; 容易 pop

修正后:
z = -0.98  ---&gt; arccos &lt; 180° ---&gt; 膝盖还留一点弯 ---&gt; 更稳</code></pre><hr id="pAUzr" class="ne-hr"><p id="uc9767f1b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以一句话总结就是：</span></p><p id="u7cbb490a" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">橙线比蓝线高，是因为这个 polynomial 在 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">-1</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text"> 附近故意把输入值“抬高一点”，把腿从“完全伸直”的极限状态拉回来一点，从而减少 knee pop。</span></strong></p><p id="ua8443220" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">我可以下一条直接给你画一张真正的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">x-z</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 输入输出示意，并把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">x=-1</span></code><span class="ne-text">、</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">z≈-0.98</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 代进 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">arccos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_angle</span></code><span class="ne-text">，让你一眼看懂它为什么能防膝盖弹跳。</span></p></details>
#### 数据流程图
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/__mermaid_v3/86cd280b04a00413564267f0604aad74.svg)

### Final Processing
[https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1/c/69c1466d-9b20-83aa-ac9a-9d82d0d81db8](https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-69affc08e9088191811d69bef0b44dc1/c/69c1466d-9b20-83aa-ac9a-9d82d0d81db8)

[https://chatgpt.com/share/69c23465-6a88-8009-863d-c0a9c69022ec](https://chatgpt.com/share/69c23465-6a88-8009-863d-c0a9c69022ec)

#### 总览
<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="uee60609b"><span class="ne-text">GPT</span></summary><p id="u3dd7ff9b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">可以，这一段我按你希望的方式来讲：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u7cb43996" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先说 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">Final Processing 的目的</span></strong></li><li id="u99d85c28" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再说 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">它的总体输入 / 输出</span></strong></li><li id="u3757460a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后把你贴的整块代码拆开，按合适位置逐段解释</span></li><li id="ufea2954b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">最后给你一张 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">Mermaid 流程图</span></strong></li></ol><hr id="yC14y" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="Txs8x"><span class="ne-text">一、Final Processing 这一步到底是干什么的</span></h1><p id="u98301567" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步是整篇论文的 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">第 5 阶段</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">前面 1～4 阶段已经做了这些事：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u337d17bd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">找到约束点应该在哪里</span></li><li id="uad896c90" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">算出 ankle 该去哪里</span></li><li id="u9866387d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">算 root 应该怎么挪</span></li><li id="u92ab585a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">让腿通过 IK / 长度调整去满足这些目标</span></li></ul><p id="ue2d8b335" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但即使这些都做完了，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">约束刚开启、刚关闭</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 的地方还是很容易出现跳变，也就是 Jerome 在视频里反复说的那个 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">pop</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。论文对这个阶段的原话就是：当前面几步在“约束持续成立”的区间里不会引入跳变，但</span><strong><span class="ne-text">当约束 active / inactive 切换时，仍然可能出现 discontinuity</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，所以最后一步要把这些 adjustment 平滑地“blend off / dissipate”到周围无约束帧里。</span></p><p id="u2a79721b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里的解释也和论文完全对上：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">前面算出来的 ankle / root 都是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">相对于原动画的 offset</span></strong><span class="ne-text">，所以最后一步不是重新发明一套 IK，而是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u491ec9db" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">检测“约束开 / 关”的边界</span></li><li id="u74177bd9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">把 offset 往前后传播一点</span></li><li id="ua933feaf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再逐渐 blend in / blend out</span></li><li id="ub7fe8ac2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这样就不会在边界 frame 上突然啪一下跳过去。</span></li></ul><hr id="KWG8U" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="I8jke"><span class="ne-text">二、这一步的总体输入和输出是什么</span></h1><h2 id="cGK4M"><span class="ne-text">1. 输入</span></h2><p id="ud5be2a30" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步真正吃进去的，不是原始 BVH，而是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">前几步已经算好的 buffer</span></strong><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udba3d6f5" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u351da7d6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">每帧哪些接触成立</span></li><li id="ucb7769de" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这里四列通常是：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">左 heel / 左 ball / 右 heel / 右 ball</span></code></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u43226ca4" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u6a595c0a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 3 阶段 root placement 算出来的 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">root 平移修正量</span></strong></li><li id="u3c1f1e7a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">不是完整 root 绝对位置，而是修正量 / offset</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uda6a22b6" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ud3e49247" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">第 2 阶段和后续流程得到的 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">左脚 / 右脚 ankle 旋转 offset</span></strong></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u043a5c37" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u48a65e72" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左脚 / 右脚 ankle 位置 offset</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7f53ad7b" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">frame_count</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="uf33a8a34" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">总帧数</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua218e1e3" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ue23ed519" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Final Processing 的邻域窗口大小</span></li><li id="u420447b1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">论文图 2 和 4.5 节里，Final Processing 专门对应 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这个窗口。</span></li></ul></ul><h2 id="yLDRy"><span class="ne-text">2. 输出</span></h2><p id="u7218e861" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一步输出的不是“最终角色姿态”本身，而是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u7c63945c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">被平滑后的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u6acf9eaf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">被平滑后的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u90a45114" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">被平滑后的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="uf1878387" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">然后这些平滑后的 buffer 再喂给：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="sxeTI" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>solved_animation = compute_final_animation()</code></pre><p id="u9474275c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">得到最后可播放的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved_animation</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u7386f769" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以你一定要分清：</span></p><p id="u476b9cd9" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">Final Processing 的直接输出是“更平滑的 offset buffer”</span></strong><span class="ne-text"><br /></span><strong><span class="ne-text">而不是最终骨骼 pose 本体。</span></strong></p><p id="u594a44f8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这也和论文的 buffer 化实现一致：论文 Figure 7 就是在讲，前面几步逐层生成 buffer，最后一层 buffer 做的就是 “adjustments are blended off as described in Section 4.5”。</span></p><hr id="JCmYz" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="gg5vS"><span class="ne-text">三、先给你一句最直观的话</span></h1><p id="u02753bd9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段 Final Processing 代码本质上做的是：</span></p><p id="ub15903a7" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">如果这一帧没有接触，那我就去前后找最近的“有接触并且算出过修正”的帧，把它们的 root / ankle 修正量衰减后借一点过来，让当前帧也带一点点修正，从而把“有约束”和“无约束”之间的边界磨平。</span></strong></p><hr id="PYylR" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="ZvDBu"><span class="ne-text">四、按代码拆开讲</span></h1><hr id="vxPem" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="EgR23"><span class="ne-text">Part A：窗口大小与主循环</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="jU17k" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>L4 = 10

for frame in range(frame_count):</code></pre><h3 id="c1Y0Q"><span class="ne-text">这两行的作用</span></h3><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub3ea3b66" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4 = 10</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="uf24bb9c1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">表示 Final Processing 最多往前看 10 帧、往后看 10 帧</span></li><li id="ue6f8f1eb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">也就是论文里 phase 5 使用的邻域长度 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u703bfd60" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">for frame in range(frame_count):</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u1958e5f6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">对整段动画的每一帧都做一次“边界平滑检查”</span></li></ul></ul><h3 id="QULdf"><span class="ne-text">工程理解</span></h3><p id="u147e432f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">前面的阶段更多是在“约束区间内部”做事。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这一阶段是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uc43ba877" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">不再只盯着约束帧</span></li><li id="u952ca385" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而是把整段动画每一帧都扫一遍</span></li><li id="uf2d1ab2d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">看看哪些 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">无约束帧</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 其实正处在约束开关边缘附近</span></li><li id="ua0f66b80" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果是，就给它一点衰减后的修正量</span></li></ul><hr id="SaLpj" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="wPYDw"><span class="ne-text">Part B：root 的 Final Processing</span></h2><p id="ub55f1a1d" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">先看这块：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="jrLXI" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code># if there is no constraints we can check if we have to smooth the hips
if not np.any(contacts[frame,:]):
    pos = np.array([0,0,0], dtype=np.float32)
    
    # backward
    backhit = -1
    for f in range(L4):
        if frame-f &gt;= 0 and np.any(contacts[frame,:]):
            backhit = f
            alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)
            alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
            pos = root_buffer[frame-f]*(alpha)
            break
            
    # forward
    for f in range(L4):
        if frame+f &lt; frame_count and np.any(contacts[frame,:]):
            alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)
            alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
            fpos = root_buffer[frame+f]*(alpha)
            if backhit &gt; -1:
                alpha = (backhit+1.0)/(backhit+f+1.0)
                pos = pos*(1.0-alpha) + fpos*(alpha)
            else:
                pos = fpos
            break
            
    root_buffer[frame] = pos</code></pre><hr id="fSqJm" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="by5Km"><span class="ne-text">B1. 这段在语义上想干什么</span></h3><p id="uea91f2c3" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它想做的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua9bc6b6d" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">只有当当前帧完全没有接触</span></strong><span class="ne-text"> 时</span></li><li id="ua2661198" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">才去决定：这一帧的 root offset 要不要从前后邻近的约束帧“借一点”过来</span></li></ul><p id="u6692dc70" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">为什么？</span></p><p id="u49d180fe" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为如果当前帧本来就在接触区间里，前面的阶段已经算过 root correction 了。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">Final Processing 主要补的是：</span></p><p id="u2cd53da1" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">约束外侧的那些帧</span></strong></p><p id="u94d1d578" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这和论文 4.5 节是一致的：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">对于当前帧里“没有因为满足约束而被直接调整”的参数，去前后邻域扫描，看有没有附近 frame 曾经被约束调整过；如果有，就把这些 adjustment blend off 过来。</span></p><hr id="QaZJe" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="Evg4c"><span class="ne-text">B2. </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">if not np.any(contacts[frame,:]):</span></code></h3><p id="u74839d67" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一行的意思是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub0b52611" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧四个接触位（左 heel/ball、右 heel/ball）全都没开</span></li><li id="uab8a4597" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">说明这一帧是“无约束帧”</span></li><li id="u6d5fbbb9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">才需要做边界补偿</span></li></ul><p id="u62bd0459" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧有接触，那 root_buffer 这帧本来就已经是约束求解阶段算出来的值，不需要这里改。</span></p><hr id="qaR2B" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="cb66N"><span class="ne-text">B3. </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pos = np.array([0,0,0], dtype=np.float32)</span></code></h3><p id="u42003392" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是先给 root 的最终平滑结果一个默认值：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2b3264b5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先假设“这一帧没有从邻居借到任何 root 修正”</span></li><li id="u27a40aba" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那么它的 root offset 就是 0</span></li></ul><p id="u28443baf" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是：</span></p><p id="u156fbb40" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">如果前后都找不到可借的修正量，那这帧就不加额外 root correction。</span></strong></p><p id="u78896a57" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这也符合论文说法：如果前后都没有找到相邻的已调整帧，那参数就保持不变。</span></p><hr id="JHZaE" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="jMnWC"><span class="ne-text">B4. backward 搜索</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="S42ND" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>backhit = -1
for f in range(L4):
    if frame-f &gt;= 0 and np.any(contacts[frame,:]):
        backhit = f
        alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)
        alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
        pos = root_buffer[frame-f]*(alpha)
        break</code></pre><p id="u11bb5367" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这段逻辑上想做的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u48519c1b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">从当前帧往过去看，最多看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 帧</span></li><li id="u0f867151" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">找到最近的一个“有接触 / 有 root 调整”的帧</span></li><li id="u75420591" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">记下它离当前帧的距离 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">backhit = f</span></code></li><li id="u04fdf4bd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">然后把那个 frame 的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 乘上一个衰减系数 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code></li><li id="u65c9f62d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">作为当前帧从“后方邻居”继承来的修正</span></li></ul><h3 id="QgYhT"><span class="ne-text">这里的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code></h3><pre data-language="python" id="ZRojL" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)
alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1</code></pre><p id="uf1cd085e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">第二行这个三次多项式就是论文前面几节反复用的那个平滑函数：</span></p><p id="u47e33488" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">[<br /></span><span class="ne-text">\alpha(t)=2t^3 - 3t^2 + 1<br /></span><span class="ne-text">]</span></p><p id="u3487afe6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它有个很重要的性质：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u028158ff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">距离很近时，权重接近 1</span></li><li id="uf15affac" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">距离变远时，权重平滑衰减到 0</span></li><li id="ua010793d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而且首尾导数为 0，过渡更柔和，不是硬折线式的线性衰减。</span></li></ul><h3 id="JtGwZ"><span class="ne-text">直观理解</span></h3><p id="u78156545" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧离最近的约束帧只差 1 帧，那它借到的 root offset 很多；<br /></span><span class="ne-text">如果差了 8~10 帧，那只借一点点，甚至接近 0。</span></p><hr id="P3SAx" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="q93Ai"><span class="ne-text">B5. forward 搜索</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="feQgV" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L4):
    if frame+f &lt; frame_count and np.any(contacts[frame,:]):
        alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)
        alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
        fpos = root_buffer[frame+f]*(alpha)
        if backhit &gt; -1:
            alpha = (backhit+1.0)/(backhit+f+1.0)
            pos = pos*(1.0-alpha) + fpos*(alpha)
        else:
            pos = fpos
        break</code></pre><p id="u2e2a813b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这部分和 backward 对称：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uddc3d8d3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">从当前帧往未来找</span></li><li id="u840e8ca1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">找到最近一个“有约束”的帧</span></li><li id="u412caf1a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">算出一个前向衰减版本 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">fpos</span></code></li></ul><p id="u9c84c5a1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">然后分两种情况：</span></p><h4 id="Yh83B"><span class="ne-text">情况 1：前面找到了，后面没找到</span></h4><p id="u77537a63" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那就直接用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">fpos</span></code></p><h4 id="A3SmY"><span class="ne-text">情况 2：前后都找到了</span></h4><p id="u8f2495d0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">那就把 backward 候选 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 和 forward 候选 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">fpos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 再做一次插值混合</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="nYS24" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>alpha = (backhit+1.0)/(backhit+f+1.0)
pos = pos*(1.0-alpha) + fpos*(alpha)</code></pre><p id="ub98e44d6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">意思是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2206c1f2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧更靠近后面的 hit，</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">fpos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 权重大</span></li><li id="ub3a5fba2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果更靠近前面的 hit，</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">pos</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 权重大</span></li></ul><h3 id="D4vRQ"><span class="ne-text">这在论文里对应什么</span></h3><p id="ucf2fdda7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">对应论文 4.5 节那个核心思想：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u588f171e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">对于无约束帧</span></li><li id="u9d5ad8cd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">前后各找最近一个曾经被调整过的帧</span></li><li id="ub5cb25bf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果两边都有，就把两边 adjustment 混起来</span></li><li id="ue1822d13" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">从而平滑跨过“约束打开 / 关闭”的边界。</span></li></ul><hr id="I8hPa" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="SVVe6"><span class="ne-text">B6. 最后一行</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="TRegj" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>root_buffer[frame] = pos</code></pre><p id="u3ad9ba02" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这行非常关键：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u55218a4a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">它不是生成新数组</span></li><li id="u07ad0ab3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">原地覆盖</span></strong><span class="ne-text">这一帧的 root correction</span></li></ul><p id="u7847bd0f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，Final Processing 结束后：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u4b2e5d69" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 不再只是“约束帧里的 root 修正”</span></li><li id="u87512883" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">而变成“整段动画上经过边界平滑后的 root 修正”</span></li></ul><hr id="HGV2y" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="vsvCX"><span class="ne-text">B7. 这一段代码里有两个你必须知道的问题</span></h3><p id="u33256565" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这很重要，我要很诚实地指出来：</span></p><h4 id="LhTs5"><span class="ne-text">问题 1：按你贴出来的字面代码，这两个判断写错了</span></h4><pre data-language="python" id="jithq" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if frame-f &gt;= 0 and np.any(contacts[frame,:]):</code></pre><p id="u1ef228b1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">和</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="hLYim" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if frame+f &lt; frame_count and np.any(contacts[frame,:]):</code></pre><p id="u889d806e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但外层已经是：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="epILQ" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if not np.any(contacts[frame,:]):</code></pre><p id="u9a1b49dd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以按字面逻辑：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u4531b011" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">外层要求当前帧 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">没有接触</span></strong></li><li id="u019fddd4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">内层却又要求当前帧 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">有接触</span></strong></li><li id="uc872d20f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那 inner </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">if</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 永远进不去</span></li></ul><p id="u23a2f800" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说：</span></p><p id="u6e4a0c7a" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">如果这就是你 notebook 里的原始代码，那 root 的 backward / forward 搜索实际上不会命中。</span></strong></p><p id="uf3712d01" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">从算法意图看，这里大概率应该写成：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="lpPaY" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>np.any(contacts[frame-f,:])</code></pre><p id="ufc5ec2a7" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">和</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="Lu7n7" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>np.any(contacts[frame+f,:])</code></pre><p id="u5e27f621" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是检查“邻居帧”是否有接触，而不是再检查当前帧。</span></p><h4 id="PhxV7"><span class="ne-text">问题 2：这里用了 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L2</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 而不是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code></h4><pre data-language="python" id="H99oe" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>alpha = (f+1.0)/(L2+1.0)</code></pre><p id="u3d99266a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">但 Final Processing 理论上对应的是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 邻域，不是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L2</span></code><span class="ne-text">。论文图 2 和 4.5 节都把最后一步单独对应到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u80b0e791" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以这里更合理的写法应该是 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4+1.0</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">这很像 notebook 里的一个遗留变量笔误。</span></p><hr id="Itn46" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="Ki7Oq"><span class="ne-text">Part C：定义 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_ankle(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text">，对单只脚做 Final Processing</span></h2><p id="u5c3f1fed" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">下面这段是核心中的核心：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="Af59a" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def _ankle(foot_contacts, rotation_offsets, position_offsets):
    if not np.any(foot_contacts[frame,:]):
        local_quat = np.array([1,0,0,0], dtype=np.float32)
        local_pos = np.array([0,0,0], dtype=np.float32)
        
        # backward
        back_hit = -1
        for f in range(L4):
            if frame-f &gt;= 0 and np.any(foot_contacts[frame-f, :]):
                xx = position_offsets[frame-f]
                back_hit = f
                alpha = (f+1.0)/(L4+1.0)
                alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
                local_quat = lab.utils.quat_slerp(np.array([[1,0,0,0]], dtype=np.float32), rotation_offsets[frame-f][np.newaxis,:], alpha)[0]
                local_pos = position_offsets[frame-f]*(alpha)
                break

        # forward
        front_hit = -1
        for f in range(L4):
            if frame+f &lt; frame_count and np.any(foot_contacts[frame+f, :]):
                yy = position_offsets[frame+f]
                front_hit = f
                alpha = (f+1.0)/(L4+1.0)
                alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
                front_quat = lab.utils.quat_slerp(np.array([[1,0,0,0]], dtype=np.float32), rotation_offsets[frame+f][np.newaxis,:], alpha)[0]
                front_pos = position_offsets[frame+f]*(alpha)

                if back_hit &gt; -1:
                    alpha = (back_hit+1.0)/(back_hit+front_hit+1.0)
                    local_quat = lab.utils.quat_slerp(local_quat[np.newaxis,:], front_quat[np.newaxis,:], alpha)[0]
                    local_pos = local_pos*(1-alpha) + front_pos*(alpha)
                else:
                    local_quat = front_quat
                    local_pos = front_pos
                break
        return local_quat, local_pos
    return rotation_offsets[frame], position_offsets[frame]</code></pre><hr id="Xdr5I" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="EGbhW"><span class="ne-text">C1. 这个 helper 的职责是什么</span></h3><p id="u531337a8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它不是处理整个人，也不是处理 root。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">它专门处理：</span></p><p id="ud7fa6195" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">某一只脚在当前 frame 的 ankle offset，要不要从前后邻居借一点过来。</span></strong></p><p id="u56a58d66" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">输入：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0ab073d1" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">foot_contacts</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u05599e92" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只看某一只脚的接触子集</span></li><li id="uaa01a801" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">例如左脚就看 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">[left heel, left ball]</span></code></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u6695afe7" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">rotation_offsets</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u487330aa" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这一只脚每帧的 ankle 旋转 offset</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="udf4e803c" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">position_offsets</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u351505c3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">这一只脚每帧的 ankle 位置 offset</span></li></ul></ul><p id="ud2752d27" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">输出：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u28f453c3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧这只脚应该使用的</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u5a85c744" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">local_quat</span></code></li><li id="udc34722a" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">local_pos</span></code></li></ul></ul><p id="u66dccdc5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，它是在回答：</span></p><p id="u12e63932" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">当前 frame 这只脚虽然没接触，但它是不是离接触区太近，应该带一点衰减后的 ankle 修正？</span></strong></p><hr id="bf0Bm" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="A5qCi"><span class="ne-text">C2. </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">if not np.any(foot_contacts[frame,:]):</span></code></h3><p id="u24e31827" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一句意思是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub41fde33" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧这只脚没有任何接触</span></li><li id="uf67a8de4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只有这种情况下才需要 blend off</span></li></ul><p id="u569f417a" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">如果当前帧这只脚本来就在接触状态：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="coEfz" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>return rotation_offsets[frame], position_offsets[frame]</code></pre><p id="u1d429632" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">直接把已有 offset 原样返回，不做额外平滑。</span></p><p id="u50c1e689" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这和论文 4.5 节一模一样：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">只对“没有因为约束直接被调整的参数”做邻域扫描和平滑。</span></p><hr id="u2CoJ" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="RiDXD"><span class="ne-text">C3. 为什么初值是 identity quaternion 和 zero position</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="HgwuK" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_quat = np.array([1,0,0,0], dtype=np.float32)
local_pos = np.array([0,0,0], dtype=np.float32)</code></pre><p id="u77a860ec" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这两个值的含义是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf1712b63" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">默认不做任何 ankle correction</span></li><li id="ubf895dce" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">旋转偏移为单位四元数</span></li><li id="u06a66dac" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">位置偏移为零向量</span></li></ul><p id="u5ed40bff" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这跟 Jerome 在语音稿里说的“我们保存的是相对原动画的 offset，而不是绝对目标姿态”完全一致。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">因为保存的是 offset，所以：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub823f479" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“没有修正”对应的旋转 offset 就是 identity quaternion</span></li><li id="u5367b68c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">“没有修正”对应的位置 offset 就是零向量。</span></li></ul><hr id="eWtZo" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="z6M12"><span class="ne-text">C4. backward 搜索：找最近的过去接触帧</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="yCH4b" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L4):
    if frame-f &gt;= 0 and np.any(foot_contacts[frame-f, :]):
        back_hit = f
        alpha = (f+1.0)/(L4+1.0)
        alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
        local_quat = quat_slerp(identity, rotation_offsets[frame-f], alpha)
        local_pos = position_offsets[frame-f] * alpha
        break</code></pre><p id="u922a1950" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这一段意思是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud2f39b1e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">从当前帧向过去看</span></li><li id="u3647bcd0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">找最近一个这只脚“有接触”的 frame</span></li><li id="u8b467924" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果找到，就把那个 frame 的 ankle offset 衰减后借过来</span></li></ul><p id="uc0dfd122" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">为什么旋转要用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">slerp(identity, offset, alpha)</span></code><span class="ne-text">？</span></p><p id="ue4176095" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为四元数旋转不能像向量一样直接线性乘 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">alpha</span></code><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">所以要做：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u0619c958" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">从“无修正姿态” </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">identity</span></code></li><li id="u2e738a2b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">插值到“完整修正姿态” </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">rotation_offsets[frame-f]</span></code></li></ul><p id="u5ccbb0c8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这正是论文里对 quaternion parameter 的处理方式：<br /></span><span class="ne-text">四元数要用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">slerp</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="ue975beb5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">为什么位置可以直接：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="P1FtS" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>local_pos = position_offsets[frame-f] * alpha</code></pre><p id="ua3bff935" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">因为位置 offset 是 3-vector，可以直接 lerp / scale。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">论文也明确说了：对 3-vector 参数，用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">lerp</span></code><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><hr id="rCPW1" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="TpsAB"><span class="ne-text">C5. forward 搜索：找最近的未来接触帧</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="krMXC" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>for f in range(L4):
    if frame+f &lt; frame_count and np.any(foot_contacts[frame+f, :]):
        front_hit = f
        alpha = (f+1.0)/(L4+1.0)
        alpha = 2*alpha*alpha*alpha - 3*alpha*alpha + 1
        front_quat = quat_slerp(identity, rotation_offsets[frame+f], alpha)
        front_pos = position_offsets[frame+f] * alpha</code></pre><p id="u297d2e3f" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">和 backward 完全对称。</span></p><p id="u95638abc" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它得到的是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub3fec955" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">从未来那个接触 frame 衰减回来的候选修正 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">front_quat/front_pos</span></code></li></ul><hr id="OZ3Km" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="QdWbP"><span class="ne-text">C6. 如果前后两边都找到了，就再合成一次</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="crPAu" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>if back_hit &gt; -1:
    alpha = (back_hit+1.0)/(back_hit+front_hit+1.0)
    local_quat = quat_slerp(local_quat[np.newaxis,:], front_quat[np.newaxis,:], alpha)[0]
    local_pos = local_pos*(1-alpha) + front_pos*(alpha)
else:
    local_quat = front_quat
    local_pos = front_pos</code></pre><p id="u4d37acba" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">语义上就是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua7a1f84f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果只有一边有 hit，就直接用那一边</span></li><li id="ua137ddb8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">如果两边都有 hit</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ub5cd48ee" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">那么当前帧更靠近哪边，就更偏向哪边的修正</span></li><li id="u1c540136" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">旋转继续用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">slerp</span></code></li><li id="uac0a3574" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">位置继续用线性插值</span></li></ul></ul><p id="u160a2020" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这和论文 Figure 3 里的三种情况一一对应：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="uacf2c30e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只受前向影响</span></li><li id="ucc56d4a4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">只受后向影响</span></li><li id="u9c0617a4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">前后都有影响，需要跨中间区域混合。</span></li></ol><hr id="DY7hQ" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="q1kgZ"><span class="ne-text">C7. 这一段在“思想上”比 root 那段更干净</span></h3><p id="ua98a1170" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你会注意到 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_ankle</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 这段在逻辑上非常完整：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8b3cd39a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">当前帧没接触才处理</span></li><li id="ua753e3d7" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">backward / forward 真正在检查邻居帧</span></li><li id="ua3e9a47d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">衰减用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">L4</span></code></li><li id="u539be2ad" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">rotation 用 slerp</span></li><li id="u3ad5a64f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">position 用 lerp/scaled vector</span></li></ul><p id="uf74f3e78" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以如果你要理解 Final Processing，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">以 </span></strong><code class="ne-code"><strong><span class="ne-text">_ankle</span></strong></code><strong><span class="ne-text"> 这段为主线最清楚</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">root 那段更像是同一思路在 root 上的平移版。</span></p><hr id="HAljX" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="U1jR5"><span class="ne-text">Part D：把 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">_ankle</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 应用到左脚和右脚</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="BH0Bc" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 0, :], ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :] = _ankle(contacts[:, :2], ankle_rotation_buffer[:,0,:], ankle_position_buffer[:,0,:])
ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 1, :], ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :] = _ankle(contacts[:, 2:], ankle_rotation_buffer[:,1,:], ankle_position_buffer[:,1,:])</code></pre><p id="uf9cb816c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这两行就是把 helper 真正落地到左右脚：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u20ce3f5a" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts[:, :2]</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="uf103ecd4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左脚两路接触：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">left heel</span></code><span class="ne-text">, </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">left ball</span></code></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u632448e6" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">contacts[:, 2:]</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u6cbcdd9c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右脚两路接触：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">right heel</span></code><span class="ne-text">, </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">right ball</span></code></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf6ba74bb" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">0</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ud6b4cd2a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左 ankle</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf2871251" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">1</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ub9742761" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右 ankle</span></li></ul></ul><h3 id="qMJk6"><span class="ne-text">这一层的意义</span></h3><p id="uc4ebf405" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">前面 helper 只是“定义算法”，这两行才是：</span></p><p id="u940ba93e" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">把 phase 5 的 ankle final processing 应到左脚和右脚整段动画上。</span></strong></p><p id="u32657ade" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">执行完以后：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ua5a18671" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左脚 ankle offset buffer 被平滑更新</span></li><li id="u9191266c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右脚 ankle offset buffer 被平滑更新</span></li></ul><hr id="sRKC5" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="AOXDQ"><span class="ne-text">Part E：</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">compute_final_animation()</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 才是把平滑后的 buffer 兑现成最终动画</span></h2><pre data-language="python" id="QWNe8" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>solved_animation = compute_final_animation()</code></pre><p id="u4d5fd53e" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这行特别容易被忽略，但它其实是整个 Final Processing 和最终结果之间的桥。</span></p><h3 id="XKeJE"><span class="ne-text">它做什么</span></h3><p id="u2dab6b0b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它会拿着：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u5562cdb6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">已经平滑好的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">root_buffer</span></code></li><li id="uf1b1b1b2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">已经平滑好的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_rotation_buffer</span></code></li><li id="u009f9056" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">已经平滑好的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">ankle_position_buffer</span></code></li></ul><p id="u2c1c3410" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">去真正重建最终动画。</span></p><p id="u3b800d62" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">Jerome 在语音稿里明确说：</span></p><p id="u7de74c44" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">做完 final processing 以后，再去对这个 solution 做 IK，<br /></span><span class="ne-text">而不是先做 IK 再做 final processing。</span></p><p id="u90826614" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这说明 notebook 的实现顺序是：</span></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u2b67d7e5" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">先把 root / ankle 的 target offset 平滑好</span></li><li id="uaa1e843c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">再一次性生成 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved_animation</span></code></li></ol><p id="u5a8f68b0" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">也就是说，这个 notebook 里：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uce3f5b64" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">Final Processing 操作的是 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">中间表示（buffers）</span></strong></li><li id="u2e4467cc" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">compute_final_animation()</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 才把中间表示变成最终可播姿态</span></li></ul><p id="u838b8de8" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是一种很好的工程组织方式，因为这样：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3d7fac1d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">平滑逻辑和 IK/materialization 解耦</span></li><li id="u98e5d0d4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">更容易调试 buffer 是否合理</span></li></ul><hr id="IWmYH" class="ne-hr"><h2 id="feBZL"><span class="ne-text">Part F：后面的 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">render(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 不是算法本体，是调试可视化</span></h2><p id="u56b9b98b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这整块：</span></p><pre data-language="python" id="q1c1W" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>def render(frame, solved=True):
    ...</code></pre><p id="u0b91b873" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">严格说不属于 Final Processing 算法本体。<br /></span><span class="ne-text">它的作用是</span><strong><span class="ne-text">把你刚刚平滑完的东西画出来给你看</span></strong><span class="ne-text">。</span></p><p id="u1f9b4606" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">我给你拆一下它在看什么。</span></p><hr id="qTGFO" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="Z8gTG"><span class="ne-text">F1. 选择显示原动画还是修复后动画</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="w73mj" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>anim = solved_animation if solved else animation</code></pre><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uf4f57dc7" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved=True</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u92720e1a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">看修复后的动画</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u1d79ff31" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">solved=False</span></code></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u1d83bc47" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">看原始滑脚版本</span></li></ul></ul><p id="u0fa78564" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是最基础的 before / after 切换。</span></p><hr id="OG9tz" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="JuOhK"><span class="ne-text">F2. 画角色和地面</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="smtov" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>p = (anim.pos[frame,...])
q = (anim.quats[frame,...])

a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(q, p)
viewer.set_shadow_poi(p[0])

viewer.begin_shadow()
viewer.draw(character, a)
viewer.end_shadow()

viewer.begin_display()
viewer.draw_ground()
viewer.draw(character, a)</code></pre><p id="ub48defa5" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这部分只是：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uab124f24" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">把当前帧的骨骼姿态转成矩阵</span></li><li id="ub729d830" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">画角色</span></li><li id="u03ced611" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">画阴影</span></li><li id="ubc87b725" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">画地面</span></li></ul><p id="u8b5bf6d6" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">它不改算法，只是显示。</span></p><hr id="dguCA" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="EkmZl"><span class="ne-text">F3. 画 constraint target</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="jYkjX" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>contacts = np.eye(4, dtype=np.float32)[np.newaxis,...].repeat(4, axis=0)
contacts[:, :3, 3] = constraint_positions_buffer[frame, :, :]

viewer.draw(target, contacts)</code></pre><p id="ub34b9876" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这里是把当前 frame 的四个约束点画成小 target。</span></p><p id="u0672c2c2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你能直观看到：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u00291a7e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left heel target</span></li><li id="ue4acf10a" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">left ball target</span></li><li id="u0cd33164" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right heel target</span></li><li id="uf6d7b04f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">right ball target</span></li></ul><p id="uf997d9ff" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">这是检查“脚底应该锁在哪里”的最好方式。</span></p><hr id="IDeie" class="ne-hr"><h3 id="EHGIL"><span class="ne-text">F4. 画调试线和三个关键坐标轴</span></h3><pre data-language="python" id="guHvh" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>viewer.draw_lines(character.world_skeleton_lines(a))</code></pre><p id="u483dc7d1" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">画骨架线框。</span></p><p id="ub9300ae2" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">然后是三组 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">draw_axis(...)</span></code><span class="ne-text">：</span></p><h4 id="e56qh"><span class="ne-text">左脚 ankle axis</span></h4><pre data-language="python" id="LtdZk" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 0, :], agquats[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :])
gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('LeftFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 0, :]
a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)</code></pre><h4 id="zwi7k"><span class="ne-text">右脚 ankle axis</span></h4><pre data-language="python" id="PWuyc" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = lab.utils.quat_mul(ankle_rotation_buffer[frame, 1, :], agquats[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :])
gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('RightFoot'), :] + ankle_position_buffer[frame, 1, :]
a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)</code></pre><h4 id="BsTv2"><span class="ne-text">hips / root axis</span></h4><pre data-language="python" id="Pa5Wo" class="ne-codeblock language-python"><code>gquat = agquats[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :]
gpos = agpos[frame, character.bone_index('Hips'), :] + root_buffer[frame, :]
a = lab.utils.quat_to_mat(gquat, gpos)
viewer.draw_axis(a[np.newaxis,...], 20)</code></pre><h3 id="W65jN"><span class="ne-text">这三组轴分别在检查什么</span></h3><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3f89f70e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">左脚 axis：Final Processing 后左脚 ankle offset 有没有平滑</span></li><li id="ue550f294" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">右脚 axis：右脚有没有平滑</span></li><li id="u9f2fbd90" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text">hips axis：root_buffer 有没有在边界前后柔和过渡</span></li></ul><p id="ub746b071" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">所以 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text">render()</span></code><span class="ne-text"> 的价值是：</span></p><p id="ufb879c72" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">它把 Final Processing 这一步本来很抽象的“offset fade”变成了肉眼可检查的坐标轴变化。</span></strong></p><hr id="OVq4L" class="ne-hr"><h1 id="Uezj9"><span class="ne-text">五、把整段 Final Processing 压成一条执行链</span></h1><p id="u16c56533" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">你可以把它记成下面这条链：</span></p><pre data-language="plain" id="xzcBP" class="ne-codeblock language-plain"><code>输入：
contacts
+ root_buffer
+ ankle_rotation_buffer
+ ankle_position_buffer

对每一帧：
    如果当前帧没有接触：
        去前后 L4 帧里找最近的接触帧
        把它们的 root / ankle offset 衰减后借过来
        如果前后两边都找到了，就按距离再混合一次

输出：
平滑后的 root_buffer
平滑后的 ankle_rotation_buffer
平滑后的 ankle_position_buffer

然后：
compute_final_animation()

最后：
得到 solved_animation 并可视化检查</code></pre></details>
